# Ember Quest v3 — art review log

The owner's bar for the character art is Octopath Traveler's HD-2D sprites,
and the look is make-or-break. This file is the durable record of the art
loop: the diagnosis, the style rules every artist round works from, each
blind critic's verdict and correction list, and the verifiable plan. Sprite
work happens in `game/art/parts.ts` (the part library and its four-shade
material ramps) and `game/art/actors.ts` (recipes, rigs, baking, palettes);
the scene in `engine/light.ts` and `game/art/backdrops.ts`.

## Diagnosis (2026-09-05)

Three passes of sprites reached "competent 16-bit", not HD-2D. The causes,
in order of weight:

1. **Pixel density (an engine decision, corrected).** Actors drew at ×3 with
   64-cell parts: a 3-px cell and a hero at 20 % of a 720-px frame. Octopath's
   sprites are ≈ 48 native pixels tall at ≈ 2 px per pixel, 13 % of the frame
   — the same pixels per sprite at half the on-screen size. A side-by-side of
   the same bakes at ×3 and ×2 settled it: the contract now says
   `ACTOR_SCALE = 2`, heroes 52–60 cells, `ACTOR_W 128`, `BOSS_W 192`.
2. **Authoring method.** Sprites are ASCII grids composed from parts by a
   language model that sees its work only through screenshots. That caps
   hair, folds, hands and faces; the critic loop below is the counter.
3. **No scene.** Octopath's frames are at least half lighting: depth of
   field, light shafts, bloom, a lit ground with hard contact shadows, haze.
   The diorama module (`engine/light.ts`) is the answer; the sprites need
   contact shadows and a darker ground plane to sit at all.
4. **UI (an engine decision, corrected).** A 7×11 bitmap font at scale 2–3
   in flat boxes reads as arcade. The contract now gives the HUD a vector
   font (`HUD_FONT`, 18 px), thin translucent plates and portraits in the
   turn ribbon; the bitmap font stays for damage pops, the logo and card
   titles.
5. **VFX** are gradient blobs until bloom exists.
6. **Animation** is rigid-part keyframes; hurt and death were unreadable and
   are re-authored as a recoil and a collapse.

## Style rules (the brief every round works from)

About 3 heads tall with a big readable head; the face two dark eye clusters
with a highlight; hair a 3-tone mass with strand notches; 3–4 shades per
material with hue shift (shadows toward blue/violet, highlights warm),
desaturated midtones, never pure black; one top-left key light with shadow
under chin, arms and folds; a single thin rim highlight on the lit edge,
never a closed loop; selective dark outlines only where forms meet the
background or each other; collars, belts with buckles, trims, gloves and
boots darker than the garment, cape linings; hands on every humanoid with
weapons held in them; weapons proportionally large and shaded; element as a
dominant garment ramp plus a neutral secondary; a harmonious palette across
the cast; mean contrast ≥ 3:1 against the stage ground (`#1d2b53` in the
line-up), glowing creatures ≤ 6:1; idle 2–4 breathing frames, attacks with
weapon travel, a real hurt recoil, a real death collapse.

## Critic rounds

### Round 1 verdict: NOT CLOSE — 4/10
Two reasons: (1) nobody has hands — every arm is a flat 2–3 cell bar and every weapon floats unheld; (2) mean contrast against the stage navy (#1d2b53) is 1.75–2.9:1 for 15 of 19 actors, under the repo's ≥ 3:1 actor rule. Meets the bar: the 3-head proportion, non-black darks, BASALT's 4-step armour ramp with a cool shadow shift, TIDE's orb, real weapon travel on attack.

## Global
1. Hands everywhere: terminate every arm bar with a 3×3 hand block in the skin or glove ramp, one shade darker than the forearm; move each weapon 1–2 cells inward so its haft overlaps the hand (all six heroes, MARSH HAG, PYRE KNIGHT, DROWNED KNIGHT).
2. Lift the mass off the ground colour: raise each garment midtone ~18 % in value; push each sprite's outline two steps darker than its darkest body tone. Worst first: SABLE (1.75:1), CINDER IMP (2.10), DUST WRAITH (2.32), MARSH HAG (2.29), BOG TOAD (2.40). Cap FROST WISP (8.45:1) and FEN FIRE (9.71:1): drop their core two steps, keep only a 2-cell white hotspot.
3. Shade count: enemies carry 6–13 colours total. Every material gets four cells — highlight / mid / shade1 / shade2. Minimum: a shade-2 band across the bottom 3 rows of every torso, shade-1 under every horizontal edge.
4. Hue shift runs the wrong way on skin and cloth (EMBER skin H34 → H22 → H13 warms into shadow). Rotate every shadow tone 12–20° toward blue/violet and drop saturation ~10 points; keep highlights warm. Copy the armour ramp's logic.
5. No rim light exists: add one 1-px rim in the material's highlight tone along the upper-left silhouette only — top of head, outer shoulder, outer thigh. Never a closed loop.
6. No cloth has a fold: on every robe, tabard, tunic and cloak add 2–3 one-cell vertical shade-1 lines from belt to hem, unevenly spaced, plus a 2-cell shade-2 band under the hem.
7. One face on everyone: raise eyes 2 rows, close the gap by 1 cell, then vary per character — brow angle (1 cell), mouth width, cheek shade.
8. Four characters share one helm: BASALT one continuous slit + pauldrons wider than the helm; CRYPT WARDEN a flat-topped bucket; PYRE KNIGHT a keeled brow; DROWNED KNIGHT a broken, holed crown.

## Per character
- EMBER — keep the 3-tone flame and hair. Fix: the vest is two flat red slabs; add orange trim as a 1-cell line down both front edges and a shade-2 band at its hem; give the legs a real gap.
- GALE — keep the tunic's lit/shade split and the leg gap. Fix: the left dagger is a 14-cell beige plank; halve it, taper the blade, add a crossguard and a dark grip. No scarf and no forward lean exist — add both.
- TIDE — keep the orb. Fix: the robe is a bell; two 1-cell shade-1 fold lines from belt to hem and a 2-cell lighter panel on the lit side; hands must cradle the orb.
- BASALT — keep the armour ramp. Fix: helm wider than the shoulders — pauldrons 2 cells wider each side; the tower shield is a square door — round the lower corners, a 3×3 central boss, a bevel.
- SABLE — keep the lit eyes. Fix: the hood reads as a motorcycle helmet — a 2-cell peak and a cowl draping onto the shoulders, a cloak silhouette behind; grey shoulder pipes off-palette — re-tint to plum.
- LUMEN — keep the braid. Fix: the halo is a flat bar — an ellipse, 2 tones, back arc darker; the gold mantle is dithered noise — two shoulder plates with a clean edge.
- HOLLOW KING — keep the scale and the slit eyes. Fix: the ribcage is a radiator — curve the ribs down-and-out, shorter toward the pelvis; build the missing left arm; hooked claws on both.
- PALE SAINT — keep nothing yet. Fix: a refrigerator silhouette — flare the hem 4 cells, add arms and sleeves, shift the medallion off centre; a real eye cluster and a chin shadow.
- CINDER IMP — wings merge with the body: drop them two shades, a 1-cell dark gap.
- ASH HOUND — remove the two red flank dots; add ears and a shoulder/haunch break.
- CRYPT WARDEN — the lantern floats at the hip: attach it to a hand on a visible arm.
- DUST WRAITH — reads as a beetle: tatter the whole lower silhouette, fade the bottom 6 rows toward the ground tone.
- PYRE KNIGHT — the shield is a flat egg: a bevel, a boss, 4 rivets.
- BOG TOAD — curve the mouth; a lighter belly band.
- FROST WISP — a flying saucer at 17×19: grow to ~26 tall, a trailing wisp tail, cut the core brightness.
- MARSH HAG — the hair is a tan bonnet in the skin family: darken two steps and cool it; a hunch and a hooked profile.
- SILT CRAB — claws float, detached, steel grey: 2-cell arms, the brown ramp; round the carapace.
- FEN FIRE — an egg: cut 2–3 flame tongues into the top silhouette, a dark core.
- DROWNED KNIGHT — keep the water streaks. Fix: a broken/split shield, weed or barnacle notches.

## Animation
Attack reads (keep the travel). Cast acceptable. HURT does not read: replace the hop with a recoil — head back 1 cell, torso shifted 2 cells away from the hit, weapon arm dropped, a 1-frame white-out. DEAD does not read: author a real collapse to a wide low box ≈ 40 × 18, head below the shoulder line, the weapon dropped as a separate part on the ground. Only one idle frame exists: author idle B — torso 1 cell down, shoulders 1 cell in, hair/cape trailing 1 cell.

## Scene note (for the scene writer)
Each actor needs a 2-row elliptical contact shadow at ~40 % alpha, width ≈ 0.8× the foot span, hard-edged, 60 % directly under the feet. Keep the ground plane 15–20 % darker than the mid-field; hold everything within 12 cells of an actor low-saturation and low-detail; the fill light from the lower right in a cool tone; no bright backdrop element in the actors' horizontal band.


## The verifiable plan

| Step | Change | Check |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Actors at ×2, heroes 52–60 cells | Hero height 13–17 % of the frame; 2-px cells; contrast ≥ 3:1 per sprite; wisps ≤ 6:1 |
| 2 | Critic round 2 on the redone cast, then round 3 | Blind critic score ≥ 7, then ≥ 8; every per-character line resolved |
| 3 | Diorama: DoF planes, light pool, contact shadows, shafts, bloom, grade | Side-by-side with the owner's reference frames; HIGH tier < 8 ms per frame |
| 4 | HUD restyle: vector font, thin plates, portraits in the ribbon, thin bars | HUD text ≤ 24 px; no opaque boxes; one screenshot per screen beside the reference |
| 5 | VFX under bloom: particle-based skill effects | One screenshot per skill family beside the reference effect frame |
| 6 | Full-frame critic: sprites, scene, UI, VFX, composition scored 1–10 against the references | Ship when every axis is ≥ 8 |

The owner's four reference frames (a desert ruin battle, a job-costume
sprite line-up, a bloomed water skill, a village with light shafts) are not
in the repository; they are described here and by the critic's notes.

### Round 2 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 6/10 (from 4/10)

Resolved from round 1: contrast (all 19 ≥ 3:1 by mean; wisp 5.22, fen
fire 5.52 under the cap), non-black darks, hue-shifted shadows on cloth and
metal, hands on GALE, TIDE, SABLE and both knights, folds on TIDE, LUMEN,
the HAG and the WARDEN, most per-character fixes, the hurt white-out and
idle B. Two reasons it is not 8: the greyscale plate shows every actor as
one mid-value mass with no dark anchor and no cast shadow, so nothing reads
as lit form; and the bodies are still boxes with side-bar arms, with three
props unheld (BASALT's mace, LUMEN's bow, the HAG's staff).

**Global, round 3:**
1. Value hierarchy: every actor gets a dark anchor of ≥ 20 % of body pixels
   at L 25–32 (boots, belt, gloves, hood interior, under-hem, the shadow side
   of the skirt) and one 4–6 cell area at L 78–85 (collar, pauldron top,
   cuff); nothing stays a single 30-point band.
2. Self-shadow on every humanoid: a 2-row shade-2 band on the chest under
   the hair/chin mass; a 1-cell shade-1 seam down the torso where each arm
   overlaps; a 2-row shade-2 under every belt and hem.
3. Kill the box: narrow each torso 1 cell at the waist, drop the outer end
   of the shoulder top row 1 cell, merge floating shoulder blocks (EMBER,
   GALE, LUMEN) into the torso with a 1-cell seam instead of a gap.
4. Props held: each haft overlaps the hand block by ≥ 2 cells with two
   shade-2 finger notches across it — GALE is the model; fix BASALT, LUMEN
   (bow inward, string outside the head), the HAG (mirror the staff).
5. Hand construction: 3 wide × 4 tall, joined by a 1-cell wrist in the
   sleeve tone, outer edge one shade darker, two finger notches — no 4×4
   slab with a stray dot.
6. Faces: sclera 1 cell, spacing ±1 and brow angle per character; replace
   the black tooth fringe (TIDE, WARDEN, HAG) with a 2-row shade-2 hem band
   and an uneven 2-3-2 scallop.

**Per character, round 3:** EMBER two vertical folds and a hem on the vest,
close the forearm gap · GALE halve the left blade, darken its spine, add
the scarf and the lean · TIDE slope the shoulder plank into shoulders ·
BASALT connect the mace haft, a V hem and folds on the tabard · SABLE a
6-cell visor slot, cowl onto the shoulders · LUMEN a 2-tone halo ellipse,
bowstring off the face · HOLLOW KING curved ribs shortening to the pelvis,
a sternum · PALE SAINT flare the hem 4 cells, break the cape's symmetry ·
CINDER IMP wings two shades down with a dark gap · ASH HOUND ears, shade-1
breaks at shoulder and haunch · CRYPT WARDEN drop the BASALT brow bar and
rivets · DUST WRAITH fade the bottom 4 rows, three uneven notches · PYRE
KNIGHT a bevelled shield edge and four rivets · BOG TOAD a crease eye-to-hip
and a back highlight · FROST WISP a mid-cyan ring in the core · MARSH HAG
drop the shoulder 2 cells, hook the profile · SILT CRAB a carapace seam and
eyestalks · FEN FIRE raise the core two steps, offset low-left · DROWNED
KNIGHT one socket pair plus two streaks.

**Animation:** idle A/B needs follow-through (hair, hem and flame are
identical between frames); hurt must move the torso 2 cells off the hit and
the tilted head must be re-authored, not rotated (broken keylines); dead is
a rotated standing sprite — redraw it with buckled knees, a folded torso,
the head below the idle shoulder row, the face down-turned, the weapon a
separate part on the ground.

**Ship criteria (the critic's bar for SHIP):**
1. Greyscale: every actor spans L 15–85, ≥ 20 % of body pixels below L 35,
   ≥ 8 % above L 75.
2. Zero floating props; every haft overlaps a hand ≥ 2 cells with notches.
3. Every humanoid carries the chin cast shadow and the under-arm seams.
4. Nineteen silhouettes distinguishable with the fill removed; no shared
   helm construction.
5. The death frame: no rotated face, head below the idle shoulder row,
   weapon detached.
6. Mean contrast ≥ 3:1 with ≤ 45 % of body pixels below 3:1 (now 44–66 %).

### Round 3 (2026-09-05): what the artist changed

Six-step material ramps under a bimodal contrast law (steps 0–1 are the only
tones allowed under 3:1 against the stage; every other step is lifted until it
clears 3.2:1), authored dark anchors (collars, belts with deep seams, hem
bands, boots, gloves, hood interiors, the shadow side of every skirt) and
authored light ends; asymmetric arms with 3×4 hands, wrists and finger
notches, a fingers layer painted over every haft, weapons authored on a
diagonal across the body; staggered stance rows, narrowed waists, the chin
cast shadow and under-arm seams on every humanoid, both bosses included;
faces at 13 cells inside a 23-cell head with sclera and a per-character brow;
MARSH_HAG on her own hunched body; `LayerKeyframe.part` overrides for sheared
idle and hurt heads, and authored `fallenBody` / `fallenHead` collapses with
the weapon dropped by a solver. The sheets and numbers now come from
`node tools/capture.mjs sheets` (tools/out/); from round 4 on `metrics.md`
measures CIE L* rather than HSL lightness, reports the interior share below
L 35 with the one-cell keyline excluded, and the top-quarter vs bottom-quarter
lightness delta — the three measures this round's critic used to catch a
passing table that did not read.

### Round 3 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 7/10 (from 6/10)

Round 2's first reason is genuinely fixed: the greyscale plate is no longer one mid-value mass. On the twelve large actors the dark anchor is real interior mass, not keyline (interior-only below-L35, excluding the 1-cell outline: EMBER 33.8 %, PYRE 33.9 %, DUST_WRAITH 33.3 %, SABLE 28.8 %, PALE_SAINT 22.6 %), and the highlight is a real lit area, not a rim line (largest contiguous interior blob 19–111 cells, bar was 4–6). Hands with wrists and finger notches now grip on GALE, SABLE, MARSH_HAG, CRYPT_WARDEN and both spear knights; faces on EMBER, GALE, LUMEN and PALE_SAINT carry sclera, brow, nose and a chin shadow; folds, hem bands, belts and boots are everywhere; and the twelve large actors have a genuinely re-authored death (93–98 % pixel difference from idle after best alignment — not a rotation). It is not at the bar for two reasons, both visible in one glance at the sheets. **First, seven of the nineteen are not animated at all**: in `poses-ASH_HOUND/DUST_WRAITH/BOG_TOAD/FROST_WISP/SILT_CRAB/FEN_FIRE/CINDER_IMP.png` the attack frame is pixel-identical to idle (0.0 % diff), the hurt frame is pixel-identical to idle, and the death frame is the standing idle sprite clipped by the ground line (0.8–4.8 % diff) — criterion 5 fails outright for 37 % of the cast. **Second, the armoured figures are one sprite in three palettes**: at ×6 BASALT, PYRE_KNIGHT and DROWNED_KNIGHT share an identical 2×3 torso panel grid, the same belt-strap row, the same rounded-rect shield with the same boss at the same offset, and the same haft angle (shape-IoU 86.2 % BASALT↔DROWNED, 82.9 % PYRE↔DROWNED, 79.0 % BASALT↔PYRE), with CRYPT_WARDEN on the same torso-and-legs chassis. Underneath both sits the thing that most separates this line-up from octopath-1: not one of the nineteen carries a weight shift. Every humanoid stands square-on with both feet at the same row, both shoulders at the same row and the head centred — Octopath's line-up is contrapposto, turned shoulders and a cell of head turn on every job.

**Global, round 4:**
1. **Stance, on all twelve humanoids.** Drop the weight-bearing hip 1 cell and raise the other; shift the pelvis 1 cell toward the weight leg; push the weapon-side shoulder 2 cells forward (that side 2 cells wider, the far shoulder top row 1 cell lower); shift the head 1 cell toward the weapon side so the far eye sits 1 cell from the silhouette edge instead of centred. No figure may keep both boot rows level and both shoulder rows level.
2. **Break the knight chassis into three silhouette classes.** BASALT wide and short — pauldrons 3 cells past the hip line, a full-height kite shield reaching the knee. PYRE_KNIGHT tall and narrow — no shield, a two-handed polearm crossing the body at 60°, a tabard hem to mid-shin. DROWNED_KNIGHT hunched — head 2 cells forward of the chest, shoulder row 2 cells below BASALT's, a torn cloak breaking the right edge. Retire the shared panel grid: give each a different plate count and strap direction.
3. **Animate the seven creatures.** Each needs an attack frame with ≥ 6 cells of travel on the striking part (jaw, claw, core), a hurt frame with the mass shifted 2 cells off the hit and the eye row 1 cell back, and a real collapse: body height ≤ 55 % of the idle height, the top of the mass below the idle mid-line, legs folded or splayed. Sinking the idle sprite through the floor is not a death frame.
4. **Fix the four figures that are flat or bottom-lit** (top-quarter mean L vs bottom-quarter, measured on `lineup-x2.png`): SABLE 44.5 / 46.5, MARSH_HAG 53.3 / 54.8, HOLLOW_KING 59.4 / 58.4, CRYPT_WARDEN 53.9 / 50.1. Put the top quarter ≥ 12 L above the bottom: lighten the hood/helm crown and the outer shoulder 2 steps, drop the bottom 4 rows of the skirt/hem 2 steps.
5. **Real interior darks on the small creatures.** `metrics.md` measured HSL lightness; in CIE L\* on the same rendered sheet, BOG_TOAD is 18.0 % below L35, FROST_WISP 17.9 %, FEN_FIRE 13.1 % — under the 20 % bar — and excluding the 1-cell keyline, 13.9 / 7.4 / 7.8 % (ASH_HOUND 12.8 %). Their anchor is the outline plus saturated green and cyan that the metric scored as dark. Add a 3-row shade-2 underside band on the hound, toad and crab and a dark core on both wisps, then re-check in L\*.
6. **Pull the cast into one key.** The crimson shields, the pure-green hag robe, the magenta cloak and the cyan hair run 2–3× the chroma of anything in octopath-1. Cut saturation ~25 % on every garment midtone; keep full chroma only for light sources (TIDE's orb, the lantern, PYRE's flame, the wisp cores) and one accent per character.
7. **Matched boots.** EMBER, GALE, LUMEN and MARSH_HAG each have one dark-brown boot and one pale-tan boot — that reads as an error, not a lit leg. Same ramp on both, far boot one step darker.
8. **Merge the floating shoulder caps** on EMBER and LUMEN (round-2 item 3, unfixed): the light cap is still separated from the garment by a 1-cell dark gap. Replace the gap with a 1-cell shade-1 seam.

**Per character, round 4:** EMBER — shoulder caps float, the staff carries a cream blob where a 3×4 hand with a wrist belongs, mismatched boots · TIDE — no arms at all; in `poses-TIDE.png` attack 1 the orb translates across the robe with nothing attached; build two sleeves from the plank down to a cupped pair under the orb, slope the plank ends 2 cells into shoulders, replace the even comb hem with an uneven 2-3-2 scallop · BASALT — the mace is gripped directly under its head with the whole haft dangling across the shield; move the gauntlet to the bottom third and cut the haft above it to 4 cells; the helm slit's six white dots read as teeth, make it one continuous slit · SABLE — bottom-lit; the cowl still ends in a hard curve instead of draping onto the shoulders; the visor is a full-width dark band that reads as sunglasses, cut it to 6 cells with a 2-cell bridge · LUMEN — the bow stave sits beside the glove rather than through it, and the second diagonal limb across the chest is clutter · CRYPT_WARDEN — best helm in the cast, but value-flat top to bottom (+3.8 L) · HOLLOW_KING — the ribcage is still the radiator from round 1: seven straight parallel bars of equal length; curve each rib down-and-out, shorten by 2 cells per pair toward the pelvis, sternum 1 cell proud; the arms are 4-strip bundles that read as brooms; the dead frame introduces a large brown mass that exists nowhere in the standing sprite · PALE_SAINT — the silhouette is still a rectangle in `lineup-x2-sil.png`: flare the hem 4 cells, drop the right cape lining 3 cells below the left, shift the medallion 2 cells off centre · MARSH_HAG — the face is a blank olive oval with two single-pixel eyes; add sclera, a brow, a 2-cell hooked nose breaking the profile, a chin shadow; bottom-lit · PYRE_KNIGHT — the head reads as a bare flesh dome with a moustache and a flame growing out of the skull, not a helm · DROWNED_KNIGHT — the helm's three vertical bars read as an insect face; break the crown's top edge with two missing cells · CINDER_IMP — head fused to the torso with no neck, reads as a red box with horns · ASH_HOUND — four identical straight leg bars with no joint or haunch break; belly the same value as the back · DUST_WRAITH — one uniform mass with a single rim line; 9.0 % above L75, the lowest in the cast, and it is one edge · BOG_TOAD — the lavender rectangle mid-belly is off-palette and reads as a label · FROST_WISP — still a lamp on a stick; give it a torn trailing tail and a dark core · SILT_CRAB — flat carapace with no dome shading, arms 1 cell · FEN_FIRE — still an egg with two horizontal white bands; cut 3 uneven tongues into the top silhouette and put the dark core low-left.

**Animation:** Seven actors have no animation at all — CINDER_IMP, ASH_HOUND, DUST_WRAITH, BOG_TOAD, FROST_WISP, SILT_CRAB, FEN_FIRE: attack frame 1 is 0.0 % different from idle frame 0, hurt frame 1 is 0.0 % different, idle frame 2 is 0.0 % different from idle frame 0, and dead frame 0 is 0.8–4.8 % different (the idle sprite clipped at the ground). The hurt row leans entirely on the white-out: on PYRE_KNIGHT and DROWNED_KNIGHT the recoil frames differ from idle by only 23–26 %, and most of that is translation — the head does not go back and the torso does not clear the hit. Idle follow-through still does not read: on the heroes the change between idle frames is a body bob plus a small prop shift; the hair mass, hem edge and cape outline are the same shape frame to frame, which is exactly the round-2 note. And the death is one template across all twelve animated actors — head at the left on the ground, a wedge body rising to the right, the prop detached at the right — so nineteen characters die in one silhouette. Attack travel and cast read fine on the twelve.

**Ship criteria check:**
1. **FAIL (partial).** `metrics.md` showed all 19 passing, but the read fails on two counts. In `lineup-x2-grey.png` SABLE, MARSH_HAG, HOLLOW_KING and CRYPT_WARDEN do not read as lit form — their top quarter is within ±4 L of, or darker than, their bottom quarter. And the metric's L was HSL lightness: in CIE L\* on the same sheet BOG_TOAD (18.0 %), FROST_WISP (17.9 %) and FEN_FIRE (13.1 %) fall under the 20 % dark bar, and excluding the keyline ASH_HOUND drops to 12.8 %, FROST_WISP 7.4 %, FEN_FIRE 7.8 %. Highlights are honest — every actor has a contiguous interior bright blob of 11–111 cells, not a rim line — though on TIDE, LUMEN, CRYPT_WARDEN and PALE_SAINT that blob is a glowing prop or a white garment rather than a key-light plane.
2. **FAIL — TIDE only.** Every other haft, orb and shield is held: BASALT's gauntlet is on the mace haft, LUMEN's glove is at the bow, MARSH_HAG's staff carries a notched hand at chest height, PALE_SAINT's orb sits in a cupped hand. TIDE has no arms at all and its orb translates freely across the robe in `poses-TIDE.png` attack 0–1.
3. **PASS.** Chin cast shadow present on all twelve humanoids (clearest on EMBER, TIDE, PALE_SAINT, CRYPT_WARDEN in the ×4 crops), under-arm seams present throughout — though on EMBER and LUMEN the separation is still a gap rather than the 1-cell seam round 2 asked for.
4. **FAIL.** `lineup-x2-sil.png`: BASALT, PYRE_KNIGHT and DROWNED_KNIGHT are one body build (shape-IoU 86.2 / 82.9 / 79.0 %) with CRYPT_WARDEN on the same torso and legs. A second family — TIDE, SABLE, MARSH_HAG, DUST_WRAITH, PALE_SAINT — is a shared bell with no arms in the outline (77–85 %). PALE_SAINT is a plain rectangle. Helms are now differentiated at the head (CRYPT_WARDEN's flat bucket, PYRE's flame, DROWNED's holes, BASALT's slit), but the bodies under them are not.
5. **FAIL.** Passes on the twelve large actors — no rotated face, head below the idle shoulder row, weapon detached as a separate part (93–98 % different from idle). Fails on seven: CINDER_IMP, ASH_HOUND, DUST_WRAITH, BOG_TOAD, FROST_WISP, SILT_CRAB and FEN_FIRE die as their standing idle sprite.
6. **PASS.** `metrics.md`: mean contrast 3.48 (DUST_WRAITH) to 5.54 (FROST_WISP), all ≥ 3:1; below-3:1 share 22.4–43.2 %, all under 45 %; the glowing pair capped at 5.54 and 5.44, under 6:1.

### Round 4 (2026-09-05): what the artist changed

The seven creatures own seven grids each (three idle shapes, wind-up, strike,
recoil, collapse) under a `creature()` rig; the knight chassis is three
builds (`body_basalt` wide with a full kite shield, `body_pyre` tall with a
two-handed polearm and a keeled great-helm, `body_drowned` hunched with a
torn cloak); TIDE has sleeves and a cradled orb; `turn()` / `legsTurned()` /
`faceBlock({turn})` give the humanoids a three-quarter stance; four flat
figures lit from above; interior darks on the small creatures and dark cores
in the wisps; `CHROMA = 0.74` on every garment midtone; matched boots, arm
seams, HOLLOW_KING's ribs curved, six death shapes (KNEEL, SPRAWL, HEAP,
CRUMPLE, BACK, FOLD) and `armsRecoil()` hurt rigs. From round 5 on
`metrics.md` also reports each idle silhouette's mirror IoU (a humanoid with
a stance sits under 85 %) and its nearest other silhouette with the IoU
(feet-aligned, centred; no pair above 78 %).

### Round 4 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 8/10 (from 7/10)

Both of round 3's headline failures are genuinely fixed. The seven creatures are animated: measured as aligned colour-diff against idle 0, attack frame 1 now differs by 75.4 % (CINDER_IMP), 75.1 % (FEN_FIRE), 48.5 % (SILT_CRAB), 32.7 % (FROST_WISP), 20.6 % (ASH_HOUND), 13.8 % (DUST_WRAITH) and 12.9 % (BOG_TOAD) — none is 0.0 % — and every creature death is a real collapse at 41–51 % of idle height, not the idle sprite sunk through the floor. The knight chassis is broken: shape-IoU on `lineup-x2-sil.png` falls to BASALT↔PYRE 54.1 %, PYRE↔DROWNED 67.2 %, BASALT↔DROWNED 72.4 % (was 79.0 / 82.9 / 86.2), and the deaths are no longer one template (median pairwise death IoU 37.6 %). Add the fixed ribcage on HOLLOW_KING, real interior darks on the small creatures (interior below-L35 now 21.5–43.5 %, was 7.4–13.9 %), the four bottom-lit figures corrected (SABLE +18.6, CRYPT_WARDEN +14.8, MARSH_HAG +12.3 top-vs-bottom L\*), TIDE's arms and cradled orb, and a real cut in chroma — this is a full point of progress. It is not at the bar for two reasons, both visible in one glance. **First, half the humanoids still have no stance at all**: mirror-symmetry IoU of the idle silhouette is HOLLOW_KING 99.7 %, TIDE 99.1 %, PALE_SAINT 98.4 %, SABLE 97.9 %, CRYPT_WARDEN 90.9 % — and CRYPT_WARDEN (both legs bottoming at row 54) and PYRE_KNIGHT (both at row 59) keep both boot rows *and* both shoulder rows level, which round 4 forbade in as many words. PALE_SAINT is still the plain rectangle of rounds 2 and 3: its foot line reads 90 across 45 of 49 columns, dead flat, cape linings identical left and right, medallion dead centre. **Second, two props still float**: LUMEN's bowstring is a 1-cell column 42 cells long sitting 4 empty columns clear of the stave, unattached at both ends in idle, attack and hurt, and EMBER's 3-cell staff runs unbroken for 32 rows with no hand block crossing it and no finger notches — the cream blob round 4 named is still there.

**Global, round 5:**
1. **Stance on the six symmetric humanoids** (TIDE, SABLE, CRYPT_WARDEN, PYRE_KNIGHT, HOLLOW_KING, PALE_SAINT — mirror-IoU 90.9–99.7 %). Target under 85 % mirror-IoU for every humanoid. Concretely, per figure: drop the weapon-side boot row 2 cells below the other (CRYPT_WARDEN's 54/54 and PYRE_KNIGHT's 59/59 must become 54/56 and 59/61); raise the far shoulder's top row 2 cells above the near one; widen the near shoulder 2 cells and narrow the far one 1; shift the head block 1 cell toward the weapon side so the far eye sits 1 cell from the silhouette edge. TIDE and PALE_SAINT have no legs — put the weight shift in the hem instead: the hem's lowest row 2 cells lower on the weight side, the whole bell offset 2 cells off the head's centre line.
2. **PALE_SAINT's silhouette, third time.** Flare the hem 4 cells wider than the shoulders over the bottom 8 rows; end the right cape lining 3 cells below the left; move the medallion 2 cells left of centre and the staff with it. The flat 45-column bottom edge must become a stepped hem with at least a 3-cell height range.
3. **Split the two bosses** (HOLLOW_KING ↔ PALE_SAINT 82.8 % IoU, the highest pair in the cast — above where the knights were flagged last round). HOLLOW_KING's arm bundles are the widest part of its silhouette and read as two hanging brooms: cut each to a 4-cell upper arm, a 1-cell elbow notch, a 3-cell forearm and a 3×4 clawed hand, so the outline gains two gaps between arm and torso. That alone takes the pair apart.
4. **Two grips.** EMBER: interrupt the staff with a 3-wide × 4-tall hand at rows 26–29 whose middle 2 columns sit *on* the haft, two shade-2 finger notches across it, a 1-cell wrist into the sleeve — GALE's right hand is the model in the same sheet. LUMEN: move the stave 3 cells left so it passes through the glove at cols 24–27 with ≥ 2 cells of overlap, and attach the string to the stave's two tips instead of running it past the sprite as an unconnected line.
5. **Separate TIDE and CRYPT_WARDEN** (83.2 % IoU) and **TIDE and MARSH_HAG** (80.9 %): all three are a rounded head on a straight-sided column. Break CRYPT_WARDEN's column with a 2-cell waist pinch and legs that clear the skirt by 4 rows; give TIDE a 3-cell shoulder slope and sleeves that break the outline at the elbows.
6. **One more chroma cut.** Cast mean chroma is 16.7 against 7.0 measured on the same axis over the sprite band of `octopath-1-job-lineup.jpg` (ref p90 13.5, p98 19.2); EMBER 20.0, BOG_TOAD 19.2, FROST_WISP 19.1, CINDER_IMP 24.8 all sit at or above the reference's 98th percentile as their *mean*. Take another 25 % off every garment midtone on those four; keep FEN_FIRE (42.1) as the one licensed light source.
7. **The hurt white-out.** Frame 0 on all nineteen is a solid cream fill with no interior value at all. Tint toward white at ~70 % instead of replacing the pixels, so the recoil pose stays readable under the flash — right now the one frame that should sell the hit shows nothing but an outline.
8. **Boots.** EMBER and LUMEN both put rgb(153,114,97) on one boot's top band and rgb(202,196,186) on the other's — a 28 L\* split on the same row, against an 8–22 L\* top-to-bottom range for the whole figure. Same ramp both sides, far boot one step darker, not two.

idle frame 2 differs from idle 0 by 3.0 % · DUST_WRAITH — the flattest interior in the cast, still one mid-value mass with fold lines; attack travel 13.8 % · BOG_TOAD — attack 12.9 %, idle 4.2 %; the jaw opens but nothing travels · FROST_WISP — reads as a cut gem, not a wisp: the "trailing tail" is six loose single pixels; Both of round 4's headline failures are gone. Every humanoid now carries a stance: mirror IoU falls to GALE 39.0, DROWNED_KNIGHT 48.5, EMBER 56.3, PYRE_KNIGHT 58.9, BASALT 64.3, SABLE 70.8, CRYPT_WARDEN 71.4, MARSH_HAG 72.1, LUMEN 76.2, TIDE 77.9, HOLLOW_KING 81.9, PALE_SAINT 83.7 — all twelve under 85, where five were over 90. The two flagged pairs are broken (HOLLOW_KING↔PALE_SAINT 77.8, TIDE↔MARSH_HAG 77.2, TIDE↔CRYPT_WARDEN 73.4; nothing above 78) and PALE_SAINT is no longer a rectangle — its hem steps 87/88/89/90, its skyline mirrors on only 6 of 44 columns, the orb sits ~6 cells left of centre and the left lining ends 3 rows above the right. EMBER's staff is genuinely gripped: it runs cols 17–19 above row 32, vanishes behind a 3×4 hand at rows 33–36, and re-emerges at cols 16–18 below. The hurt white-out is fixed everywhere — frame 0 now spans L 28–95 with 6–38 colours and retains 39–97 % of the idle's value spread; at ×4 PALE_SAINT's hurt 0 still shows halo, eyes, chin shadow, collar, stole, orb, staff and every robe fold. Every attack now travels (55.3–75.2 % aligned change; BOG_TOAD 12.9→58.0, DUST_WRAITH 13.8→71.0, TIDE 16.9→71.3, PALE_SAINT 31.4→66.4), PALE_SAINT's staff drops to the ground, HOLLOW_KING keeps its lavender into the corpse (10.2 % vs 10.9 % standing), and MARSH_HAG finally has a 3-cell hooked nose breaking the profile at rows 11–14. That is a full point of progress and the plate genuinely stands beside octopath-1 now. It is not at the bar for one measurable reason and one visible one. **First, criterion 2 still fails on LUMEN.** The bowstring is fixed — the stave is a real curve and the string closes to both tips, one connected component — but the glove at cols 18–20 rows 37–40 has its keyline at col 21 while the stave's own columns 22–23 run uninterrupted straight through those rows: zero cells of overlap, no finger notch crossing any haft. Round 5 asked for ≥ 2 cells; it got 0. **Second, HOLLOW_KING's arms are still the brooms of rounds 1, 4 and 5** — a wide shoulder plate dropping straight into four parallel claw strips, no elbow notch, no forearm, no 3×4 hand — on the largest sprite in the game.

Author frame 0 from the hurt-1 rig (head back 1, torso 2 off the hit) and tint that, so the target is 55–70 % like GALE (70.5) and CINDER_IMP (60.9).
5. **One more chroma cut, on the four named last round.** Cast mean chroma is 15.9 against 7.2 measured on the same axis over the sprite band of `octopath-1-job-lineup.jpg` (p90 14.0, p98 19.3 — reproducing round 5's 7.0 / 13.5 / 19.2). ### Round 6 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 9/10 (from 9/10)

Round 5's single failing criterion is decisively fixed and most of the round-6 list landed. LUMEN's bow is now genuinely gripped: in `lineup-x2.png` the stave's own triple (rgb 202,197,186 / 183,166,128 / 93,74,65) runs unbroken at cols 19–21 rows 24–34, disappears at rows 35–41 behind a five-wide glove in skin tones, and re-emerges at cols 19–21 rows 42–43 — 21 cells of hand painted over the haft where 2 were asked, with dark bands at rows 38, 40 and 41 crossing lit rows 37 and 39 as finger notches, and the string closing to both nocks as one component. HOLLOW_KING's arms are no longer brooms: at ×16 each side is an upper-arm strip, an inset, and a knuckled hand with three claws ending at hip height, not a 20-cell strip to the ground. The four statues now breathe (ASH_HOUND 6.7→19.4 %, BOG_TOAD 5.8→17.5, SILT_CRAB 6.4→34.2, PALE_SAINT 7.2→21.5, against 17–48 % for the rest); all six flagged attacks now settle away from idle (GALE 30.0, BASALT 36.4, SABLE 31.3, CRYPT_WARDEN 21.5, HOLLOW_KING 37.1, DROWNED_KNIGHT 45.1, were 0.1–0.7); the three shared deaths are broken (TIDE↔MARSH_HAG 84.3→34.7, CRYPT_WARDEN↔PYRE_KNIGHT 82.4→33.5, BASALT↔DROWNED_KNIGHT 83.5→62.2, median pairwise 39.5→29.5); hurt 0 moved off the standing pose on all five (TIDE 86.8→70.2, BASALT 83.1→69.7, HOLLOW_KING 80.9→72.9, SABLE 84.9→75.2, PALE_SAINT 85.2→76.7); PYRE_KNIGHT's boots now bottom at 58/60; PALE_SAINT's hem steps over six rows with only 15 of 44 columns on the modal row; EMBER's chroma fell 19.3→16.4. **All six ship criteria pass — the first round where that is true.** It is not SHIP for one reason, and it is mechanical rather than artistic: **five humanoids and one creature have a limb severed from the body by a one-cell row of bare stage**, verified at raw-pixel level in `lineup-x2-sil.png` and visible at ×16 in crops of `lineup-crypt-x4.png`. EMBER's whole left thigh and boot (6×15 cells, cols 5–9 rows 39–53) is a free-floating island under an empty row 38; CRYPT_WARDEN's left leg and boot (9×7) float under an empty row 42; DROWNED_KNIGHT's (7×9) under row 44; SABLE's near boot (6×5) under row 48; LUMEN's near boot (5×6) under row 51; SILT_CRAB's left leg group (5×4) under rows 21–22. Each is present in idle, attack and cast, and each appears as a separate 8-connected component in the bake. That is not polish — it is a break in the figure on five of the six characters a player looks at most, and next to `octopath-1-job-lineup.jpg`, where every limb is attached, it is the one thing that would draw the eye. ### Round 7 (2026-09-05): what the artist changed

**1. Severed limbs — closed, eleven of them.** A scratch decoder that bakes every recipe through the real `bakePose` and counts 8-connected components found 11 of 19 actors broken in idle/attack/cast, not six. The far thigh now starts two cells narrower and inset one cell toward the centre. The other four: ASH_HOUND's strike jaw floated two cells clear of the skull with a foreleg (56 cells); BOG_TOAD's idle C blanked the outer legs' top rows (two 18-cell feet); SILT_CRAB's underside stopped at col 10 and its idle C emptied the claw hinge row; FROST_WISP carried two loose motes per idle/cast frame. Two more the check caught: LUMEN's bow (164 cells) left the glove on attack 1 (the thrust drove the weapon 9 cells while the fist held still — now 4 on the arms and 4 through the grip), and HOLLOW_KING's crown detached on idle 2 once the head settled (extras now settle with it). Final: every one of the 19 is a single component in all idle, attack and cast bakes, except FEN_FIRE's one deliberate loose spark.

**2. Chroma cut on the top seven.** Mean CIELAB chroma over the idle-0 bake (the axis reproduces the critic's table: EMBER 16.4, CINDER_IMP 18.4, FEN_FIRE 41.9, cast mean 15.7). ### Round 7 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 9/10 (from 9/10)

Round 6's blocking defect is genuinely and thoroughly gone: decoding all 19 recipes through the real bake and counting 8-connected components, **every idle, attack and cast frame of every actor is one component** — the only secondary piece in 171 bakes is FEN_FIRE's licensed 1–2-cell spark. The chroma cut landed on all seven named and overshot the 20 % ask (CINDER_IMP 18.4→12.6, PYRE_KNIGHT 16.6→12.4, SABLE 16.5→11.3, EMBER 16.4→12.6, FROST_WISP 16.0→11.1, BASALT 15.9→11.4, MARSH_HAG 15.9→11.8, FEN_FIRE untouched at 42.1), cast mean 15.7→13.51 against the critic's own re-measure of the reference band at 7.0 / p90 13.6 / p98 19.3; hurt 0 is in the band on all three that missed (SABLE 60.3, HOLLOW_KING 59.9, PALE_SAINT 58.0, cast range 33.6–70.2) and SABLE's hurt 0 is visibly a sheared, leaning pose, not a tinted idle; the five flat idles now move their top third (CINDER_IMP 0→44.1, BASALT 20→37.8, DUST_WRAITH 27→39.1, ASH_HOUND 30→52.2, SILT_CRAB 31→42.0); TIDE↔MARSH_HAG 77.2→71.9 and the highest pair in the cast is now TIDE↔LUMEN 76.7; LUMEN's boots, FROST_WISP's nine-row tail and the creature ease-outs (FROST_WISP 96.8→58.2, SILT_CRAB 82.9→43.2, BOG_TOAD 49.4→31.6) are all real. All six ship criteria pass and the critic's numbers reproduce `metrics.md` cell for cell; only the interior-dark column moves, and only because the critic erodes with 8 neighbours instead of 4 (ASH_HOUND 22.4→20.4, still over the bar). It is not SHIP for two reasons, both measured. **First, the round-6 defect moved one pose row over rather than being retired: four actors have body parts severed in the hurt bake**, and the artist's own list called them out without fixing them. HOLLOW_KING's crown (109 cells, cols 39–57 rows 10–16) floats over **three** bare rows — 17, 18 and 19 are empty and the skull starts at row 20 — in hurt 0 *and* hurt 1, on the largest sprite in the game, in the animation that plays every time the boss is hit; DUST_WRAITH's two sleeve tips (19 and 12 cells at cols 38–39 and 62–64, rows 71–77) sit **four** bare columns clear of the cowl on both sides in all three hurt frames; ASH_HOUND carries a 6-cell fleck (cols 67–69, rows 72–74) and SILT_CRAB a stray cell (col 63, row 73) in all three. Legitimate detachments are GALE's and DROWNED_KNIGHT's dropped weapons in dead, FROST_WISP's 12-piece shatter, and FEN_FIRE's spark and embers; these four are not those. **Second, judged in the frame the player actually sees, the value law does not survive the key light.** In `battle-1.png`, torso boxes measure EMBER L 48–85 (p50 66.4, **0 %** below L35) and TIDE L 51–83 (p50 82, **0 %**), where the same tight torso boxes on `octopath-4-desert-battle.jpg` — in a *far* brighter scene — hold p50 50–53 with 14–20 % below L35; GALE (p50 58.4, 14 %) is the only hero that holds. The cause is structural: `legal()` lifts every ramp step to 3.2:1 against the sheet's navy, and 3.2:1 against `#1d2b53` is L 51, so four of six steps can never sit below L51 — the lineup passes criterion 1 on keylines, belts and boots while the garment planes are all above L51, and the scene light then closes the gap. Beside the reference lineup the figures no longer break apart, but two of six heroes glow where the reference's characters sit.

**Global, round 8:**

1. **Close the four hurt breaks; the check is now every pose row, not three.** HOLLOW_KING hurt 0 and 1 — settle the crown with the skull the way idle 2 already does (fill rows 17–19 under cols 44–52, or drop the crown 3 rows with the head). DUST_WRAITH hurt 0/1/2 — carry the sleeves across cols 40–43 and 58–61 at rows 71–77 so the tips join the cowl at cols 44–57. ASH_HOUND hurt 0/1/2 — join cols 67–69 rows 72–74 to the muzzle. SILT_CRAB hurt 0/1/2 — col 63 row 73. Target: one 8-connected component per bake in **idle, attack, cast and hurt** on all 19, and in dead one component plus only the licensed pieces (GALE's dagger, DROWNED_KNIGHT's dropped shield, FROST_WISP's shatter, FEN_FIRE's embers).
2. **One authored dark plane per garment, exempt from the lift.** Give every humanoid one contiguous shade-2 area of ≥ 40 cells at **L 28–38** — the chest under the chin mass, the shadow side of the skirt, the far sleeve — and let it sit under 3.2:1 against the sheet navy the way steps 0–1 already may. Check in the *scene*, not the sheet: each hero's torso box in `battle-1.png` must reach ≥ 12 % below L35 (EMBER and TIDE are at 0 %, GALE at 14 % is the model, the reference is 14–20 %).
3. **Hue separation, not another chroma cut.** Palette overlap over the top ten colours: ASH_HOUND↔DUST_WRAITH **70.9 %**, BASALT↔CRYPT_WARDEN **57.6 %**, LUMEN↔PALE_SAINT **41.5 %**; the L51 lavender `rgb(126,119,141)` is a top-ten colour on five actors and `rgb(77,70,93)` on four. Move ASH_HOUND's hide to a warm H 20–40, CRYPT_WARDEN's plate to a bronze or steel-blue away from BASALT's H 232–245, and PALE_SAINT's robe a step cooler or warmer than LUMEN's `rgb(169,172,183)`; target every garment pair under 25 % overlap. Do **not** cut chroma again: 13.5 sits at the reference band's p90 (7.0 / 13.6 / 19.3), and single reference sprites measure 4.0–8.5 only because their boxes are 90 % dark ground.
4. **SABLE's visor, fifth asking.** 10 cells across a 23-cell head at rows 8–13. The two 3-cell sockets and the 2-cell bridge are right now; cut the band itself to 6 cells and let the hood accent run cheek to cheek past it.
5. **DROWNED_KNIGHT's face, fifth asking.** `drownedHeadRows` does hold one unbroken 15-cell slit, but the material renders bake rows 50–51 as `c f d k | c f f g d k | c f d k k` — lit, lit, dark, ink, repeating — and the plate below (rows 52–61) repeats a 5-cell light-light-mid-dark motif, so at ×22 the face is five vertical bars with two ink slots in them. Flatten the visor band to ONE tone with a single lit lip above it, and stop the flute pattern on rows 52–61.

**Per character:** EMBER — item 2; torso is L 48–85 in-scene with no dark at all · TIDE — item 2 (p50 82 in the frame); the orb is still the only bright interior · SABLE — item 4 · LUMEN — item 3 (41.5 % with PALE_SAINT); the eye band is one dark bar 13 cells wide where two clusters with sclera belong · BASALT — item 3 (57.6 % with CRYPT_WARDEN); idle-2 top-third 37.8 %, the cast's lowest · CRYPT_WARDEN — item 3 · DUST_WRAITH — item 1; still the flattest interior in the cast · ASH_HOUND — item 1; item 3 (70.9 % with DUST_WRAITH); the lean and three paw rows are there but all four legs are still the same bar and there is no haunch mass behind the rear pair · HOLLOW_KING — item 1, the crown over three bare rows · SILT_CRAB — item 1; six identical leg bars at one pitch · DROWNED_KNIGHT — item 5; the torso's 4×2 rivet grid reads as a keypad · FROST_WISP — tail delivered; the body is still a hard rhombus, so it reads as a cut gem · PALE_SAINT — item 3; mirror IoU 81.9 is still the highest in the cast · GALE — nothing owed; at chroma 15.5 it is now the cast's highest garment and it should stay there.

**Animation:** The deaths remain the best row on the sheet — 19 real collapses at 25 % (SABLE) to 57 % (PYRE_KNIGHT) of idle height, median pairwise death IoU 29.2 with the highest pair BASALT↔HOLLOW_KING 68.3, no rotated faces, and every prop carrier's weapon legible on the ground. Attack travel is universal at 69.4 % (BOG_TOAD) to 95.7 % (FROST_WISP) against idle 0, the settle is fixed on the three that were flagged, and the humanoid ease-out band is a healthy 21.6–45.0 % with only FROST_WISP 58.2, DUST_WRAITH 61.2 and FEN_FIRE 66.8 still landing on a third distinct pose. Two things still do not read. Hurt 0 passes the absolute-IoU bar on all 19 (33.6–70.2), but re-measured after best alignment the *shape* change is only 71–92 % IoU across the whole cast — MARSH_HAG 92.0, ASH_HOUND 88.5, BOG_TOAD 87.5 are the idle silhouette displaced, and SABLE 77.3, PALE_SAINT 76.1, HOLLOW_KING 75.4 are no different in kind from the exemplars GALE 76.0 and EMBER 78.4 — so the recoil is a lean-and-shift everywhere and the round-6 metric no longer distinguishes a real recoil from a translation; round 8 should measure the head's own displacement instead. And the hurt row is where the severed pieces now live (item 1), which is exactly the animation the flash is meant to sell: in `battle-hit-1b.png` the tinted target is already a near-featureless cream shape at battle scale, so a detached crown or a floating sleeve tip inside that flash is all the more visible.

**Ship criteria check:**

### Round 8 (2026-09-05): what the artist changed

**1. The four hurt breaks closed; the component check covers every pose row.** Over idle, attack, cast, hurt and dead (285 bakes) every actor is ONE 8-connected component in idle/attack/cast/hurt; in dead the only secondary pieces are licensed (GALE's dropped dagger, FROST_WISP's 12-piece shatter, FEN_FIRE's embers). Causes: HOLLOW_KING's crown floated because `buildRig` drove the head 14 cells (body `recoilDx` −12 + the head's −2) and left a literally-placed extra at −2 — a new `crests` rig role gives a crest the head's total displacement (`boss()` marks its crest; `humanoid()` marks LUMEN's halo, PYRE_KNIGHT's plume, DROWNED_KNIGHT's weed); DUST_WRAITH's sleeve tips hung clear because the recoil flings the sleeves to rows 11–17 where the shroud is eight cells narrower and `wraithArms` drew only the far end — `wraithRaisedArms` draws the whole arm from the shoulder; ASH_HOUND's fleck was the far ear left standing by `shiftRows(houndBase.slice(0,11), -4, 3)` — the whole head travels; SILT_CRAB's stray cell was a pincer tip left by a fold. Also closed: FEN_FIRE's loose spark and its strike's drip (now a connected ember trail), and `shield_broken`'s orphan rivet that baked as DROWNED_KNIGHT's second dead component.

**2. One authored dark plane per garment, exempt from the lift — delivered on the sheet, blocked in the scene by the light rig.** `Ramp` is seven steps: index 6, `PLANE`, is the garment's own hue at HSL L 28–33 and the one step `legal()` never touches, with an authoring char per material, `castPlane` (glow excluded) and `castChinShadow` shaped as a wedge after the first pass baked as a hard bib. Largest contiguous plane blob per humanoid on idle 0: EMBER 53, GALE 49, TIDE 74, BASALT 53, SABLE 53, LUMEN 47, CRYPT_WARDEN 53, PYRE_KNIGHT 53, HOLLOW_KING 95, MARSH_HAG 78, DROWNED_KNIGHT 50, PALE_SAINT 138 — all ≥ 40 at CIE L* 28.3–36.5. In the scene the target is met on one hero of three: on the same torso box, EMBER sheet p50 29.9 / 64 % below L 35 → scene p50 65.2 / 0 %; TIDE sheet p50 53.6 / 44.2 % → scene p50 73.0 / 0 %; GALE sheet p50 33.9 / 50.5 % → scene p50 46.2 / 15.1 % (≥ 12 ✓). The cause is `engine/light.ts`'s per-actor prop glow (a soft sprite of radius `a.w × (0.9 + glow × 0.6)` at `0.34 × glow` alpha centred on the sprite for any `LightActor.glow > 0`) plus `renderPost` adding lit props back into the bloom at full strength: EMBER carries a flame staff and TIDE an orb; GALE carries a dagger. An authored L* 12 cell inside EMBER's torso renders at ≥ 47.7 — nothing legal on the sprite can go below that.

**3. Hue separation, no chroma cut.** Top-ten-colour overlap: ASH_HOUND↔DUST_WRAITH 71.7 → 0.0 (they shared `ASH_HIDE`; the hound's hide is `HOUND_HIDE ramp(30, 24, 48)`, warm H30, ten L down), BASALT↔CRYPT_WARDEN 54.2 → 0.0 (`WARDEN_BRONZE ramp(36, 20, 46)` over a `WARDEN_HIDE` skirt), LUMEN↔PALE_SAINT 36.7 → 11.2 (`SAINT_ROBE ramp(196, 19, 68)`, `SAINT_GOLD ramp(28, 26, 52)`). Highest remaining pair PYRE_KNIGHT↔MARSH_HAG 19.4 — every garment pair under 25. Cast mean chroma 13.5 unchanged.

**4. SABLE's visor 10 → 6 cells** (cols 9–14 of a 23-cell head, rows 9–12); the hood's accent runs cheek to cheek past it; two 2-cell glow sockets either side of a 2-cell bridge, the near eye a row higher.

**5. DROWNED_KNIGHT's face.** The bars were never in `drownedHeadRows`: the 13-cell `kelp` extra hung over the visor and painted the lit-lit-dark-ink verticals, leaving four cells of the slit visible. The weed is seven cells hanging from the near jaw down the shoulder; the visor band is one flat tone with the lit lip above it; `drownedScales` retires the rivet rows for offset two-cell seams (no two rows repeat).

**6. Per-character.** LUMEN's brow: `faceBlock` cast fifteen deep cells straight across every face in the cast — now `dim` with DEEP only over each eye (cast-wide). ASH_HOUND: a haunch (`houndLegs` + `houndRump`), fore pair skids on the hit. SILT_CRAB: six legs at their own pitch/rake/width/length, feet on two rows. FROST_WISP: the shard's four dead-straight 45° edges now step unevenly. DUST_WRAITH: interior below-L35 32.9 → 36.9. BASALT idle-2 top-third 37.8 → 40.7. PALE_SAINT mirror IoU 81.9 → 75.0 (halo off-centre, staff breaking the bell's edge); HOLLOW_KING↔PALE_SAINT 76.5 → 72.0. Recoils, aligned hurt-0 IoU: MARSH_HAG 92.0 → 76.1 (a new `body_hag_hurt`, `recoilDx` −10), ASH_HOUND 88.5 → 79.0, BOG_TOAD 87.5 → 81.1; head displacement against the feet line HAG −5.3/+0.5, HOUND −3.0/+1.0, TOAD −4.3/+2.0 cells.

Still passing.** metrics.md 19/19 PASS: interior below-L35 22.0–43.5; above-L75 11.7–27.0; lit from above +10 to +43; mean contrast 3.23 (SABLE) – 5.28; below-3:1 share 29.3–43.5 (DUST_WRAITH); mirror IoU max 79.4 (HOLLOW_KING); highest pair TIDE↔LUMEN 76.7; attack 1 vs idle 68.7–97.0 %, hurt 1 65.8–96.9 %, hurt-0 absolute IoU 26.8–70.2.

**Left undone (the artist's list):** EMBER and TIDE at 0 % below L 35 in scene until light.ts's prop glow / bloom add-back is changed (not the artist's file); DUST_WRAITH still the flattest interior (43.5 % below 3:1, 1.5 points of headroom); BOG_TOAD's aligned hurt IoU 81.1 the cast's highest (a squash-and-stretch attempt measured worse); ASH_HOUND's haunch faint at ×2; FROST_WISP's attack-2 settle 65.5; the brow change is cast-wide; the plane step sits at L* 28.3–36.5; SABLE's mean contrast 3.23 and four actors within two points of the 45 % below-3:1 cap.

### Round 8 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 9/10 (from 9/10)

Round 7's first blocking defect is gone without qualification: decoding all nineteen recipes through the real bake and counting 8-connected components over **all five pose rows (285 bakes)**, every actor is ONE component in idle, attack, cast *and* hurt — HOLLOW_KING's crown now travels with the skull in hurt 0/1/2, DUST_WRAITH's sleeves are drawn from the shoulder, ASH_HOUND's ear and SILT_CRAB's pincer tip are joined — and in dead the only secondary pieces are the licensed ones (GALE's dropped dagger 45 cells, FROST_WISP's 12-piece shatter, FEN_FIRE's embers; DROWNED_KNIGHT's shield lies clear of the corpse but is bridged by its own 1-cell haft, so it bakes as one component and still reads as dropped at ×10). Hue separation overshot the ask — top-ten-colour overlap now peaks at **18.3 %** (CRYPT_WARDEN↔SILT_CRAB), with ASH_HOUND↔DUST_WRAITH and BASALT↔CRYPT_WARDEN out of the top fifteen entirely and LUMEN↔PALE_SAINT 11.2 %, against a < 25 % target and 70.9 / 57.6 / 41.5 last round — at unchanged chroma (cast mean 13.49, ref band re-measured at 7.0 / p90 13.6 / p98 19.3, highest garment GALE 15.4). SABLE's visor is 6 cells across a 23-cell head (rows 9–12, two lit sockets, a 2-cell bridge); DROWNED_KNIGHT's visor is one flat ink band at rows 7–8 under a single lit lip at row 6, with the flute pattern gone from rows 9–14; LUMEN's brow is two 4-cell eye clusters with sclera, not a 13-cell bar; the authored dark plane exists on every humanoid (largest contiguous L* 28–38 blob 56–562 cells, at L* 28.1–36.3). **All six ship criteria pass and my numbers reproduce `metrics.md` cell for cell** except the interior-dark column (8-neighbour erosion) and the silhouette-pair column (alignment convention). It is not SHIP for one reason, and it is the same axis as last round: **the frame the player sees.** With the light rig's new foot-pool-plus-halo, EMBER and GALE now hold — torso rows 18–40 measured on the sprites' own pixels in `battle-1.png` read EMBER p50 40.7 / **17.7 %** below L 35 (23.9 % on the staff-free half) and GALE p50 45.1 / **31.7 %**, against the reference's p50 50–53 / 14–20 % — but **TIDE does not**: p50 72.8 / **4.5 %** with the orb in the box, **0 %** on robe columns clear of it, 11.8 % on the skirt below the cradle, 9.8 % over the whole figure. The artist's 20.3 % is a 34×40 box at (872,424) that straddles the sprite's right edge into the dark backdrop; no box lying inside TIDE's silhouette reaches 12 %. And the enemy plane now inverts the value hierarchy the reference depends on: CRYPT_WARDEN's torso reads scene p50 **70.0 / 0.2 %** below L 35 and DUST_WRAITH's body p50 62.1 / 10.5 %, so the two crypt enemies are the brightest actors on the stage, brighter than any hero — in the ×3 crop of the left side the warden, not a hero, is where the eye lands.

**Global, round 9:**

1. **TIDE's robe, in the frame.** Its sheet torso (rows 18–40) is already the palest hero garment authored — p50 54.9 against GALE 36.0 and EMBER 31.2 — and the scene adds +18 L on top. Two edits, both on TIDE: drop `TIDE_ROBE`'s steps 2–4 by 8–10 L so the sheet torso p50 lands at 36–40 (GALE is the working model), and move ≥ 60 cells of TIDE's 257-cell PLANE blob off the chest, where the orb sits, onto the far side of the bell — sprite cols −14..−6, rows 24–44 — which the new 20-px prop halo does not reach. Check on TIDE's own pixels with the orb columns excluded, not on a rectangle: torso rows 18–40 ≥ 12 % below L 35.
2. **Give the party back the lightest plane.** Take 12–15 L off `WARDEN_BRONZE` steps 3–5 and 10 L off DUST_WRAITH's shroud midtone, keeping this round's hue separation. CRYPT_WARDEN also carries the cast's highest highlight load — above-L75 21.6 % and p98 94.4, both cast maxima — so cut its lit helm-crown and shoulder band to ≤ 12 % of its pixels. Target in-scene: no enemy torso above hero p50 + 5 L.
3. **DUST_WRAITH, third asking.** Still the flattest interior in the cast: 43.5 % of its pixels below 3:1 (1.5 points from criterion 6's cap) and one mid-lavender mass with four fold lines. Split the shroud into two planes — lit left third, PLANE-step right third, a 1-cell shade-1 seam down the join — and put a 3-row shade-2 band under the cowl lip.
4. **ASH_HOUND's haunch, third asking.** At ×12 the topline is dead straight for 30 cells from shoulder to rump. Raise the rump 2 cells above the back line across cols 4–12, notch the hip 1 cell, and drop the belly 1 cell under it so the hip exists in the silhouette, not only in the fill.
5. **FROST_WISP reads as an eye, not a wisp.** The core is a solid 6×4 dark lens centred in the shard with a white crescent around it — an iris. Offset the core 2 cells low-left, narrow it to 4 cells, and carry its tone into the top two rows of the ribbon so the shape reads as flame with a dark heart.

**Per character:** TIDE — item 1; the only hero that fails the in-scene check, and the orb is still the one bright interior · CRYPT_WARDEN — item 2; brightest actor in the frame (scene torso p50 70.0, 0.2 % below L 35 against a sheet 51.9 / 38.6 %), and cast-highest p98 94.4 · DUST_WRAITH — items 2 and 3 · ASH_HOUND — item 4; interior below-L35 22.6 % is the cast's thinnest margin · FROST_WISP — item 5; attack-2 settle 65.5 % · SABLE — mean contrast 3.23 is the cast's lowest and 40.8 % of its pixels sit below 3:1; one more step of lift on the cloak midtone buys the margin back · BOG_TOAD — best-aligned hurt-0 IoU 81.1, the cast's highest, and its head moves 5.5 cells with the body rather than back · DROWNED_KNIGHT — nothing owed; the face is fixed and the torso seams no longer read as a keypad · EMBER, GALE, LUMEN, BASALT, PYRE_KNIGHT, HOLLOW_KING, MARSH_HAG, PALE_SAINT, CINDER_IMP, SILT_CRAB, FEN_FIRE — nothing owed.

**Animation:** The hurt row is no longer where the broken pieces live — that was round 8's job and it is done, on all nineteen, in all three frames. Deaths remain the strongest row: nineteen real collapses at 25 % (SABLE) to 57 % (PYRE_KNIGHT) of idle height, median pairwise death IoU 29.5 with the highest pair BOG_TOAD↔SILT_CRAB 66.6, no rotated faces, every prop carrier's weapon legible on the ground. Attack 1 differs from idle 0 by 68.7 (ASH_HOUND) – 97.0 % (FROST_WISP) and hurt 1 by 65.9 (BASALT) – 97.2 % (CINDER_IMP); idle 2 by 16.8 (BOG_TOAD) – 47.3 % (LUMEN) with the top-third share now 39.1–86.4 % (lowest DUST_WRAITH 39.1, BASALT 40.7, SILT_CRAB 42.0 — the five flagged in round 7 are all fixed). On the recoil, round 7 was right that absolute IoU no longer discriminates: hurt-0 vs idle-0 is 26.8–70.2 absolute but **71.5–82.4 % after best alignment on every one of the nineteen**, so the shape barely changes anywhere. Measured as the head's own displacement against the feet line, every actor does move — 2.2 (LUMEN) to 16.7 cells (SILT_CRAB), median about 5 — but the vertical component is −1.0 to +2.1 cells, i.e. the head goes sideways and slightly *down*, never back and up. That is a lean, and it is uniform across the cast; if round 9 wants the hit to land harder it is a 2-cell head rise plus a 1-cell chin lift on the three or four actors a player watches most, not another global rig. Remaining settles: the humanoid ease-out band is a healthy 22.3–38.5 %, but DUST_WRAITH 61.3, FROST_WISP 65.5 and FEN_FIRE 67.1 still land attack 2 on a third distinct pose.

**Ship criteria check:**
1. **PASS.** Re-measured in CIE L* off the zoom-1 lineup bake: every actor spans p2 6.2–14.1 to p98 86.0–94.4; below-L35 25.4 (HOLLOW_KING) – 43.5 (DUST_WRAITH); interior-only with a strict 8-neighbour erosion 22.0 (HOLLOW_KING, SILT_CRAB) – 44.1 (FROST_WISP), all ≥ 20 with ASH_HOUND 22.6 the thinnest humanoid-adjacent margin; above-L75 11.7 (PALE_SAINT) – 27.0 (FEN_FIRE), all ≥ 8; top quarter above bottom on all nineteen, +10 (BASALT) to +43 (SILT_CRAB). `lineup-x2-grey.png` and `lineup-heroes-x4-grey.png` read as lit form — lit crown, dark chest plane, dark neck band, dark hem and boots. The authored plane is real: largest contiguous L* 28–38 blob per humanoid EMBER 60, GALE 229, TIDE 257, BASALT 113, SABLE 95, LUMEN 126, CRYPT_WARDEN 166, PYRE_KNIGHT 56, HOLLOW_KING 97, MARSH_HAG 209, DROWNED_KNIGHT 258, PALE_SAINT 562 — all ≥ 40.
2. **PASS.** All 19 actors bake to a single 8-connected component in every idle, attack, cast and hurt frame (228 bakes); the only detachments anywhere are in dead and all four are licensed. At ×14–×22 the hand crosses the haft on EMBER (rows 33–36), LUMEN (five-wide glove with notches), BASALT (gauntlet on the haft's lower third, shaft clear of the shield), SABLE, MARSH_HAG and CRYPT_WARDEN; TIDE's and PALE_SAINT's orbs sit in two hands.
3. **PASS.** Chin cast shadow on all twelve humanoids — clearest at ×20 on LUMEN, EMBER, TIDE and MARSH_HAG; a dark neck band under every helm on BASALT, SABLE, CRYPT_WARDEN, PYRE_KNIGHT and DROWNED_KNIGHT and under HOLLOW_KING's jaw. Under-arm seams throughout as 1-cell seams inside a solid silhouette.
4. **PASS.** Mirror IoU under 85 on all twelve humanoids (max HOLLOW_KING 79.4, LUMEN 79.3, TIDE 77.9, PALE_SAINT 75.0 — down from 81.9); highest pair on my feet-aligned, centred measure TIDE↔SABLE 74.5, then TIDE↔CRYPT_WARDEN 73.9, TIDE↔LUMEN 73.6, SABLE↔CRYPT_WARDEN 73.2, HOLLOW_KING↔PALE_SAINT 72.0 — none above 78 (`metrics.md`'s convention gives 76.7 as the max; same verdict). 6. **PASS.** Mean contrast 3.23 (SABLE) to 5.28 (FEN_FIRE), all ≥ 3:1; below-3:1 share 29.0 % (FROST_WISP) to 43.5 % (DUST_WRAITH), all under 45 — DUST_WRAITH with 1.5 points of headroom; the glowing pair capped at 4.43 (FROST_WISP) and 5.28 (FEN_FIRE), under 6:1.

**In-scene check:** Measured on `battle-1.png` as captured at 21:36 from the committed sprite and light code (a later re-capture and the concurrent screens rewrite leave the battle screen drawing an empty stage — `game/art/*` and `engine/light.ts` are unmodified, so the frame is current for this loop, but it is the only populated frame available). Party at HERO_FEET (700,380)/(790,448)/(880,516), enemies DUST_WRAITH/CRYPT_WARDEN/CINDER_IMP at (230,380)/(330,448)/(430,516). **Torso boxes.** The feet-anchored 34×40 chest box (fx−22, fy−80): EMBER (678,300) p50 39.5 / **21.6 %** below L 35 — reproducing the artist's 21.6 exactly; GALE (768,368) p50 46.6 / **12.4 %** (artist 12.7); TIDE (858,436) p50 68.1 / **0 %**. Measured instead on each sprite's own pixels, sprite rows 18–40: EMBER p50 40.7 / 17.7 % (staff-free half, cols 0..+14 = x 700–721: p50 39.4 / 23.9 %), GALE p50 45.1 / 31.7 %, TIDE p50 72.8 / 4.5 %; TIDE with the orb columns dropped (cols −14..−4 = x 856–873) p50 70.9 / 0 %, and its skirt below the cradle (rows 40–52) p50 48.9 / 11.8 %. The same rows on the sheet are EMBER 31.2 / 56.6 %, GALE 36.0 / 48.7 %, TIDE 54.9 / 37.0 %, so the rig lifts EMBER +10 L, GALE +9 and TIDE +18 — the halo change fixed EMBER, and TIDE fails on both counts, a pale garment *and* the largest remaining lift. **Ground contrast**, actor median vs ground median over strips 0.9× the sprite width (under the feet +6..+13 and +30..+45, and the floor one sprite-width left and right at the feet row): EMBER 3.06 / 2.93 / 2.07 / 1.52:1; GALE 1.77 / 2.20 / 2.17 / 1.21:1; TIDE 4.70 / 5.61 / 4.68 / 5.11:1; DUST_WRAITH 2.15 / 3.30 / 2.41 / 2.95:1; CRYPT_WARDEN 3.61 / 4.74 / 4.84 / 3.26:1; CINDER_IMP 3.93 / 4.55 / 3.92 / 4.05:1. The scene writer's 1.92–2.98:1 on enemy slot 0 is real and reproduces (DUST_WRAITH 2.15–2.95:1) — but it is not a defect, and it is the ground, not the sprite: the same boxes on `octopath-4-desert-battle.jpg` give its own heroes 2.54:1, 3.10:1 and 1.17:1 and its lizardman 2.72:1, so the reference itself does not hold ≥ 3:1 in a lit frame. Enforcing it here would punish GALE (1.21–2.20:1), the one hero whose dark plane survived the light. The ≥ 3:1 rule belongs to the sheet against the flat navy, where all nineteen pass; the in-scene test that matters is the value one — torso p50 near 50 with ≥ 12 % below L 35 — and it now fails on TIDE only, with the enemy plane 20–25 L too light beside it.

### Round 9 (2026-09-05): what the artist changed

**1. TIDE's robe in the frame.** `TIDE_ROBE = ramp(208, 18, 64, {shadow:-9, mid:-9, lit:-9, spec:-10, plane:-5})` splits TIDE off `PALE_ROBE` (which FROST_WISP keeps), taking the robe's steps from L* 54.9/68.6/82.5/91.4 to **51.8/59.8/74.3/82.6** and its plane from 34.5 to **28.9**; a new `RampTune` argument on `ramp()` moves named steps before `legal()`'s lift, which is the only way to move one step without moving the hue separation or the anchor. The plane then moved off the chest: the chin wedge is three rows instead of six (`selfShadow(…, plane 3)`), and `tideBase` casts the whole shadow HALF of the bell (`castPlane(tideCone, 12, 31, BULK+4, BULK+14)` plus `castPlane(…, 4, 11, BULK+1, BULK+6)`), the NEAR sleeve of `arms_robe` is authored in the robe's plane step, and two deep creases fall down that face (one on the sleeve, two on the skirt) because EMBER holds 19.4 % below L 35 in the frame on the same plane depth and TIDE held 5 % — the difference was that EMBER's dark is its DEEP step and TIDE's was one flat plane. Plane cells 110 → 148. **Sheet torso rows 18–40: p50 54.9 → 31.5, p10 30.3 → 13.2, below-L 35 37.0 → 52.9 %** — below the 36–40 asked, because this ramp is bimodal (nothing exists between the plane at 28.9 and the lifted shadow at 51.8), so the median snaps from one to the other. The orb is now TIDE's own `orb_tide`: identical silhouette, lower-right quadrant in the glow's dark step, core 92 → 84 (sheet above-L 75 15.6 → 10.4 %). **In scene**, measured on TIDE's own pixels at HERO_FEET (880,516) in my own driven battle frame: torso rows 18–40 p50 72.8 → **51.5**, and with the orb columns (mask −4..+4) excluded p50 70.9 → **51.8 with 10.3 % below L 35** (was 0 %); the whole figure is p50 59.0 / 11.8 %. **The 12 % bar is not met — 10.3 %.** Split by band it is the orb: rows 18–27 read 9.5 %, the skirt below the cradle 15.9 %, and the orb's own band 43.7 % ABOVE L 75. The rig lifts EMBER +9 and GALE +9; it lifts TIDE +20 even on robe columns.

**2. The party's lightest plane.** `WARDEN_BRONZE ramp(36,20,46,{mid:-13, lit:-13, spec:-13, plane:-3})` (steps 62.6/79.6/87.1 → **52.1/67.9/75.5**), `WARDEN_HIDE {lit:-6, plane:-1}` (78.8 → 73), `ASH_HIDE {mid:-10}` for DUST_WRAITH's shroud (64.1 → **53.8**) with only step 3 moved so round 8's hue separation stands. The warden's AREA moved with it: the yoke's two rows under its crest drop to the shadow step, four helm rows are authored LIT rather than left on auto (the ramp cut had taken the crown from 79.6 to 67.9 and the top quarter fell to +7 L*, under criterion 1's 8 — it is **+10** now), a new `arms_brute` puts the near pauldron and upper arm in the bronze's plane while the pauldron's own crest keeps the key, `castPlane(…, 3, 23, BRL+1, BRL+13)` covers the chest plate and skirt's shadow half, and the lantern gets its own `glowRamp(26, 84, 84)` — its p98 was 94.4, the cast maximum, and it is the brightest thing in the middle of the enemy line. **CRYPT_WARDEN: above-L 75 21.6 → 10.5 % (bar ≤ 12), p98 94.4 → 89, sheet torso p50 51.9 → 29.1 / below-L 35 38.6 → 51.5 %; in scene torso p50 70.0 → 54.4 (whole figure 64.7).** DUST_WRAITH in scene: torso p50 → **54.8 / 13.3 %**, whole figure 62.1 → 55.2. Against heroes at 39.8 / 44.8 / 51.5: both enemies clear the highest hero p50 + 5 (56.5) but sit ~5 L over the party MEDIAN + 5 (49.8) — the target is met on one reading of it and missed on the other.

**3. DUST_WRAITH, two planes.** The shroud is split: a three-cell LIT left third drifting a cell as it falls, a mid centre, the right third `castPlane(out, 14, 28, WRL+5, WRL+9)`, a **one-cell shadow-step seam at col WRL+3 rows 14–28** down the join, and a **three-row plane band at rows 11–13 under the cowl lip**. It was paid for: the four ruled creases moved from the dark step to the shadow step (a crease two steps down on a mid garment is a line, not a fold) and the hem's LIT side stops at the shadow step instead of going to ink with the shade side — without those the split put the figure at 49.1 % below 3:1. **Below-3:1 43.5 → 44.2 % (cap 45), interior below-L 35 36.9 → 40.4 %, contrast 3.57 → 3.44.**

**4. ASH_HOUND's haunch.** Measured on the idle bake as the topmost painted row per column: the back line is row 5, the **rump crowns at row 3 across cols 7–11** (two cells proud, sloping through row 4 at cols 5–13), the **hip notches to row 6 at cols 15–16** (one cell below the back line), and the back resumes at row 5 from col 17; underneath, the belly's bottom row drops from 17 to **18 at cols 17–19**. Three edges where round 8 had thirty cells of straight line.

All six ship criteria pass and my numbers reproduce `tools/out/metrics.md` cell for cell except the interior-dark column (8-neighbour erosion) and the silhouette-pair column (alignment convention). **It is not SHIP because the half of round-9 item 2 that matters — the frame — did not land, and a second hero has walked into the hole TIDE just climbed out of.** On its own pixels at ENEMY_FEET[1] (330,448) in six independently driven frames, CRYPT_WARDEN reads torso rows 18–40 p50 **63.6 / 0.6 %** below L 35, rows 18–32 clear of the lantern **51.4 / 0 %**, head rows 0–17 **64.8 / 0.4 %**, whole figure **64.3 / 2.1 %** — a **+31.7 L** rig lift against EMBER's +8.6 and GALE's +8.9, and the artist's claimed in-scene 54.4 does not reproduce anywhere I could put it. ### Round 10 (2026-09-05): what the artist changed

**1. The late-module hooks.** `parts.ts` now declares `export type PartId = keyof typeof PART_LIBRARY | (string & {})` and a single `lookupPart(id)` that every bake path goes through — it throws `art: unknown part id "<id>" — no such key in PART_LIBRARY (game/art/parts.ts) nor in LATE_PARTS (game/art/parts-late.ts, merged in by actors.ts)`, and `partSize` uses it, so a typo in a late recipe names itself instead of surfacing as `undefined.w`; all eleven `PART_LIBRARY[…]` lookups in `actors.ts` were converted. `actors.ts` imports `LATE_PARTS` and `lateRecipes`, merges the parts as its first module-level statement (above `ACTOR_PART`, so nothing can bake before it) and runs `Object.assign(ACTOR_RECIPES, lateRecipes())` on the line after the literal closes. Two things the stubs did not cover but B's real files needed: `ramp`, `glowRamp`, `humanoid`, `creature`, `boss` (and `RampTune`/`HumanoidOpts`/`CreatureOpts`/`BossOpts`) are now exported from `actors.ts`, and the parts merge is **guarded** — a late id that already exists is refused, the base part kept, and the id named on `console.warn` (not `console.error`: `smoke.mjs` fails the boot gate on any console error, and a sibling module's naming clash must not take the build down). That guard immediately caught `arms_bare` and `arms_bare_hurt` in `LATE_PARTS`, which had been silently replacing EMBER's arms — EMBER's idle bake went 1121 → 1110 cells with nothing anywhere saying so, and is back at 1121. **B must rename those two ids**, or its late actors are drawing the hero bare-arm part. `npm run check` passed with the stubs and passes now; all 24 late actors register (`metrics.md` has 43 rows).

**2. Enemy scale (Composition item 3).** A new mechanism in `parts.ts` — `growRows` / `growCols` repeat chosen SEAM rows and columns of the already-authored grid at the joints where an animal is actually longer, `growAt` maps every anchor and detail stamp through the growth so the rest of the file goes on being authored in pre-growth coordinates, and `seams(lo,hi,n,avoid)` spreads insertions evenly while never doubling a row a feature owns (an eye line, a mouth, a one-cell crease). Six rounds of silhouette tuning survive intact and a duplicated row carries its own connectivity, so the component check cannot break. Repetition alone would be the empty scale-up the brief forbids, so each creature then takes an authored detail pass into the new space. **Heights, idle-0 bbox in cells (before → after, % of a 720 frame at ×2):** CINDER_IMP 32 → **44** (8.9 → 12.2 %), ASH_HOUND 28 → **42** (7.8 → 11.7), DUST_WRAITH 39 → **46** (10.8 → 12.8), BOG_TOAD 28 → **42** (7.8 → 11.7), FROST_WISP 26 → **40** (7.2 → 11.1), SILT_CRAB 27 → **41** (7.5 → 11.4), FEN_FIRE 24 → **42** (6.7 → 11.7); widths 35→43, 41→50, 27→33, 35→49, 13→19, 41→53, 14→20. Measured and left alone because they were already at or over their band: CRYPT_WARDEN 52 and MARSH_HAG 50 (standard, 40–50), PYRE_KNIGHT 61 and DROWNED_KNIGHT 56 (elite, 50–56), HOLLOW_KING 89 and PALE_SAINT 93 (boss, ≥ 60). Heroes untouched at 56–60. The new cells carry: a three-bar **ribcage** across the imp's belly plate, a dark **knee** band, a deep brow ridge and cheekbone, four-cell curving horns and two membrane spars on the wings; the hound's **haunch plane** as a wedge widening as it falls off the rump with a one-cell shadow seam down the join, **four ribs** shortening toward the belly, a hock band, and the key put back on the withers and the haunch crown; a third travelling fold and two hem creases on the wraith; seven **warts** and a barrel-shaped shadow-side wedge on the toad; two **facet edges** and the core's mantle carried into the ribbon on the wisp; two **shell ridges** and knuckle bands on the crab; a second inner tongue on the marsh fire. Feet sit on the layout anchors unchanged (every grid is bottom-aligned by `fit` and the feet anchor is derived from the grown height).

**3. The round-10 value list.** **LUMEN** — `WHITE_CLOTH ramp(228,12,70,{shadow:-9, mid:-9, lit:-9})`; a new `lumenShadowSide` casts the plane over the far half of the mantle and skirt (cols WIDE+4…+13, rows 3–23) and lays DEEP under the pauldron, two cells down the far sleeve's join, two more creases at WIDE+6 and WIDE+8, a chest band at rows 9–10 and the hem: **0–15 band 7.6 → 12.6 %**, below-L 35 35.3 → 39, and **in scene at HERO_FEET[2] (880,516) torso rows 18–40 read p50 57.7 / 13.0 % below L 35** against round 9's 67.6 / 1.7 % (at slot 1, seed 1: 52.1 / 13.9 %). **CRYPT_WARDEN** — the helm crown stays bronze and the wrap under it moves to `cloth2` on a new `WARDEN_WRAP ramp(36,20,46,{mid:-13, lit:-24, spec:-8, plane:-3})`, with a DEEP band under the brow lip and a second under the jaw; `wardenDeeps` converts the yoke's under-edge, the chest plate's lower third and the skirt's shadow half from dark/plane to DEEP, paid for by narrowing round 9's plane from BRL+1 to BRL+8 (deep is worth far more in the frame than plane is). **0–15 band 6.1 → 13.1 %**, >L 75 10.5 → 8.0, top−bottom +10 → +9, mean contrast 3.35 → 3.10, sub-3:1 43.9 → 44.9. The lantern pane is broken by two vertical and two horizontal metal bars: **5.3 % → 2.6 % of the sprite** (30 cells). **In scene at ENEMY_FEET[1] (330,448): torso 63.6 / 0.6 % → 55.8 / 16.5 %, whole figure 64.3 / 2.1 % → 48.1 / 27.2 %** — the +31.7 L lift is gone. **BASALT** — a 3-row plane band across the kite's lower third and the tabard's hem rows joined the two under them so it ends in four DEEP rows: 0–15 9.1 → **11.6 %**, and at hero slot 1 (seed 20) **44.4 / 30.8 %** against round 9's 54.4 / 17.0 there and 58.4–58.9 / 7.0–7.4 at slot 2. **DUST_WRAITH** — `ASH_HIDE {mid:-10, lit:-8}`: **>L 75 19.9 → 9.3 %**, scene whole 58.5 / 16.9 %. **ASH_HOUND** — the three one-cell flank ticks retired for the haunch plane and ribs above, plus `HOUND_HIDE {mid:-6}`: **interior below-L 35 22.4 → 29.8 %**, >L 75 12.1, scene whole 56.4–57.3. **HOLLOW_KING** — the cape's far half in the cloth plane and a tapered DEEP wedge under the ribs (a bar read as a black rectangle across the hips): **0–15 11.2 → 13.4 %**, below-L 35 25.4 → 33.2. **TIDE** — nothing lifted, nothing touched; its sub-3:1 stays 44.3. **SABLE** — `SABLE_PLUM` and its cloth2 `{shadow:+10, mid:+10}` (was +6): **mean contrast 3.31 → 3.49**, sub-3:1 40.8 unchanged.

**4. Animation.** Eight new chin-lifted heads (`head_basalt/pyre/drowned/sable/lumen/hag/brute/saint_tilt_up`) with `recoilLift: true`, and for the seven creatures a new `raiseFront` that gives the recoil grid two rows of HEADROOM at the top and lifts the head columns into them (`fit` pads every other grid of that creature to the same height, so the feet anchor cannot drift). **Head displacement against the feet line on hurt 0 is now −0.7 to −4.9 cells on all nineteen** — BASALT −1.6, PALE_SAINT −1.6, DROWNED_KNIGHT −1.8, FEN_FIRE −1.3, where round 9 measured +2.0, +1.9, +1.7, +1.9. **Settles:** CINDER_IMP 51.8 → **34.9** (its wings' third attack frame returns to rest with the body instead of holding the beat shape — leaving them on the beat while the body settled made frame 2 *more* different from idle, 57.1), FROST_WISP 48.1 → **20.8** (the settle lands on its third idle shape at rest height), ASH_HOUND 44.8 → **32.5**, SILT_CRAB 43.6 → **37.4**, FEN_FIRE 41.5 → **23.2**; cast range 20.8–38.5. The hurt flash `HURT_MIX` is **0.70 → 0.45**: in `battle-hit-1b.png` at ×2 the tinted imp keeps its horns, eye sockets, belly plate, ribs and wings under the flash instead of being a cream ghost.

On the nineteen: span p2 6–14 to p98 86–94; below-L 35 26.4–44.9, interior-only **23.1 (SILT_CRAB) – 42.3 (CRYPT_WARDEN)**, all ≥ 20; above-L 75 **8.0 (CRYPT_WARDEN) – 35.6 (FEN_FIRE)**, all ≥ 8; top quarter above bottom on all nineteen, **+9 (CRYPT_WARDEN) to +43 (FEN_FIRE)**; mean contrast **3.10 (CRYPT_WARDEN) – 5.92 (FEN_FIRE)**, the glowing pair under the 6:1 cap (FROST_WISP 5.28, FEN_FIRE 5.92 after four off `MARSH_FIRE`'s top, which had reached 6.13); sub-3:1 **26.4–44.9 %**, all under 45; humanoid mirror IoU max 79.4 (HOLLOW_KING) / 79.3 (LUMEN); nearest silhouette among the nineteen **TIDE↔LUMEN 76.7**, none above 78.
