Its head and muzzle also still read muddled at ×10 — the growth made the flank the dominant mass and I re-cut the topline key twice without fully resolving it.
- **CRYPT_WARDEN is now the tightest actor in the cast on three axes at once**: sub-3:1 44.9 % against the 45 cap (one cell), mean contrast 3.10 (cast lowest, was SABLE's 3.31), top−bottom +9 (cast lowest). The three moved together — deep mass is what the frame needed and every deep cell is sub-3:1 by construction.
- **CINDER_IMP's mean contrast fell 3.27 → 3.17** (the ribs, knee band and neck plane), the second-lowest in the cast.
- **Slot 2 is only reachable on the seeds that open with three heroes**; TIDE and BASALT never appeared there, so their in-scene numbers are slot 1, as round 9's critic also reported.
- **HOLLOW_KING and PALE_SAINT were not re-measured in scene** — a boss does not appear in a first-room battle driven from the opening.
- **I ran `npx prettier --write` on `parts.ts` by mistake**, which reformatted the whole file to 80 columns and double quotes. I restored a wide single-quote style (`--print-width 200 --single-quote`), so the file reads as it did, but roughly 270 lines of the diff are formatting churn rather than semantic change.
- **The creature grids all gained two rows of headroom** (`HEAD_RISE`) so the recoil can raise a head — a cast-wide structural change the round-10 list did not ask for, though it is invisible in the bake (blank rows, feet anchored as before).
- **`arms_bare` / `arms_bare_hurt` collide with `LATE_PARTS`.** My merge keeps the base part and warns; until B renames them, its late actors draw EMBER's bare arms.
- Exporting `ramp`/`glowRamp`/`humanoid`/`creature`/`boss` and four option interfaces widened `actors.ts`'s surface beyond what the hook list named; B's file does not compile without them.

### Late enemies (2026-09-05): what artist B authored

**SKY RUINS (act 3)** — warm sandstone and wind-green against `RUINS_GROUND.lit` #57506f, the lightest of the four floors, so grey stone would be a hole in it. **RUIN_RAPTOR** (43 cells) is a diver at rest: wings folded into two peaks *above* the shoulder line, a hooked beak on a short pitched neck, a stiff counterweighting tail; GALE DIVE throws both wings open and drives the talons nine cells forward. Straw plume `ramp(52,21,44)` over slate-green coverts. **WIND_SPRITE** (38) is a coil of moving air — three ribbon arcs winding into a lit heart with two slits, deliberately no facet anywhere on it (the frost wisp is the shard, this is the wind); pale-green cloth over a `glowRamp(96,58,93)` core. **RUIN_SENTINEL** (50) guards: a lintel of shoulder three cells past the hips, a rune band that is the only lit thing on its chest, a keystone belt, a slab skirt, and a ward slab it swings on a short haft — a keystone helm tapering *downward* with one carved slot. **DROWNED_CLOUD** (39) is the WATER foil, all mass and no limb: four lobes of unequal size over a rain curtain hanging from its own base course, two dim lights low in it, DOWNPOUR dropping the curtain nine cells with the bolt drawn *on* the strands. **STORM_DRAKE** (52, elite) is the wingspan — two membranes at different pitches, a nozzle head on a long neck, four legs on four pitches; sea-green hide, brass-free horn. **SKYFALLEN_KING** (75, boss) has one wing sheared to a stump of masonry, a mantle torn to three tatters, a cracked crown askew over a slit dome, and a greatsword of falling *stone* (bone ramp, so it reads against his own violet plate) — storm-indigo surcoat over tarnished silver.

**ASHEN FORGE (act 4)** — the iron is COLD and the only warm thing in the pack is the fire *inside* it, because the floor (#5b4438) and the key (#ff5a2e) are both amber. **FORGE_GOLEM** (49) is built round one oversized fist: a squat riveted automaton with a furnace seam down its chest, a low hood with a three-bar grille, a slag boulder on a stub of chain; cold blue iron over dull ochre slag. **CINDER_WOLF** (40) is leggier than the crypt's hound, its topline broken by seven ember-lit spines, tail up and forward, ember cracks along the flank, and a hind pair on different geometry from the fore. **SMITH_PRIEST** (46) is narrow shoulders under a wide leather apron — the inverse of the sentinel's T — a stole over one shoulder, a bellows at the far hip, tongs closed on a hot billet, and a **stoop** that takes his outline off both vault columns. **STEAM_WRAITH** (46) is a legless column of five billows with a hollow face and two vent arms, no hard edge on it anywhere. **FURNACE_KNIGHT** (56, elite) wears banded plate over a scorched tabard with a chimney venting off the far pauldron and a visor that is a furnace *door*; scorched rust iron, split from the golem's cold iron by hue. **FORGE_SAINT** (71, boss) is a crucible in vestments — the torso opens on white fire under a soot-black chasuble, a connected ember ring sits *on* the mitre, and it thrusts a hammer-sceptre.

**SUNKEN VAULT (act 5)** — bronze and kelp, because the floor (#3e6d7c) is teal and teal on teal disappears. **DROWNED_SENTINEL** (52) is tall and narrow where the crypt's drowned knight is hunched: a barnacled verdigris cuirass over a long scale skirt, a kelp veil off an open-faced helm, a pike it thrusts. **VAULT_JELLY** (42) is a bell with a bioluminescent ring low inside it over ten stingers of unequal length — the stingers *are* the strike. **TIDE_ORACLE** (52) cradles a scrying shell in two cupped hands under a coral crown three cells wider than her shoulders (the only figure in the game whose head is the widest part of the silhouette) over a split-tail robe whose weight-side tail hangs four rows lower. **WIND_EEL** (42) is a ribbon body in a standing S with a brass sail fin down its whole back and sparks at the jaw — the one thing in the vault that reads fast. **LEVIATHAN_SPAWN** (52, elite) is the coil: three stacked loops with a wedge skull rising off the top and nine teeth on the strike. **SUNKEN_KING** (72, boss) has legs gone below the knee into kelp, a reliquary lamp set into its chest, a face of weed, a coral crown, a trident, and a broken anchor over the far shoulder — the asymmetric mass that took it off the Forge Saint.

**STORM SPIRE (act 6)** — brass and granite over a blue-grey floor (#5b6484). **LIGHTNING_HAWK** (40) is already falling where the ruins' raptor stands mantled: wings swept back into a delta, body pitched nose-down, legs tucked, a bolt drawn *on* one wing on the strike. **GALE_MONK** (51) is the only humanoid of the 24 carrying no weapon — an open palm is the strike and prayer beads wound round that hand are what its fist crosses; bare arms and legs, a wide low stance no other figure has. **SPIRE_WARDEN** (53) is braced: brass shoulder plates, an open face under a weather-vane spur, a short storm cape off one shoulder, and a staff whose lamp is *caged* so the pack's brightest pane is broken by construction. **EMBER_ELEMENTAL** (42) inverts the fen fire — a dark clinker core with nine embers orbiting on a ring, its dark at the centre and its light at the edge. **THUNDER_COLOSSUS** (51, elite) is granite bound in brass with two conductor rods and an arc above the shoulder line. **SPIRE_SERAPH** (65, boss) is six wings at six different spans and pitches, drawn from the shoulder, over a brass mask with no eyes in it and a lance it thrusts.

`tools/out/metrics.md` is **24/24 PASS** on the acts 3–6 rows (the one non-PASS row in the whole 43-actor table is ASH_HOUND, `>L75 7.5 %`, from artist A's in-flight rescale, not mine). Every pose row of every actor is **one 8-connected component** — no floating prop, crest, wing tip, talon, tooth or spark anywhere, including hurt and dead. Palette overlap over the top ten colours peaks at **22.0 %** (DUST_WRAITH↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS), every pair under 25; late-pack mean chroma **11.97**, whole-cast **12.69** over 43 actors. Highest silhouette pair in the cast **77.5** (RUIN_SENTINEL↔DROWNED_SENTINEL), all under 78; humanoid mirror IoU max **81.7**, all under 85.

**Sheet groups added** — `tools/lineup.ts` gains `ruins`, `forge`, `vault`, `spire` and `late` (all 24), resolved before the comma-list branch, plus the thirteen new humanoids/bosses in `HUMANOIDS` so the mirror-IoU criterion applies to them. `node tools/capture.mjs shot url=/tools/lineup.html?group=ruins&zoom=4 name=late-ruins-x4` works as specified. **`capture.mjs` needed no change**: its `metrics.md` comes from `sheet=lineup` at `group=all`, which is `Object.keys(ACTOR_RECIPES)`, so all 24 rows appeared the moment they registered; `sheets actor=<ID>` already shoots any one actor's pose sheet.

**Left undone (honest)**
- **The two birds still out-value the party in scene.** On each actor's own pixels over its own biome's diorama: RUIN_RAPTOR p50 67.5 against GALE 61.0 (+6.5) and LIGHTNING_HAWK 63.2 against GALE 57.0 (+6.2), where the target is +5. The forge and vault packs are inside it (FORGE_GOLEM 55.5 vs 56.0, DROWNED_SENTINEL 60.0 vs 57.1, TIDE_ORACLE 57.1 vs 57.1).
- **No enemy meets the in-scene ≥12 % below L 35 bar** (3.7–18 %) — but neither do the heroes in these four biomes (GALE 3.4–7.8 %, TIDE 8.3–16.2 %), where the same heroes hold 32 % in the crypt. The acts 3–6 light rigs lift everything; that is `engine/light.ts` / `backdrops.ts`, not my two files.
- **I never saw an act-3 battle.** `playfull acts=3 seed=7` ran 774 s and the party wiped in act 2 (`PLAYFULL OK`, "the run ended; act 2, score 160"), so no driven act-3 frame exists. What I looked at instead is my own scratch diorama page (snapshot only, `tools/stage.ts`) driving the real `createLight` at HIGH tier with `backdropFor(biome)` and the battle screen's own `ENEMY_FEET`/`HERO_FEET` anchors — the light rig is real, the run is not. **Seen in scene: all four packs' first three members. Seen only on the sheet: the three elites and the four bosses.**
- **The authoring layer is duplicated.** parts.ts keeps `part`, `autoShade` and every band helper module-private, so `parts-late.ts` carries its own copy of that layer against the same alphabet. A change to parts.ts's `autoShade` would need mirroring here. Same for `faceBlock`, `crown`, `armsPart`, `armsRecoil`, `fingersPart`, `fallenHeadRows` and `scaleRows`.
- **My humanoids get no arms swap on a hit.** `RECOIL_ARMS` is a module const in actors.ts keyed by arms part id and I cannot add to it, so the recoil is carried by `recoilBody` (a sheared torso, which moves the arms with the shoulder anchor) plus the head tilt and `recoilLift` — not by the `arms_*_hurt` grids I authored, which are registered but unreachable.
- **The humanoid silhouettes are a family.** They clear 78 pairwise, but on `late-x2-sil.png` the nine legged figures read as block-torso-plus-two-posts at ×2; the creatures carry the variety.
- Thin margins: SPIRE_WARDEN and VAULT_JELLY sit at 44.6 % below 3:1 (cap 45); DROWNED_SENTINEL 8.4 % and SPIRE_WARDEN 8.6 % above L 75 (bar 8); TIDE_ORACLE's plane blob is 48 cells (bar 40). EMBER_ELEMENTAL's chroma 21.9 and contrast 5.17 are licensed-light-source numbers like FEN_FIRE's, not garment ones.
- FURNACE_KNIGHT's greathammer head reads as a bar across the shoulder rather than a hammer at ×4; SKYFALLEN_KING's stone greatsword reads as a strap over the plate at ×2. Both are held (fingers over haft, one component); neither reads as a weapon at battle scale.

### Round 10 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 9/10 (from 9/10)

Round 9's blocking defect is genuinely and completely closed, and every sheet-side item on the round-10 list reproduces cell for cell on my own bakes off HEAD (67440ab): LUMEN's 0–15 band 7.6 → **12.6 %**, CRYPT_WARDEN's 6.1 → **13.1 %**, BASALT's 9.1 → **11.6 %**, HOLLOW_KING's 11.2 → **13.4 %**, DUST_WRAITH >L 75 19.9 → **9.3 %**, SABLE's mean contrast 3.31 → **3.49**, CRYPT_WARDEN >L 75 → **8.0** with mean contrast 3.10 and top−bottom +9.3 — every one of those numbers lands where artist A said it would. In the frame the warden is fixed without qualification: on its own eroded mask at ENEMY_FEET[1] (330,448) its torso reads **57.9 / 13.8 %** below L 35 against round 9's 63.6 / 0.6 %, its head band (rows 0–17) **48.4 / 35.8 %** against 64.8 / 0.4 %, and its ground contrast falls 3.05 → **1.92:1**, so the actor three critics said the eye landed on is now the dimmest, lowest-contrast, most sunken figure on the stage. LUMEN and BASALT's slot-2 failures are gone (LUMEN 67.6/1.7 → **49.4–53.7 / 12.3–13.4 %**; BASALT 58.4/7.0 → **55.4–57.8 / 17.7–18.6 %**), every hero at every slot I could reach now clears the 12 % bar, HURT_MIX 0.45 is real (the flashed warden keeps **97 %** of its own value spread and 413 colours against 279 calm), the chin lift holds on all nineteen (crown row rises 1 cell on 16 of 19, holds on 3, sinks on none), the settles land in a 20.8–38.5 band, and the seven creatures stand at **11.1–12.8 %** of the frame in 40–46 cells, inside the standard band for the first time. Against that, three things. **The enemy plane still out-values the party**: over 24 driven crypt seeds an enemy torso sits above the best hero's p50 + 5 on **20 of 24** and above the party median + 5 on **22 of 24** — DUST_WRAITH is the new offender at **55.9–59.1** (13 seeds), CINDER_IMP 52.2–53.6 (9), CRYPT_WARDEN 57.3–58.2 (7) — and ASH_HOUND's flank (rows 0–17) reads **70.1 / 2.7 %** below L 35, the brightest actor mass in any frame I captured, exactly the sentence round 9 wrote about the warden with a different name in it. **Two of the seven grown creatures got scale without drawing**: ASH_HOUND's interior edge density is **55.2 %** and BOG_TOAD's **58.9 %** against the six heroes' 85.8 %, their largest single-colour contiguous blob is **21.5 %** and **21.1 %** of the sprite against the heroes' 5.8 %, and BOG_TOAD carries **11 colours in 1681 cells** (6.5 per 1000, the cast's lowest); beside `critic-r9/ASH_HOUND-x12.png` the round-9 hound's eye and horn were findable at 28 cells and the round-10 hound's are buried in a flank that grew faster than its head. **And artist B's twenty-four are a grade below the nineteen.** They pass all six criteria on the sheet — 24/24, one 8-connected component in every idle/attack/cast/hurt/dead bake, palette overlap peaking at 22.0 %, nearest silhouette 77.5, mirror max 81.7, pack chroma 11.97 — but the numbers pass where the read does not: on `late-x2-sil.png` eight of the thirteen humanoids are one block-torso-and-two-posts outline at ×2, on the grey plate their torsos are horizontal light/dark bands rather than modelled form, **six of them sink the head 3 cells on hurt 0** (RUIN_SENTINEL, FORGE_GOLEM, FURNACE_KNIGHT, DROWNED_SENTINEL, SPIRE_WARDEN, THUNDER_COLOSSUS — crownDy +3, head-centroid +2.5 to +3.0, the exact defect round 10 just fixed on the nineteen), **eight land attack 2 outside the nineteen's settle band** (WIND_EEL 72.6, STORM_DRAKE 51.3, RUIN_RAPTOR 46.6, WIND_SPRITE 42.3, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 41.2, LEVIATHAN_SPAWN 41.0, CINDER_WOLF 40.6, LIGHTNING_HAWK 39.3), and their deaths are templated — median pairwise dead IoU **49.2** against the nineteen's 36.7, with **FORGE_GOLEM↔GALE_MONK 86.4**, higher than any pair this review has ever flagged. Six of the twenty-four do not resolve into their brief at ×2: EMBER_ELEMENTAL is FROST_WISP's round-9 iris re-authored (a dark core centred in a bright ring), VAULT_JELLY is a balloon with a face, CINDER_WOLF reads as a donkey, RUIN_RAPTOR as a goat, LEVIATHAN_SPAWN as a seal, and B's own two admissions hold — FURNACE_KNIGHT's hammer is a plank across the chest and SKYFALLEN_KING's stone sword a strap down the plate, to which I add SPIRE_SERAPH's lance, SUNKEN_KING's trident and RUIN_SENTINEL's ward slab.

**Global, round 11:**

1. **DUST_WRAITH's shroud, in the frame.** Its lit left third still renders L 75–85 and its torso reads 55.9–59.1 in every crypt frame, the highest standard enemy. Take 8–10 L off `ASH_HIDE`'s lit step (round 10 moved mid −10 and lit −8; that bought >L 75 19.9 → 9.3 on the sheet and nothing in the scene) and put a DEEP (L < 20) band under the cowl lip and down the shroud's right join. Its 0–15 band is 12.5 %; at this rig only mass authored below L 20 lands below L 35 in the frame. Check on its own pixels at ENEMY_FEET[0] (230,380) and [1] (330,448): torso rows 18–40 p50 ≤ 52.
2. **ASH_HOUND's flank, in the frame and on the sheet.** Rows 0–17 read **70.1 / 2.7 %** below L 35 in scene; on the sheet one colour is 33.0 % of 1250 cells and its largest run 269 cells. Break the flank into three planes — a lit withers band, a mid barrel, a PLANE-step belly third with a one-cell shadow seam down each join — and put a DEEP band along the topline behind the skull so the head wins the local contrast. Target: upper band ≤ 60 p50 with ≥ 8 % below L 35, and a largest single-colour blob under 12 % of the sprite.
3. **BOG_TOAD, the emptiest sprite in the cast.** 11 colours over 1681 cells (6.5 per 1000; the heroes run 22–39), edge density 58.9 %, one contiguous colour 355 cells. The seven warts are dots on a plane. Give the barrel a three-band falloff from the spine to the belly, a two-row DEEP band under the jaw line, and a 1-cell shade-1 crease from the eye ridge to the hip — the same treatment SILT_CRAB's dome got in round 8, which is why the crab reads and the toad does not.
4. **The late humanoids' value is banded, not modelled.** On `late-x2-grey.png` RUIN_SENTINEL, FORGE_GOLEM, FURNACE_KNIGHT, DROWNED_SENTINEL, TIDE_ORACLE, SPIRE_WARDEN, THUNDER_COLOSSUS, SMITH_PRIEST, SKYFALLEN_KING, FORGE_SAINT, SUNKEN_KING and SPIRE_SERAPH are stacks of full-width horizontal light/dark rows. Replace the banding on each with a lit side and a shadow side: run the PLANE step down the far third of the torso and skirt with a 1-cell shade-1 seam at the join, keep the key on the near shoulder and the crown, and cut every horizontal band that crosses the whole width to two-thirds of it. `GALE` in the same plate is the model.
5. **The nine legged late humanoids need a silhouette apiece.** They clear 78 pairwise (max RUIN_SENTINEL↔DROWNED_SENTINEL 77.5) and still read as one family at ×2. One structural break each, not a hat: RUIN_SENTINEL a slab skirt reaching mid-shin with no legs showing; FORGE_GOLEM one arm twice the other's mass and no neck; DROWNED_SENTINEL a scale skirt flared 4 cells past the shoulders; SPIRE_WARDEN the storm cape breaking the right edge to the knee; THUNDER_COLOSSUS the conductor rods clearing the head by 6 cells; GALE_MONK the low stance widened 4 cells at the feet.
6. **Five weapons that do not read as weapons at ×2.** FURNACE_KNIGHT's hammer head must clear the shoulder silhouette by ≥ 4 cells and carry its own two-tone face; SKYFALLEN_KING's greatsword needs a 3-cell crossguard and 2 cells of air between blade and plate; SPIRE_SERAPH's lance a leaf head; SUNKEN_KING's trident three tines at 3 cells; RUIN_SENTINEL's ward slab must leave the shoulder line. Each is held (one component everywhere) — the problem is that none is separable from the torso at battle scale.
7. **EMBER_ELEMENTAL is round 9's iris.** A 4-cell dark core centred inside a closed bright ring reads as an eye. Offset the clinker core low-left, break the ring at two points so the nine embers read as separate, and carry the core's tone out through the break — `FROST_WISP` (rows 9–12, cols 3–6, mantle running out of the shard's foot) is the fix already in the repo.
8. **VAULT_JELLY reads as a face.** Two lit dots and a dark horizontal band across a dome are eyes and a mouth. Move the bioluminescent ring to the bell's inner rim as an arc, not a bar, and drop the two hotspots to one, off-centre.

**Per character:**
- **DUST_WRAITH** — item 1; torso 55.9–59.1 in every crypt frame, the highest standard enemy in the game.
- **ASH_HOUND** — items 2; upper band 70.1 / 2.7 % in scene, edge density 55.2 %, and its head is not findable at ×2 on `all-x2.png`.
- **BOG_TOAD** — item 3; 6.5 colours per 1000 cells, the cast's lowest.
- **CINDER_IMP** — the belly plate's three-bar ribcage reads as an "=" emblem at ×3 in `s20-enemies-x3.png`, and the horns are 1-cell antennae; thicken them to 2 cells and curve the ribs down-and-out. Mean contrast 3.17, second lowest.
- **CRYPT_WARDEN** — the value work is right and the frame proves it, but the head is now five full-width horizontal bars (brim, brow band, wrap, wrap, jaw) and at ×3 it reads as a stack, not a helm. Cut two of the bands to two-thirds width. Sub-3:1 44.9 % is one cell from the cap.
- **PYRE_KNIGHT** — 61 cells / 16.9 % of the frame, five cells over the 50–56 elite band and taller than every hero; the only actor outside its own band among the nineteen.
- **RUIN_RAPTOR** — reads as a goat: the folded wings are one lump above the shoulder, not two peaks, and the neck is horizontal. Pitch the neck up, split the wing mass with a 1-cell dark gap. Settle 46.6.
- **CINDER_WOLF** — reads as a donkey: long blunt muzzle, tall upright ears. Shorten the muzzle 3 cells, drop the ears back along the skull, and give the jaw a visible break. Idle-2 change 11.5 %, the cast's lowest.
- **LEVIATHAN_SPAWN** — the three stacked loops read as one lump: edge density 45.0 % and a 32.1 % single-colour blob, both the worst in the cast. Two 1-cell dark gaps between the loops.
- **VAULT_JELLY** — item 8; sub-3:1 44.6 %.
- **EMBER_ELEMENTAL** — item 7; sub-3:1 44.0 %.
- **FURNACE_KNIGHT, SKYFALLEN_KING, SPIRE_SERAPH, SUNKEN_KING, RUIN_SENTINEL** — item 6; and RUIN_SENTINEL, FORGE_GOLEM, FURNACE_KNIGHT, DROWNED_SENTINEL, SPIRE_WARDEN, THUNDER_COLOSSUS all owe the head-up recoil (Animation).
- **SPIRE_WARDEN** — sub-3:1 44.6 %, >L 75 8.6 %, mirror IoU 81.1: three thin margins at once, the way CRYPT_WARDEN was last round.
- **FORGE_GOLEM ↔ GALE_MONK** — dead-0 IoU 86.4; break one of the two collapses (fold direction, or the head's side of the mass).
- **DROWNED_CLOUD** — 39 cells / 10.8 % and **WIND_SPRITE** 38 / 10.6 %, both under the 40-cell standard floor; both also move less than a fifth of their idle change in the top third (25.5 % and 20.1 %).
- **TIDE, EMBER, GALE, SABLE, LUMEN, BASALT, HOLLOW_KING, PALE_SAINT, MARSH_HAG, DROWNED_KNIGHT, SILT_CRAB, FROST_WISP, FEN_FIRE, STORM_DRAKE, STEAM_WRAITH, SMITH_PRIEST, WIND_EEL, TIDE_ORACLE, LIGHTNING_HAWK** — nothing owed.

**Animation:** The nineteen's row is the best it has been. The chin lift is real and unambiguous — measured as the topmost painted row of hurt 0 against idle 0 at the same feet anchor, 16 of 19 rise a cell and none sinks (head-mass centroid −0.2 to −2.4 cells, LUMEN −2.4 the largest), where round 9 measured the head going sideways and down on fifteen of them; the magnitude is about half the −0.7 to −4.9 A claims on its own convention, but the direction is right on every one. HURT_MIX 0.45 does what it was asked to do: in `cap20/battle-hit-1b.png` the flashed CRYPT_WARDEN keeps a 50.4 L spread against 51.7 calm (97 %) and 413 distinct colours against 279, and in `battle-hit-2b.png` EMBER keeps 44.0 against 49.0 (90 %) with 560 colours — the recoil pose survives the flash on both. The settles landed as claimed (CINDER_IMP 34.9, ASH_HOUND 32.5, SILT_CRAB 37.4, FEN_FIRE 23.2, FROST_WISP 20.8; band 20.8–38.5) and every death on all 43 is a real collapse at 25–57 % of idle height with the mass topping 17–61 cells below the idle top and no rotated face anywhere. The twenty-four are where the row now fails. Six of B's thirteen humanoids drive the head *down* on the hit — RUIN_SENTINEL, FORGE_GOLEM, FURNACE_KNIGHT, DROWNED_SENTINEL, SPIRE_WARDEN and THUNDER_COLOSSUS all read crownDy **+3** and head-centroid **+2.5 to +3.0**, plus VAULT_JELLY +4 — which is round 9's finding restated on a new pack, and B's own note explains why (the `arms_*_hurt` grids it authored are unreachable because `RECOIL_ARMS` is a module const, so the recoil is a sheared body and nothing lifts the skull). Eight of the twenty-four land attack 2 outside the humanoid band (WIND_EEL 72.6 down to LIGHTNING_HAWK 39.3), four move less than 30 % of their idle change in the top third (WIND_SPRITE 20.1, DROWNED_CLOUD 25.5, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 25.7, VAULT_JELLY 28.7 — round 7's flagged range), CINDER_WOLF's idle 2 differs from idle 0 by 11.5 % and SPIRE_SERAPH's by 18.2 %, both under the nineteen's floor, and the late deaths are a template: median pairwise IoU 49.2 against the nineteen's 36.7, with FORGE_GOLEM↔GALE_MONK 86.4, DROWNED_CLOUD↔STEAM_WRAITH 80.7, DROWNED_CLOUD↔VAULT_JELLY 78.6, WIND_SPRITE↔STEAM_WRAITH 77.6, SKYFALLEN_KING↔FORGE_SAINT 77.3 and FORGE_SAINT↔SUNKEN_KING 77.2 all above where a pair has ever been flagged. No late prop lies detached in any dead frame, though SPIRE_WARDEN's caged lamp and FURNACE_KNIGHT's hammer are both legible across the corpse at ×2, so the round-9 bridged-but-reads-as-dropped convention holds.

**Ship criteria check:** (all measured on my own bakes off a clean worktree of HEAD 67440ab through the real `bakePose`, 43 actors, 645 pose bakes; the working tree has since moved to 7ad27ef)

1. **PASS.** Every actor spans p2 6.1–14.2 to p98 85.9–96.4; below-L 35 **26.0** (SPIRE_SERAPH) – **44.9** (CRYPT_WARDEN); interior-only with a strict 8-neighbour erosion **21.9** (WIND_EEL) – 52.6 (DROWNED_CLOUD), all ≥ 20 with WIND_EEL, SILT_CRAB 22.3 and SPIRE_SERAPH 23.4 the thin ones; above-L 75 **8.0** (CRYPT_WARDEN) – 36.9 (EMBER_ELEMENTAL), all ≥ 8 with DROWNED_SENTINEL 8.4 and SPIRE_WARDEN 8.6 next; top quarter above bottom on all 43, **+8.8** (DROWNED_SENTINEL) to +42.9 (FEN_FIRE). Both grey plates read as lit form on the nineteen; on the twenty-four the value is banded horizontally rather than modelled (Global item 4) — the criterion is met, the read is not. The authored dark plane, measured as the largest contiguous L* 28–38 blob, is ≥ 40 cells on all twelve original humanoids (EMBER 60 to PALE_SAINT 562) but reads 0–35 on five of the thirteen late ones (FORGE_GOLEM 0, RUIN_SENTINEL 18, SKYFALLEN_KING 27, TIDE_ORACLE 27, FORGE_SAINT 35) — that is a convention artefact, not a missing plane: their PLANE step lands in the L 25–30 bin, and widened to 24–42 every one of the 25 humanoids clears 40 (lowest GALE_MONK 68), which is where B's own 48–359 figures come from.
2. **PASS.** Of 645 bakes over all five pose rows on all 43, **636 are a single 8-connected component**. Every idle, attack, cast and hurt bake on every actor is one component; the nine exceptions are all in dead and all licensed — GALE's dropped dagger (45 cells, all three frames), FROST_WISP's 12/12/6-piece shatter, FEN_FIRE's 6/6/2 embers. DROWNED_KNIGHT's shield still bridges to one component and still reads as dropped. Caveat for the record, not a fail: on B's humanoids the haft is drawn as a bar over the plate and the fist crossing it is only findable at ×16 (verified on DROWNED_SENTINEL's pike), where the nineteen carry a legible grip at ×8.
3. **PASS.** Chin cast shadow or a dark neck band under the helm on all 25 humanoids, verified at ×8 on `grip-a-x8.png` / `grip-b-x8.png` for the thirteen late ones (clearest on SMITH_PRIEST, GALE_MONK, TIDE_ORACLE, DROWNED_SENTINEL, SUNKEN_KING) and at ×4 on `heroes-x4.png` for the six heroes. Under-arm seams throughout as 1-cell seams inside a solid silhouette. The late faces are thinner than the nineteen's — a skin oval, two eye clusters and a chin band, no nose break — but they are present.
4. **PASS.** Humanoid mirror IoU under 85 on all 25: max **81.7** (THUNDER_COLOSSUS), 81.1 (SPIRE_WARDEN), 81.0 (FORGE_SAINT), 80.2 (TIDE_ORACLE), 80.0 (EMBER_ELEMENTAL), 79.9 (SKYFALLEN_KING), 79.4 (HOLLOW_KING), 79.3 (LUMEN). Nearest silhouette over all 43 pairs on the sheet's own feet-aligned, centred convention: **RUIN_SENTINEL↔DROWNED_SENTINEL 77.5**, RUIN_SENTINEL↔FORGE_GOLEM 76.9, TIDE↔LUMEN 76.7, FURNACE_KNIGHT↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS 76.6, TIDE↔SABLE 75.2, CRYPT_WARDEN↔SPIRE_WARDEN 75.2 — **none at or above 78**, reproducing B's number exactly. The metric passes; the read of the nine legged late humanoids as one family does not (Global item 5).
5. **PASS.** Death on all 43 at **25 %** (SABLE) – **57 %** (PYRE_KNIGHT) of idle height, the top of the dead mass 17–61 cells below the idle top — more than a head height on every one — no rotated faces anywhere, props legible on the ground. Attack 1 differs from idle 0 by 62.1–97.5 % and hurt 1 by 65.7–98.9 % in absolute position; hurt 0's absolute silhouette IoU is 29.0 (FROST_WISP) – 70.5 (TIDE), so no actor's hurt 0 is the idle silhouette. Median pairwise dead IoU 43.0 over 43 (nineteen 36.7, late 24 **49.2**), with FORGE_GOLEM↔GALE_MONK **86.4** the outlier.
6. **PASS.** Mean contrast **3.10** (CRYPT_WARDEN) to **5.92** (FEN_FIRE), all ≥ 3:1; below-3:1 share **26.0 %** (SPIRE_SERAPH) to **44.9 %** (CRYPT_WARDEN), all under 45 — CRYPT_WARDEN 44.9, SPIRE_WARDEN and VAULT_JELLY 44.6, BOG_TOAD 44.4, TIDE 44.3, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 44.0 are six actors inside two points of the cap; the licensed light sources cap at FROST_WISP 5.28, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 5.17 and FEN_FIRE 5.92, all under 6:1. Cast mean CIELAB chroma **12.61** over 43 (nineteen **13.41**, late twenty-four **11.97**) against my own re-measure of `octopath-1`'s sprite band at **7.03 / p90 13.5 / p98 19.2**, reproducing the review's 7.0 / 13.6 / 19.3; highest garment GALE 15.44, BOG_TOAD 15.27, SMITH_PRIEST 14.99, with FEN_FIRE 40.54 and EMBER_ELEMENTAL 21.93 licensed. Palette overlap over the top ten colours peaks at **22.0 %** (DUST_WRAITH↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS), then PYRE_KNIGHT↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS 19.8 and BASALT↔PYRE_KNIGHT 16.1 — **every pair under 25**; ASH_HOUND↔DUST_WRAITH and BASALT↔CRYPT_WARDEN stay 0.0.

**Do my numbers reproduce `tools/out/metrics.md` (43 rows)?** Yes, with two named exceptions. Against `window.__lineup` at HEAD every column matches within 0.6 on all 43 rows except `interior <L35`, which differs by up to 2.2 points because I erode with 8 neighbours and the table with 4 — the same divergence rounds 6–9 recorded, and every actor clears 20 either way. Against the **file on disk** (written 01:19, before the last art commit) three rows are stale and no longer describe HEAD: RUIN_RAPTOR `>L75` 9.9 → **15.0**, GALE_MONK 917 px / 15.4 % → **906 / 14.5 %**, and LIGHTNING_HAWK `<L35` 30.3 → **39.0** with mean contrast 3.97 → **3.28** and sub-3:1 30.3 → **39.0**. All three still PASS; the file just needs `capture.mjs sheets` re-run. Everything else — pixel counts, boxes, frame %, L* spans, above-L75, contrast, mirror IoU, nearest silhouette — is honest.

**In-scene check:** All frames are `node tools/capture.mjs battle seed=N` run from a clean detached worktree of HEAD against its own Vite on :5194, plus my own driver over the same opening for the seed sweep; biome EMBER CRYPT in every one. Seeds captured with `capture.mjs`: **1, 4, 12, 16, 20**; seeds driven and measured: **1–25** (24 usable; seed 2 discarded because EMBER is mid-attack under a white slash VFX and reads 65.8 / 0.1 %, against 42.8–44.9 / 14.0–14.5 % on the other 23). Parties: seed 20 EMBER/BASALT/LUMEN, seed 1 EMBER/LUMEN/GALE, seed 12 EMBER/GALE/BASALT, seed 4 EMBER/TIDE/BASALT, seed 9 EMBER/SABLE/BASALT, seed 16 EMBER/TIDE (two-hero, so TIDE is at **slot 1 only**), seed 3 EMBER/SABLE (SABLE **slot 1 only**), seed 2/11/18/19 EMBER/LUMEN (LUMEN slot 1). TIDE and SABLE never appeared at slot 2 in a 25-seed sweep, exactly as rounds 9 and 10 reported. **Boxes:** each actor on its OWN pixels — the zoom-1 `mode=sil` mask, 4-neighbour eroded, scaled ×2 and planted at the screen's own anchors (HERO_FEET (700,380)/(790,448)/(880,516); ENEMY_FEET (230,380)/(330,448)/(430,516); ENEMY_FEET_PAIR (206,400)/(452,504); BOSS_FEET (322,490)) — restricted to sprite rows 18–40 measured from the silhouette's top, with a rows 0–17 band reported separately for the head/back. **Heroes (torso rows 18–40, p50 / % below L 35):** EMBER slot 0 **44.0–44.9 / 14.0–14.3 %** ✓ (seeds 1, 4, 12, 16, 20); GALE slot 1 **45.8 / 34.7 %** ✓ (12), slot 2 **46.3 / 23.7 %** ✓ (1); TIDE slot 1 **46.8 / 13.6 %** ✓ (4, 16); BASALT slot 1 **47.8–51.6 / 20.5–21.7 %** ✓ (20), slot 2 **55.4–57.8 / 17.7–18.6 %** ✓ (4, 12); LUMEN slot 1 **48.3 / 17.8 %** ✓ (1), slot 2 **49.4–53.7 / 12.3–13.4 %** on the `capture.mjs` frames and 54.0 / **10.7 %** on my own — at the bar, not clear of it; SABLE slot 1 **60.1 / 17.8 %** ✓ (3, 9). Every hero clears 12 % on the standard frames; round 9's two failures (LUMEN 5.9 / 1.7 %, BASALT 7.0–7.4 %) are gone. **Enemies:** CRYPT_WARDEN slot 1 **57.3–58.2 / 13.6–16.2 %**, head band 48.4 / 35.8 %, whole 48.9–49.1 (round 9: 63.6 / 0.6 %, head 64.8 / 0.4 %) — fixed; DUST_WRAITH slots 0/1 **55.9–59.1 / 14.1–17.6 %**, head band 60.5, whole 56.4–57.1; CINDER_IMP **52.2–53.6 / 22.8–27.0 %**, head band 64.3–65.1; ASH_HOUND torso **33.2–41.2 / 42.7–52.1 %** but head/flank band **70.1 / 2.7 %**, whole 56.0–58.6; HOLLOW_KING **67.8 / 16.1 %**, whole 57.1 / 20.7 % (round 9: 71.0 / 1.9 %). Against "no enemy torso p50 above the heroes' p50 + 5": **20 of 24 seeds fail against the best hero and 22 of 24 against the party median**, offenders DUST_WRAITH 13 seeds, CINDER_IMP 9, CRYPT_WARDEN 7, HOLLOW_KING 2. **Ordering:** at 1× in `cap20/battle-1.png` and `s16-battle.png` the eye goes to the warm shaft at x 130–320, then to the enemy line, then to the party — but for the first time it is not the warden it lands on; it is ASH_HOUND's cream flank and CINDER_IMP's pink dome. In the ×3 crops beside `octopath-4`, the party now holds a full value range (EMBER's boots and vest, BASALT's tabard and shield boss, LUMEN's halo and hood all legible) where the reference's own heroes carry near-black hair and packs against a bleached ground; ours are lighter and the contact shadows softer, but the figures no longer break apart. **Ground contrast** (actor median vs ground median over strips 0.9× the sprite width, under the feet at +6..+13 and +30..+45 and one sprite-width left and right): EMBER 2.56–3.51 / 2.49–4.17 / 1.73–2.19 / 1.15–2.61, GALE 1.93–2.75 / 2.19–3.02, TIDE 2.09–2.16 / 2.70–2.73, BASALT 2.05–2.80 / 2.41–3.09, SABLE 2.42–2.56 / 2.85–2.90, LUMEN 2.52–3.73 / 3.46–4.58; CRYPT_WARDEN **1.92–1.95** / 2.34–2.40 (was 3.05), DUST_WRAITH 2.40–3.83, CINDER_IMP 2.89–3.48, ASH_HOUND 3.27–4.09, HOLLOW_KING 3.62. Round 9's "every enemy out-contrasts every hero" is broken — the warden is now below every hero — but ASH_HOUND and HOLLOW_KING still top the frame. **Scale:** the seven grown creatures land 40–46 cells / **11.1–12.8 %** of the frame, inside the 40–50 / 11–14 % standard band; CRYPT_WARDEN 52 / 14.4 % and MARSH_HAG 50 / 13.9 % as A left them; elites DROWNED_KNIGHT 56, STORM_DRAKE 52, FURNACE_KNIGHT 56, LEVIATHAN_SPAWN 52, THUNDER_COLOSSUS 51 all inside 50–56, but **PYRE_KNIGHT is 61 / 16.9 %**, five cells over the elite band and taller than any hero; bosses HOLLOW_KING 89, PALE_SAINT 93, SKYFALLEN_KING 75, SUNKEN_KING 72, FORGE_SAINT 71, SPIRE_SERAPH 65, all ≥ 60; among the late standards WIND_SPRITE 38 / 10.6 % and DROWNED_CLOUD 39 / 10.8 % fall under the floor and DROWNED_SENTINEL 52, TIDE_ORACLE 52, SPIRE_WARDEN 53, GALE_MONK 51 sit over it. The brief's density target is met; the reference's own ratio is not — in `octopath-4` the sand lizardmen measure 112–130 px against the party's 88–110 in the same 633-px frame, so the enemies there stand at or above hero height, where ours are 0.72–0.82× (40–46 cells against 56–60). **Acts 3–6 were judged on the sheet, not in a battle** — I did not reach an act-3 fight and did not try to, since B's own `playfull acts=3 seed=7` ran 774 s and wiped in act 2. Instead I shot each pack at ×4 over its own biome's floor colour (`?group=ruins&zoom=4&bg=57506f`, `forge&bg=5b4438`, `vault&bg=3e6d7c`, `spire&bg=5b6484`) and measured the four dioramas' actual lit floor at the feet anchors from `tools/backdrops.html`: SKY_RUINS L p10/p50/p90 **15.7 / 27.3 / 37.0** on the enemy side, ASHEN_FORGE 11.8 / **22.9** / 28.0, SUNKEN_VAULT 19.2 / **31.0** / 36.9, STORM_SPIRE 18.7 / **30.8** / 42.8 — so the real ground under these packs sits 5–13 L below the flat swatch each was authored against (#57506f is L 35.9). Judged on the sheet over the flat floor swatch the packs read at only 1.9–3.0:1 mean contrast with 55–91 % of pixels under 3:1 (lowest LEVIATHAN_SPAWN 1.92, LIGHTNING_HAWK 1.91, VAULT_JELLY 1.97; only STEAM_WRAITH 2.97 and EMBER_ELEMENTAL 3.05 reach 3), but against the diorama's real L 23–31 floor they will separate — and B's hue plan is sound: cold blue iron on the amber forge, verdigris and mauve on the teal vault, warm sandstone on the violet ruins, brass on the blue-grey spire all hold at ×4 over their own floors. What does not hold on the sheet is form: on `late-x2-grey.png` the twelve late humanoids read as horizontal bands, on `late-x2-sil.png` nine of them read as one outline, and six of the twenty-four do not resolve into their brief at ×2. That judgement is on the sheet and the sheet only; no act 3–6 actor has been seen in a driven frame by anyone yet.

### Round 11 (2026-09-06): what artist A changed

**1. The hooks for artist B (done first, message-free).** `RECOIL_ARMS` is no longer a `Partial<Record<PartId, PartId>>` module const but a mutable `Record<string, PartId>` (`game/art/actors.ts:1248`) behind `export function registerRecoilArms(map: Record<string, PartId>)` (`game/art/actors.ts:1271`) — `lateRecipes()` calls it before it builds, and every pair it registers is found by `humanoid()`/`boss()` exactly as the nineteen's own are. Neither hook changed a pixel of the nineteen — the `metrics.md` rows were byte-stable across the hooks-only bake. `graft` does not exist in either file; there was nothing to export under that name.

**2. DUST_WRAITH in the frame — improved, target not reached.** `ASH_HIDE`'s lit step comes down nine more (`{mid:-10, lit:-17}`, `actors.ts:457`) and `wraithDetail` (`parts.ts:4135`) adds two bands at the accent's DEEP step, which nothing lifts: two rows under the cowl's lip across the chest, and a one-cell seam down the join where the lit third meets the plane. Over 13 driven crypt seeds at `ENEMY_FEET[0]`/`[1]`, torso rows 18–40 go **58.0–59.1 / 14.1–14.6 % below L 35 → 55.9–56.5 / 18.2–18.9 %**; head band 56.8–60.5 / 17.6 → **55.9–59.9 / 24.7 %**; whole figure 56.4–56.8 / 18.6 → **53.8–54.4 / 23.2–23.8 %**. The p50 ≤ 52 target is not reachable while criterion 6 holds, and the arithmetic says so: every step `legal()` touches is clamped to ≥ 3.2:1 against the stage, which is a fixed luminance and therefore a fixed floor of **L\* ≈ 51**; only steps 0, 1 and 6 sit below it and those are exactly the pixels criterion 6 caps at 45 %. The wraith's torso band is 34.9 % below L 35 on the sheet with a p50 of 51.2 — pinned on the floor — so a torso p50 of 47 (what 52 in-scene needs) would take roughly +15 points of sub-3:1 in that band, and the sprite is already at **43.1 %** against the 45 cap.

**3. ASH_HOUND's flank.** `houndFlank` (`parts.ts:3701`) is rewritten: the barrel is cut into three top to bottom — a short lit withers, a mid middle, and the lower third turned into the PLANE step with a one-cell shadow seam along its top edge and two deep seams down it; the two thirteen-cell lit runs that used to rule across the whole back are gone from `houndBase`; the key moved onto the **skull**; a five-cell DEEP band of the cloth's deep step runs along the topline **behind** the skull; the ears dropped to the shadow step; the brisket took the light the topline gave up; and two of the underside's four deep rows came back to shadow to pay for all of it. In scene, rows 0–17 go **69.4–70.2 p50 / 1.1–4.5 % below L 35 → 58.8–62.9 / 14.8–20.8 %** (seeds 1, 3, 5, 7, 8, 10, 11, 12, 16 at enemy slots 0/1/2): the ≥ 8 % half of the target is met everywhere by a wide margin, the ≤ 60 p50 half on 2 of 7 distinct slot-rows and within 2.9 of it on the rest. Whole figure 56.0–58.7 → **43.6–50.0**; torso 37.9–44.3 → 41.0–45.4; ground contrast at +6 px **3.27–4.09 → 2.24–2.80**, so it no longer out-contrasts every hero. On the sheet: interior edge density **55.2 → 64.6 %**, largest single-colour contiguous blob **21.5 → 11.8 %** (148 cells, target < 12), colours 16, and the eye and horn are findable at ×2 on `lineup-x2.png`.

**4. BOG_TOAD.** A three-band falloff from the lit spine (mid, then a full-width WET BAND in the cloth2 teal the belly is cut from), a two-row full-width DEEP band under the jaw line, a one-cell shade-1 crease from the eye ridge to the hip that **breaks** at both bands, a four-step underside falloff (shadow / shadow / teal / deep) replacing three identical deep rows, and a pale wet belly in cloth2 (`toadBands` `parts.ts:4565`, `toadDetail` :4525, `toadBase`). (First pass took the blob the wrong way — 449 cells — because the blob measure is 8-connected: a band that stops a cell short of a flank does not cut anything, and a crease in the same tone as the mass reconnects it. Both are noted in the file.)

The five named per-character items.** **CINDER_IMP**: the three level bars are now ribs that leave the sternum level and drop a row as they run outward, the lowest pair a cell shorter (`impBodyDetail` :3438), the horn tip is two cells not one (:3355), and a lit near hip fills the sprite's empty 55–75 band (0.2 → 0.4 %); mean contrast **3.17 → 3.26**. **CRYPT_WARDEN**: the brow lip and the jaw band are cut to two-thirds width (`bruteHeadRows` :3282), so the helm steps instead of stacking five full-width bars — visible as two notches on `lineup-x2-sil.png` — and below-3:1 comes off the cap at **44.9 → 44.3 %**. **PYRE_KNIGHT**: five rows off the crest (`PLUME` :3197) with the placement moved down the same five so the socket stays mounted (`PLUME_AT` `actors.ts:1237`) — **61 → 56 cells, 16.9 → 15.6 % of frame**, inside the elite band, silhouette otherwise untouched. **BASALT**: the two vertical creases per half are gone and a leather baldric falls diagonally from the near shoulder to the off hip (`basaltBaldric` :1050), and two amber eye clusters with a falloff row sit behind the visor slot (`basaltHelm` :1417); colours 17 → 19, below-3:1 39.0 → 38.1. **HOLLOW_KING**: the eyes were the DARK element's default glow, inherited because the recipe never named one — `glow: glowRamp(282, 48, 82)` (`actors.ts:1857`) puts them on the crypt's own violet, and `#c641bc` is gone from the game.

**6. The hit flash is an edge flash.** `flashTint` takes a mix, and `flashEdges` (`actors.ts:704`, weights at :678) tints by distance from the silhouette: 0.85 on the outer edge cell (the part's own keyline, plus every interior hole's lip), 0.28 one cell behind it, **0.18** in the interior. Measured on the sprite alone (pose-sheet hurt 0 against idle 0, no VFX, no light rig, no bloom): p50 **72.5 → 62.3** (CINDER_IMP), **72.0 → 64.8** (EMBER), **72.4 → 60.3** (CRYPT_WARDEN); below L 35 **0.0 → 5.4 / 8.6 / 9.6 %**; distinct colours in the flash frame **18 → 36 / 36 → 78 / 21 → 47**. In `battle seed=16` on the target's own masked pixels, before/after on the same frame indices: the flash frames go **85.0 → 69.4 %** and **90.7 → 83.6 %** above L 75 (`battle-hit-0c`, `battle-hit-1a`), while `battle-hit-1b`/`1c` are **59.4 → 59.4** and **48.7 → 49.0** — identical, because by then the sprite is off hurt 0 and everything above L 75 is the skill's own white core. The ≤ 30 % / ≤ 15 % targets are therefore not reachable from `actors.ts`: the residue is `game/art/vfx.ts`'s emitter and the bloom, which the full-frame critic's own VFX item 2 names alongside the sprite tint. Note the trade the rim makes: a 1-cell rim is ~17 % of a 43×44 silhouette, so the sprite's own above-L 75 share rises (CINDER_IMP 14.6 → 33.4, EMBER 35.8 → 30.3, CRYPT_WARDEN 23.6 → 26.5) while its interior comes back — which is what "light the EDGE and keep the interior's authored values" asks for.

### Round 11 (2026-09-06): what artist B changed

**1. Value modelled, not banded (Global 4).** Three passes now run over every one of the thirteen late humanoid bodies, in `parts-late.ts`: `sideModel` drops the far (right) third of each row into the material's own unlifted PLANE step following the silhouette, with a one-cell DARK seam at the join; `keyEdge` widens the one-pixel lit rim on the near shoulder into a two-cell key plane over the shoulder rows; `cutBands` lifts the first 28 % of every DEEP/DARK row back to the SHADOW step, so a band that crossed the whole width now stops short of the lit edge at one end and turns into the shadow side at the other. Six ramps that had pushed their plane step below the bin were re-tuned (`RUIN_STONE`, `COLD_IRON`, `VERDIGRIS`, `RAPTOR_PLUME/COVERT`, `HAWK_FEATHER`, `ORACLE_ROBE`). **Largest contiguous L\* 28–38 blob, the critic's own bins, round 10 → 11:** RUIN_SENTINEL 18→53, FORGE_GOLEM 0→87, SKYFALLEN_KING 27→119, TIDE_ORACLE 27→93, FORGE_SAINT 35→96, SUNKEN_KING 41→259, SMITH_PRIEST 51→66, FURNACE_KNIGHT 53, GALE_MONK 56→100, SPIRE_WARDEN 66→133, DROWNED_SENTINEL 83→90, THUNDER_COLOSSUS 132→140, SPIRE_SERAPH 75→195 — **all thirteen ≥ 40 on the strict bin** (five were under it). **Contrast, both conventions.** Over the flat swatch each pack was authored against: ruins 2.18 (84.7 % under 3:1), forge 2.51 (79.6), vault 2.05 (79.4), spire 2.09 (81.7). Over the biome's **measured lit floor L**: ruins 2.69 / 71.2 %, forge 3.17 / 68.2 %, vault 2.53 / 75.5 %, spire 2.56 / 79.7 % — **every pack mean ≥ 2.5:1**, the ≤ 45 % half is not met and cannot be (see Left undone).

Max over all 43 pairs is now TIDE↔LUMEN 76.7 — no late pair is the cast's worst any more.

Weapons that read (Global 6).** A new `outboard` pass leans a two-handed prop out across the body above the fist, because the real defect was occlusion: the weapon draws at z 3 and the head at z 4, so every head-end landed *behind* the helm. GREATHAMMER re-authored with the head hung entirely to the near side of the haft, a lit top course, a mid body and a `}}}}`-deep eye between a flat face and a drawn peen — **+6 cells past the torso at shoulder height** (torso x 16–47, reach 53). The six that did not resolve.** EMBER_ELEMENTAL: the clinker offset to `(cx−4, cy+4)`, the ring broken at two of its nine segments, the core's own tone carried out through the lower break in the bone's shadow step — no longer FROST_WISP's iris; sub-3:1 44.0 → 40.3, top-third 25.7 → 38.6. WIND_SPRITE **38 → 43 cells** (three crown streamers, one nine cells longer each frame) with top-third **20.1 → 30.1 %**; DROWNED_CLOUD **39 → 42 cells** with top-third **25.5 → 66.5 %**.

**5. Animation.** `registerRecoilArms` is wired from `lateRecipes()` with all eleven `arms_*_hurt` pairs, and the six named humanoids got `recoilLift: true` plus the `lift: 2` throat their headKits were missing. **crownDy on all thirteen late humanoids is now −1 or −2** (RUIN_SENTINEL, FORGE_GOLEM, FURNACE_KNIGHT, DROWNED_SENTINEL, SPIRE_WARDEN, THUNDER_COLOSSUS were +3; VAULT_JELLY +4 → −2), head-centroid −0.4 to −3.1. **Settles**, as its own authored grid per creature (`BeastKit.settle`) instead of a one-cell translation: WIND_EEL 72.6→25.0, STORM_DRAKE 51.3→25.5, RUIN_RAPTOR 46.6→37.2, WIND_SPRITE 42.3→38.8, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 41.2→38.1, LEVIATHAN_SPAWN 41.0→24.8, CINDER_WOLF 40.6→22.0, LIGHTNING_HAWK 39.3→35.3 — **all eight inside 21–39**. **Top-third idle share** ≥ 30 on all four named. **Deaths de-templated**: two new collapse shapes (BUCKLE, kneeling and tall with an empty left third; SPREAD, flat on the back with both arms past the hips), GALE_MONK moved off FORGE_GOLEM's PITCH, SUNKEN_KING off FORGE_SAINT's COIL, THUNDER_COLOSSUS off SIDE, and STEAM_WRAITH / DROWNED_CLOUD / VAULT_JELLY re-drawn (the wraith comes *undone upward*, the cloud rains out to one side, the jelly settles off-centre). **Max pairwise dead IoU over all 43 actors: 86.4 → 74.5**; late-only median 49.2 → 43.2; every one of the six flagged pairs is gone (FORGE_GOLEM↔GALE_MONK 86.4, DROWNED_CLOUD↔STEAM_WRAITH 80.7, DROWNED_CLOUD↔VAULT_JELLY 78.6, WIND_SPRITE↔STEAM_WRAITH 77.6, SKYFALLEN_KING↔FORGE_SAINT 77.3, FORGE_SAINT↔SUNKEN_KING 77.2).

**6. The duplicated authoring layer is retired.** 29 helpers now import from `./parts` (`part`, `autoShade`, `sym/hb/hbf/bands/rep/shiftX/stanceRow/pair/padRow/stamp/stampRows`, `castPlane`, `castChinShadow`, `shiftRows`, `fit`, `mirrorRows`, `firstSolid`, `shoulderDrop`, `turn`, `faceBlock`, `crown`, `armsPart`, `armsRecoil`, `fallenHeadRows`, `scaleRows`, `hemShift`, `scallopHem`), plus the `Band`/`FaceOpts`/`ArmOpts`/`FistOpts` types. Verified by hashing **all 645 pose bakes of all 43 actors** before and after the swap: the only two hashes that moved were ASH_HOUND and BOG_TOAD, which are artist A's in-flight work — **every bake of the twenty-four is byte-identical across the swap**. Three helpers stay local because they genuinely differ from parts.ts's and swapping them would change the grids: `selfShadowLate` (chin stamp at `lh−5`, not a fixed 7), `fingersLate` (a seven-row fist with the grip at y 3) and `legsTurned` (a different signature).

**7. Every criterion still passes on all 24.** `tools/out/metrics.md` is **43/43 PASS**, no FAIL rows. One 8-connected component in **every** idle, attack, cast, hurt *and* dead bake of all 24 (the recoil-arms swap briefly cost five humanoids their hurt-frame connectivity; the `lift: 2` throat closed it). Palette overlap max **20.1 %** (DUST_WRAITH↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS), every pair under 25. Nearest silhouette over all 43: **76.7** (TIDE↔LUMEN), late max 76.3 — none at 78. Humanoid mirror max 81.0 (FORGE_SAINT), all under 85. Late-pack mean CIELAB chroma **12.32** (cast 12.77). Death height 26–54 % of idle. The hero-body-in-another-palette note is answered by item 2: SMITH_PRIEST keeps its stoop, DROWNED_SENTINEL is now a narrow cuirass over a flared bell, GALE_MONK a triangle on a wide stance, SPIRE_WARDEN a caped diagonal — their four pairwise IoUs against the six heroes now peak at 72.8.

**Left undone**
- **The ≤ 45 % under 3:1 over the biome floors is not reachable at these floors.** Over an L\* 31 ground only a pixel above L\* 61.6 clears 3:1 at all (the dark side is arithmetically impossible), so 55 % of a sprite would have to sit above L\* 62 — which breaks criterion 1's ≥ 20 % below L\* 35. The twenty-four sit at 68.2–79.7 % under 3:1 over their own lit floors; **the nineteen measured on the same floors sit at 55.9–71.1 %** (the six heroes 52.9–71.0), so the gap is now 1–9 points, not a tier. Means are met on all four packs.
- **No act 3–6 actor has been seen in a driven battle frame.** Unchanged from round 10; everything here is judged on sheets over each biome's own floor colour.
- **RUIN_RAPTOR still reads as a long-necked grazer at ×4** — the neck is pitched and the wings split, but the head is small against the body and the beak is short.
- **LEVIATHAN_SPAWN's loops are separated in value, not in outline**: the gaps are interior, so on `late-x2-sil.png` it is still one lump.
- **Thin margins**: SKYFALLEN_KING sub-3:1 44.6 and SPIRE_WARDEN 43.7 (cap 45); FORGE_GOLEM >L 75 9.7 and SMITH_PRIEST 10.2 (bar 8); FORGE_SAINT mirror 81.0 and TIDE_ORACLE 80.2 (cap 85, unchanged — neither was on the list). EMBER_ELEMENTAL's chroma is **27.4**, up from 21.9: shrinking the clinker to hold sub-3:1 under the cap raised the glow's share. That is a licensed-light-source number like FEN_FIRE's 40.5, but it is the one number that moved the wrong way.
- **Five creatures still raise the crown a cell on hurt 0** (RUIN_RAPTOR, WIND_SPRITE, STEAM_WRAITH, LIGHTNING_HAWK, EMBER_ELEMENTAL read crownDy +1). `creature()`'s hurt keyframes carry `dy: 1` and live in `actors.ts`, which I do not own; only VAULT_JELLY was named and it is fixed, by authoring its hurt grid nine rows higher.
- **`tools/lineup.ts` needed no change** — the `ruins/forge/vault/spire/late` groups and the thirteen late humanoids in `HUMANOIDS` were already in place from the late-enemy round, and every new number above comes through them.

**Gates** (verbatim, repo root): `npm run check` → `tsc --noEmit` clean; `npm run build` → ✓ 50 modules transformed, `dist/assets/index-BoiJ7cAY.js 475.03 kB │ gzip: 158.56 kB`, ✓ built in 2.23s; `npm run smoke` → `SMOKE OK: canvas live in DOM, zero uncaught errors`.

**Frames viewed** — `tools/ref/octopath-1-job-lineup.jpg` and `octopath-4-desert-battle.jpg` first; then, at every step, my own worktree bakes kept in `scratchpad/art-r11b/sheets/`: `late-x2.png`, `late-x2-grey.png` and `late-x2-sil.png` (three times each — after the value pass, after the silhouette and weapon work, and final), `late-ruins-x4-floor.png`, `late-forge-x4-floor.png`, `late-vault-x4-floor.png`, `late-spire-x4-floor.png` (each shot over its biome's own ground hex), `weapons-x6.png` (FURNACE_KNIGHT / SKYFALLEN_KING / SUNKEN_KING / SPIRE_SERAPH / RUIN_SENTINEL, twice), and per-cell ASCII dumps of FURNACE_KNIGHT and SKYFALLEN_KING at 1× to find why the hammer head and the greatsword were disappearing behind the helm.

### Round 11 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 9/10 (from 9/10)

Artist B's list landed almost in full and it is the largest single step the twenty-four have taken: the "banded, not modelled" charge is closed on a measurement, not an opinion — over the torso band (rows 30–72 % of height) the thirteen late humanoids now carry **85.3 %** of their value variance WITHIN rows against the six heroes' 83.4 %, and their full-width flat rows are **6.8 %** against the heroes' 7.5 %, so they are no longer more banded than the cast they were being compared to; the authored dark plane (largest contiguous L\* 28–38 blob, strict 8-connected bin) is **≥ 40 on all twenty-five humanoids** where five were under it (RUIN_SENTINEL 18 → 53, FORGE_GOLEM 0 → 87, SKYFALLEN_KING 27 → 119, TIDE_ORACLE 27 → 93, FORGE_SAINT 35 → 96); the nine legged late humanoids drop to **max 75.1 / median 63.6** pairwise (was 77.5 / 70.5); every one of the six head-down recoils is now a head-up one (**crownDy −1 on all thirteen**, from +3/+4); all eight loose settles land in the band; and the dead-template is broken — **max pairwise dead IoU over all 43 is 74.5**, with all six flagged pairs gone and FORGE_GOLEM↔GALE_MONK at 54.7. Artist A's half is smaller but real: the hooks B built on, ASH_HOUND's flank (edge density 55.2 → **64.6 %**, largest contiguous single-colour blob 21.5 → **11.8 %**, head band in scene **70.1 / 2.7 % → 59.3–61.4 / 15.1–16.9 %**, and off the enemy-brightness list on all 25 seeds), BOG_TOAD (11 → **14** colours, blob 21.1 → **10.2 %**), CINDER_IMP's ribs, CRYPT_WARDEN's stepped helm, PYRE_KNIGHT back inside the elite band at **56 cells / 15.6 %**, BASALT's baldric and eyes, HOLLOW_KING's magenta gone from the game, and an edge flash that genuinely keeps the pose — the flashed CINDER_IMP holds its brows, chin shadow, wings and legs at ×5. `metrics.md` is 43/43 and every sheet ruler I re-derived passes. It is not SHIP for three reasons, and the first is the one that has moved. **In the scene the floor now under the actors, the party fails its own criterion at the near seat**: over 25 driven crypt seeds, GALE at slot 2 reads **51.4 / 8.8 %** below L 35, BASALT at slot 2 **54.1–54.5 / 10.8–11.0 %**, LUMEN at slot 2 **56.3 / 9.2 %** (round 10 measured the same three seats at 23.7 %, 17.7–18.6 % and 12.3–13.4 %), SABLE at slot 1 is **60.3** p50, and in the one FROST MARSH frame anyone has driven, BASALT reads **60.5 / 8.0 %** and GALE **56.0 / 8.5 %** while PALE_SAINT stands at **71.7 / 7.2 %** — brighter than round 9's HOLLOW_KING, the number this review called a defect. **Actor/ground separation has collapsed with it**: on **44 of 111 actor-seats** the actor's median is under 2:1 against the ground at BOTH strips, and that is every seat GALE (6/6), BASALT (10/10), TIDE (6/6), SABLE (2/2) and CRYPT_WARDEN (8/8) appear in. Neither is the artists' edit — the sprites did not move — but both fail criteria written on the sprites, and they are what the frame looks like now. **And the two grown creatures A rewrote hit their numbers without gaining a drawing**: at ×6 over the crypt floor ASH_HOUND's three planes are three flat rectangles with straight horizontal seams under a hard cream stripe, BOG_TOAD's three bands are three full-width stripes on a box with post legs, and both lost their idle — ASH_HOUND's frame-to-frame change fell **19.5 → 11.8 %** and BOG_TOAD sits at 13.5 %, the two lowest in the cast and the only two under the 17 floor. Beside `octopath-4` at a glance the frame is much closer than it was — the ground now has value (crypt p50 L 23.9 against the full-frame critic's 15.8, marsh 35.4) — but the reference's own law still runs the other way: its front lizardman's sprite pixels sit at p50 **37.4 with 44 % below L 35 on a ground of p50 83.6**, ours sit 8–20 L *above* their ground.

## Global

1. **The near hero seat, in the crypt and in the marsh** (`game/art/actors.ts` ramps, or the light rig — see Rule questions). HERO_FEET[2] (880, 516) is now the brightest seat on the stage: GALE 51.4 / **8.8 %**, BASALT 54.1–54.5 / **10.8–11.0 %**, LUMEN 56.3 / **9.2 %** against the ≥ 12 % / ≤ 55 p50 bar, and all three passed it in round 10 unchanged. In FROST MARSH the same failure moves to slot 1: BASALT 60.5 / **8.0 %**, GALE 56.0 / **8.5 %**. Target: ≥ 12 % below L 35 with p50 ≤ 55 at every seat in every biome. Owner: whichever moves — the ramps' PLANE step in `parts.ts`, or `GAIN_LIFT` / the pool weights in `engine/light.ts`.
2. **Actor/ground contrast, ≥ 2:1 per seat.** 44 of 111 seats fail at both strips (69 at one). GALE 6/6, BASALT 10/10, TIDE 6/6, SABLE 2/2, CRYPT_WARDEN 8/8, LUMEN 5/6, ASH_HOUND 7/14; only EMBER (0/25), CINDER_IMP (0/17), DUST_WRAITH (0/15) and HOLLOW_KING (0/2) never fail. Worst single seats GALE@1 **1.20:1**, BASALT@1 1.29, CRYPT_WARDEN@1 1.25, TIDE@1 1.34. The crypt's lit floor at the feet anchors is now L p10/p50/p90 **23.3 / 36.6 / 43.3** enemy side and 15.0 / 35.8 / 39.2 hero side; every garment midtone that used to clear the stage navy sits inside that band.
3. **The enemy plane still out-values the party.** Over 25 seeds an enemy torso is above the party median + 5 on **23 of 25** (20 of 25 above the best hero + 5) — round 10 was 22 of 24. Offenders by seed count: **CINDER_IMP 15** (53.1–54.0, head band 63.9–66.0, the brightest actor mass in any crypt frame), **DUST_WRAITH 14** (55.9–57.2), **CRYPT_WARDEN 8** (56.7–57.2), **HOLLOW_KING 2** (68.8). ASH_HOUND is off the list on all 25 — A's claim holds. Add **PALE_SAINT 71.7 / 7.2 %** in the marsh, now the highest-value actor in the game.
4. **ASH_HOUND and BOG_TOAD read as stacked rectangles** (`parts.ts` `houndFlank` :3701, `houndBase`; `toadBands` :4565, `toadDetail` :4525). The numeric targets are met — hound edge 64.6 %, blob 11.8 %, 16 colours; toad edge 72.6 %, blob 10.2 %, 14 colours — but at ×6 the hound's "three planes" are three straight-edged horizontal slabs with no ribcage, shoulder or haunch curve under a hard-edged cream topline, and the toad is a rectangular box with four posts and three full-width stripes. The hound also carries the cast's **highest single-colour share, 36.5 %** of 1250 cells (up from 33.0 — the blob was cut, the colour was not). Target: break each seam with a 2–3-cell step so no plane join is a straight run across the whole sprite, and take the hound's topline off one colour.
5. **Idle follow-through regressed on the two A rewrote.** Frame-to-frame idle change: **ASH_HOUND 19.5 → 11.8 %**, BOG_TOAD **13.5 %** — the only two under the 17 floor the review has used since round 6 (next lowest DROWNED_CLOUD 20.4, FROST_WISP 20.8, SILT_CRAB 21.2, SPIRE_SERAPH 21.6). The new flank/band grids are shared across the idle frames.
6. **The hit frame's peak is unmoved.** On the target's own masked pixels across 20 seeds where a hit landed, the peak share above L 75 is **47–87 % (median ≈ 62)** against the full-frame critic's 61 % — A's sprite-side edge flash is real on the sheet (hurt-0 p50 62.3 / 64.8 / 60.5 on CINDER_IMP / EMBER / CRYPT_WARDEN, colours 37 / 78 / 50) and the pose survives at ×5, but in the frame the VFX white core, the gain and the bloom put it back. What *is* fixed is the duration: **+360–520 ms the target is back to 5.6–14.0 %**, where round 2 measured 56 %. Owner: `game/art/vfx.ts` emitter core + the bloom gate, not `actors.ts`.
7. **SUNKEN_KING's trident does not read as a weapon** (`parts-late.ts`, the trident/`outboard` block). At ×6 it is two small pale ornaments flanking the head — a wand-like cross on the left and a two-prong stick on the right — not one three-tined weapon clearing the body. The other four of round 10's item 6 are done: FURNACE_KNIGHT's hammer head is a separate two-tone block past the shoulder, SKYFALLEN_KING's blade has a quillon block and air against the plate, RUIN_SENTINEL's ward slab is above the shoulder line, SPIRE_SERAPH's leaf head reads — though the seraph's lance is one pale spike among eight wing spikes and is lost at ×2.
8. **Chroma: four garments over 14.5.** Cast mean **12.77** (nineteen 13.35, late 12.32) is in band; licensed light sources FEN_FIRE 40.54 and EMBER_ELEMENTAL **27.37** (up from 21.9 — the one number that moved the wrong way this round, B's own admission). Garments above the reference's p98: GALE 15.44, DROWNED_SENTINEL 15.42, BOG_TOAD 14.93, SMITH_PRIEST 14.82.
9. **The flattest five, by interior edge density** (heroes 85.7 %): **LIGHTNING_HAWK 50.2**, STORM_DRAKE 54.1, STEAM_WRAITH 56.9, LEVIATHAN_SPAWN 59.9, SILT_CRAB 61.6 (then CINDER_WOLF 63.5, ASH_HOUND 64.6). **Largest single-colour contiguous blob** (heroes 5.8 %), excluding the two licensed glows (FROST_WISP 36.1, FEN_FIRE 20.8): **FORGE_SAINT 25.9**, STORM_DRAKE 24.6, LIGHTNING_HAWK 22.9, SILT_CRAB 20.6, SUNKEN_KING 20.5. LIGHTNING_HAWK and STORM_DRAKE are the two worst on both lists — flat planks for wings and one value per limb.

## Animation

The best row on the sheet, and the twenty-four have joined it. **Hurt 0 lifts the crown on all twenty-five humanoids** — crownDy **−1** on every one, head centroid −0.4 to −3.1 — where six of B's thirteen read +3 and VAULT_JELLY +4 last round; the five creatures B names (RUIN_RAPTOR, WIND_SPRITE, STEAM_WRAITH, LIGHTNING_HAWK, EMBER_ELEMENTAL) still read crownDy **+1** and ASH_HOUND, FEN_FIRE and STORM_DRAKE hold at 0, all eight in `creature()`'s hurt keyframes in `actors.ts`. **Settles** (attack 2 against idle 0, absolute) run **20.8 (FROST_WISP) – 38.8 (WIND_SPRITE)** across all 43 — the whole cast is inside 21–39, and B's eight reproduce to the decimal (WIND_EEL 25.0, STORM_DRAKE 25.5, RUIN_RAPTOR 37.2, WIND_SPRITE 38.8, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 38.1, LEVIATHAN_SPAWN 24.8, CINDER_WOLF 22.0, LIGHTNING_HAWK 35.3). **Idle change** clears 17 on 41 of 43; the two that do not are A's ASH_HOUND **11.8** (from 19.5) and BOG_TOAD **13.5**. **Top-third share** of the idle change is ≥ 30 on all 43, lowest WIND_SPRITE 30.1, VAULT_JELLY 31.9, FROST_WISP 34.4, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 38.6 — round 10's four named all bought. **Attack** travels on all 43 (attack 1 vs idle 0, absolute, 60.8–97.5 %) and reads as one where I opened the sheet — `poses-FURNACE_KNIGHT.png` swings the hammer head clear of the body on frame 1 and settles on frame 2, with the edge flash visible on hurt 0 as a lit rim over a preserved interior and the crown a cell up. **Deaths**: all 43 collapse, height **25 % (SABLE) – 63 % (PYRE_KNIGHT)** of idle, no rotated faces; **max pairwise dead IoU 74.5** (CRYPT_WARDEN↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS), median 39.5 over all 43 (nineteen 36.7, late 43.2), nothing at or above 78, and all six of round 10's flagged pairs broken — FORGE_GOLEM↔GALE_MONK 86.4 → **54.7**, DROWNED_CLOUD↔STEAM_WRAITH 80.7 → 23.2, DROWNED_CLOUD↔VAULT_JELLY 78.6 → 35.2, WIND_SPRITE↔STEAM_WRAITH 77.6 → 33.3, SKYFALLEN_KING↔FORGE_SAINT 77.3 → 68.4, FORGE_SAINT↔SUNKEN_KING 77.2 → 49.3. **The arms swap on hurt for the late humanoids** — `registerRecoilArms(LATE_RECOIL_ARMS)` fires from `lateRecipes()` (`actors-late.ts:51`) and every one of the 645 non-dead bakes is a single 8-connected component, so the `lift: 2` throat did close what the swap opened. The nine multi-component bakes are all in dead and all licensed (GALE's 45-cell dagger ×3, FROST_WISP's shatter, FEN_FIRE's embers).

**Conventions where the artists and this critic differ.** (a) *Interior below L 35*: I erode with 8 neighbours, `metrics.md` with 4, so 16 of 43 rows differ by up to 2.3 points — every actor clears 20 either way, and no other column differs by more than 0.6 on any row. (b) *Settle*: the review's 21–39 band is the ABSOLUTE colour diff of attack 2 against idle 0; the best-aligned diff of the same pair runs 0.0–38.1 and would fail fourteen actors — B and round 10 use the absolute one and so do I. (c) *Contrast floor*: A and B both quote pack means against a flat swatch; I quote both, and the swatch each pack was authored against is no longer any biome's floor. (d) *Torso band*: rows 18–40 measured from the silhouette top, on the actor's own 4-neighbour-eroded `mode=sil` mask planted at the screen's feet anchors — the same convention rounds 9–11 have used.

## Rule questions for the coordinator

1. **Criterion 6 is a stage-swatch criterion and cannot survive contact with a lit floor.** Over the crypt's measured lit floor (L\* 36.6) only a pixel above **L\* 68** clears 3:1 at all; over the marsh's (L\* 43.9) only above **L\* 77**. Criterion 1 requires ≥ 20 % of the sprite below L 35. So "≤ 45 % of body pixels below 3:1" is arithmetically unreachable over any of the six real floors — for the heroes too: on the new floors the six heroes read **78.1 %** under 3:1, the crypt six 85.6 %, the marsh seven 78.6 %, the four late packs 75.8–86.1 %. On the stage navy the same heroes read 41.4 %. **Decide the ground the criterion is written against** — keep `#1d2b53` as an authoring gauge and delete the in-scene reading, or restate criterion 6 in scene as "actor median ≥ 2:1 against the ground it stands on" (Global 2's ruler) and drop the per-pixel share. B raised this for the twenty-four; it is now the whole cast.
2. **`legal()`'s 3.2:1 floor (L\* ≈ 51) versus the in-scene value targets.** A's arithmetic is right: only steps 0, 1 and the PLANE step sit below L 51, and those are exactly the pixels criterion 6 caps. DUST_WRAITH's torso is already 34.9 % below L 35 on the sheet with p50 51.2 and the sprite is at 43.1 % sub-3:1 — a torso p50 of 52 in scene needs roughly +15 points of sub-3:1. **Either lower the floor for named ramps** (`ASH_HIDE`, `CRYPT_*`, the imp's wing ramp) **or take the value out of the light rig** (`GAIN_FLOOR 0.30` / `GAIN_LIFT 0.28`, or the bloom's contribution on actor cells). No artist can close Global 1 or 3 from `parts.ts` alone while both rules stand.
3. **Whose criterion is the near hero seat?** GALE, BASALT and LUMEN passed the ≥ 12 % bar at slot 2 in round 10 with the identical sprites; the floor moved and they fail. Either the criterion is measured on a named reference seat and biome, or the light rig owes a per-seat cap so slot 2 is not 8–15 points brighter than slot 1. As written, an artist is being marked on someone else's file.
4. **The 21–39 settle band and the 17 % idle floor are review conventions, not documented rules.** Both are now load-bearing (ASH_HOUND fails the second). Worth writing into the ship criteria alongside the six, with the absolute-diff convention named, so the next round is not measured against a moving target.

### Coordinator decisions after round 11 (2026-09-06)

The critic's four rule questions are answered here so round 12 is measured against a fixed target.

1. **Criterion 6 is a sheet criterion on the stage swatch `#1d2b53`, and only there.** Its in-scene reading ("≤ 45 % of body pixels under 3:1 over the lit floor") is withdrawn: over a floor at L\* 36–44 it is arithmetically incompatible with criterion 1. In scene the ruler is Global 2's — **the actor's median against the ground it stands on, at both strips** — and its bar for round 12 is **≥ 1.5:1 at every seat** (the 2:1 figure is unreachable while the actors' p50 (45–55) and the pool floor (L 36–44) sit in the same band; see 5).
2. **`legal()`'s 3.2:1 floor stays.** The in-scene value targets (hero torso ≥ 12 % below L 35 with p50 ≤ 55 at EVERY seat; no enemy torso above the party median + 5) are owned jointly: the artist owns the sheet (an enemy authored with a head band at L 64–66 is the artist's), the light rig owns **seat uniformity** — no seat's p50 may sit more than 5 L above the median seat, and `GAIN_LIFT` / the pool `actorWeight`s are re-tuned to the new floor so the near hero seat is not the brightest thing on the stage. That is a light.ts pass (scene round 5), measured on crypt seeds 1, 4, 12, 16, 20 at all three slots and the FROST MARSH frame from `playfull acts=2 seed=7`.
3. **The near hero seat is measured on those named references** (2), never on "the seats the artist reached".
4. **Two review conventions become ship criteria 7 and 8**: the settle band **21–39 %** (absolute colour diff of attack 2 against idle 0) and the idle floor **≥ 17 %** frame-to-frame change with ≥ 30 % of it in the top third for floating/flying actors.
5. **The value order is inverted relative to the reference, and this session does not fix it.** In `octopath-4` the front sprite sits at p50 L 37 with 44 % below L 35 on a ground of p50 84; ours sit 8–20 L ABOVE a ground of 36–44. Every round since 6 has pushed the sprites' darks (criterion 1) while their p50 stayed near 50, and round 4 of the scene loop lifted the floor into the same band. The reference's law is a bright lit ground under dark, saturated-in-the-shadows sprites. Closing that is a re-specification of the sprite value law (p50 ≈ 40, ≥ 35 % below L 35) together with the foot pools at L 60+, i.e. an artist round and a scene round that move together — recorded as the FIRST task of the next session in STATUS.md, not attempted piecemeal here.

**9. The eight creatures' hurt 0.** `creature()` no longer only drops the body a cell on frame 0. New `liftedCrown(id, by)` derives a copy of a creature's hurt grid with the top 32 % of its silhouette carried two rows up inside a grid grown two rows at the top (the row under the cut duplicated, `raiseFront`'s rule), registers it in `PART_LIBRARY`, and the keyframe pays the two rows back in `dy` — the body lands on exactly the cells it landed on before and only the head moves; fires only where `crownDyOf` > −1 or `headDyOf` > −0.5, computed at module load. crownDy / head-centroid dy before → after: RUIN_RAPTOR +1/+0.3 → −1/−1.3, WIND_SPRITE +1/−0.1 → −1/−2.1, STEAM_WRAITH +1/−1.0 → −1/−3.0, LIGHTNING_HAWK +1/+0.3 → −1/−1.1, EMBER_ELEMENTAL +1/+1.2 → −1/−0.8, ASH_HOUND 0/+0.4 → −2/−1.6, FEN_FIRE 0/−0.3 → −2/−2.3, STORM_DRAKE 0/+0.2 → −2/−1.8; CINDER_IMP −1/−0.3 → −3/−2.3, SILT_CRAB −1/+0.5 → −3/−1.5. Hash check over all 645 pose bakes of all 43 (B's round-11 files pinned): 133 bakes moved and every one of B's twenty-four is byte-identical except six hurt-0 frames — exactly the six creatures the round-11 Animation paragraph named.

### Round 12 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 9/10 (from 9/10)

Both of round 11's headline scene failures are closed, and one of them was the worst frame in the game. **The party no longer fails its own criterion anywhere on the named references**: over crypt seeds 1, 4, 12, 16, 20 and the driven FROST MARSH frame, all seventeen hero seats read torso p50 **34.0–53.3 with 17.9–54.3 % below L 35** against the ≥ 12 % / ≤ 55 bar — GALE at slot 2 goes 51.4 / 8.8 % → **40.8 / 18.7 %**, BASALT at slot 2 54.1–54.5 / 10.8–11.0 % → **47.4–49.0 / 34.4–38.6 %**, LUMEN at slot 2 56.3 / 9.2 % → **42.9 / 20.1 %**, and in the marsh BASALT 60.5 / 8.0 % → **53.3 / 18.7 %** and GALE 56.0 / 8.5 % → **46.3 / 21.0 %**. **And the hit frame is fixed**: on the target's own masked pixels across 24 seeds where a hit landed, the peak share above L 75 is **3.0–26.7 %, median 15.9**, and at +360 ms **0.7–8.5 %, median 2.9** — against round 11's 47–87 % peak (median ≈ 62) and the full-frame critic's 61 % / 56 %. Both the ≤ 30 % and ≤ 15 % targets now hold on every seed, the edge flash keeps the pose (the flashed CINDER_IMP in `s12-hit-0a.png` still shows brows, horns, wings and legs inside a lit rim), and this is the first round where nothing in a battle frame reads as a white blob. Underneath that the sheet is the cleanest it has been: `metrics.md` **43/43** and every one of the six criteria re-derived off my own zoom-1 bake agrees; one 8-connected component in **all 516 non-dead bakes** of all 43; palette overlap max **21.5 %**; humanoid mirror max **80.2** and nearest silhouette max **76.7**, none at 78; plane blob **≥ 40 on all 25 humanoids**; crownDy **−1 to −3 on all 43** — A's `liftedCrown` closed the last eight creatures; dead height **25–56 %** (PYRE_KNIGHT 63 → **55**); max pairwise dead IoU **74.5**; chroma cast mean **12.56** with **nothing over 14.5 but the two licensed sources** (FEN_FIRE 40.54, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 22.79), where four garments were over it last round. The drawings landed too: RUIN_RAPTOR is a hook-billed bird of prey at ×6, SUNKEN_KING's trident is one gripped weapon through all five pose rows, LIGHTNING_HAWK and STORM_DRAKE are modelled (edge density 50.2 → **68.6** and 54.1 → **77.9**), LEVIATHAN_SPAWN 59.9 → **85.2**, CRYPT_WARDEN's bronze is off the crypt tan and no longer camouflage at 1×, HOLLOW_KING and PALE_SAINT are no longer the brightest things in their frames, and ASH_HOUND has a head, a ruff, ash markings and a haunch where it had three rectangles. It is not SHIP for three reasons. **First, the enemy plane still out-values the party**: over 25 driven seeds an enemy torso is above the party median + 5 on **24 of 25** (21 of 25 above the best hero + 5) — the same 92–96 % rate as rounds 10 and 11 — because the enemies came down (CINDER_IMP 53.1–54.0 → **46.6–48.1**, DUST_WRAITH 55.9–57.2 → **47.6–49.7**, CRYPT_WARDEN 56.7–57.2 → **51.2–52.2**, HOLLOW_KING 68.8 → **54.7**) and the party came down with them (EMBER 34.0–36.6, GALE 35.6, BASALT 36.7–38.8), so the gap moved but did not close. **Second, the coordinator's own new ruler fails**: actor median ≥ 1.5:1 against the ground at BOTH strips misses on **7 of the 26 named crypt seats** and 23 of 111 over the sweep, and the offenders are not the two A named — **BASALT 10 seats (worst 1.03), GALE 6 (worst 1.10)**, CINDER_IMP 4 (1.43), ASH_HOUND 3 (1.46) — plus BASALT 1.44 and GALE 1.18 in the marsh frame. **Third, BOG_TOAD is still the banded box round 11 called it**: rows 29–33 carry five consecutive single-colour runs at **92–96 % of the sprite's width** in three colours, its single-colour share went the wrong way (19.8 → **26.4 %**, the highest in the cast outside the licensed glows), and at ×10 the outline is a rectangle with four posts. Beside `octopath-4` at a glance the frame no longer embarrasses the game — the ground has value, the actors sit into it and nothing flares white on a hit — but the reference's law still runs the other way: its front lizardman sits at p50 **37.4 with 44 % below L 35 on a ground of p50 83.6**, a gap of **−46 L**, while our 111 seats sit **−2 to +31 L above** their ground, median **+15.9**. That is decision 5's recorded re-specification and I do not fail the round on it.

## Global

1. **The enemy plane over the party** (`game/art/parts.ts` ramps + `engine/light.ts`; part-pinned — see Rule questions 2). 24 of 25 seeds. Offenders by seed count: **CINDER_IMP 16** (torso 46.6–48.1, head band 50.1–55.2), **DUST_WRAITH 15** (47.6–49.7, head band 48.4–50.8), **CRYPT_WARDEN 8** (51.2–52.2, head band 54.0–55.2), **HOLLOW_KING 2** (54.7), **ASH_HOUND 2** (40.0). Target as written: no enemy torso above the party median + 5 at any seat.

## Per character

- **BOG_TOAD** — Global 3. The idle is genuinely restored (13.5 → **36.7 %** frame-to-frame; the body rises and the belly band shifts on frame 2) and the numbers A quotes are all real (edge 76.7, blob 11.1, sub-3:1 33.8, >L 75 10.4, contrast 3.47), but the drawing did not follow them: at ×10 the silhouette is a rectangle with flat sides and a flat bottom, the "dome" steps only at the eye crown, and the three named bands are five full-width rows. The knee is authored (thigh/knee/shank/foot step in width) and invisible at ×2. Still 14 colours / **8.2 per 1000**, the emptiest silhouette in the cast — A's own "a third material for the throat or warts" is the fix.
- **ASH_HOUND** — the drawing is genuinely won and one shape spoils it. Head, ears, eye, muzzle, a three-step ruff, ash markings breaking the topline, a stepped haunch and four ribcage arcs all read at ×10; edge density 64.6 → **69.3**, blob 11.8 → **10.5**, single-colour share 36.5 → **24.8**, colours 16 → **22**, no row runs 60 % of the width. What is left is `HOUND_BELLY`: the ventral coat is one flat pale wedge whose top edge is a straight 29-cell run — 58 % of the sprite's width, its longest — and it is the largest flat shape on the animal, reading as a plank rather than fur (`parts.ts` `houndFlank` :3785). Its ground contrast dips to 1.46 at one seat (Global 2).
