- **LUMEN** — nothing owed, and every driven seat passes (torso 39.8–42.9 / 17.9–28.4 %), so the rig verifier's LUMEN failure does not reproduce on a driven frame. One observation nobody has recorded: LUMEN's head band is **65.2–66.2 p50 with 6.3–18.5 % below L 35**, the brightest actor band in the game by 6–8 L over BASALT's helm (57.9–59.3). It is a halo and reads as licensed, but it is what the eye lands on in the party.
- **SABLE** — the one hero seat that fails outside the named references: **57.5 p50 / 32.7 %** at slot 1 on seed 9 (53.9 / 45.8 at slot 1 on seed 3). Round 11 read 60.3 / 17.8 at the same seat, so it improved without being touched; the rig verifier's SABLE failure does reproduce on a driven frame.
- **BASALT, GALE** — nothing owed on the sheet; they are Global 2 (BASALT 10 failing seats, GALE 6, the worst ratios in the game at 1.03 and 1.10). BASALT also carries the largest same-sprite seat difference: whole-figure p50 **38.9 at hero1 against 45.0–45.1 at hero2**.
- **EMBER, TIDE, MARSH_HAG, SILT_CRAB, FEN_FIRE, DROWNED_KNIGHT, FROST_WISP (bar Global 6), WIND_SPRITE, RUIN_SENTINEL, DROWNED_CLOUD, FORGE_GOLEM, SMITH_PRIEST, FURNACE_KNIGHT, SKYFALLEN_KING, DROWNED_SENTINEL, TIDE_ORACLE, WIND_EEL, GALE_MONK, SPIRE_WARDEN, EMBER_ELEMENTAL, THUNDER_COLOSSUS** — nothing owed on the sheet. SILT_CRAB's suture is real (edge 61.6 → **72.6**, blob 20.6 → **12.8**) and takes it off the flattest list.

## Animation

Still the best row on the sheet, and this round finished it. **Hurt 0 lifts the crown on all forty-three** — crownDy **−1 to −3** on every actor, head-centroid dy −0.4 to −3.1 — where round 11 had five creatures at +1 and three at 0; A's `liftedCrown` fires exactly where `crownDyOf > −1`, and the six creatures it touched read **RUIN_RAPTOR −2, WIND_SPRITE −1, STEAM_WRAITH −1, LIGHTNING_HAWK −1, EMBER_ELEMENTAL −1, ASH_HOUND −2, FEN_FIRE −2, STORM_DRAKE −2**, with CINDER_IMP and SILT_CRAB deepening to −3. **Settles** (attack 2 against idle 0, absolute) run **20.8 (FROST_WISP) – 38.8 (WIND_SPRITE)**: 42 of 43 inside 21–39, FROST_WISP the single miss; the next lowest are CRYPT_WARDEN 22.4, FEN_FIRE 23.2, THUNDER_COLOSSUS 23.4, SMITH_PRIEST 24.0, and the top of the band is SUNKEN_KING 38.7, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 37.9, SILT_CRAB 37.7, PYRE_KNIGHT 37.4. **Idle change clears 17 on all 43** for the first time — A's two are restored (ASH_HOUND 11.8 → **18.2**, BOG_TOAD 13.5 → **36.7**) and the new floor is SILT_CRAB 19.6, DROWNED_CLOUD 20.4, FROST_WISP 20.8, SPIRE_SERAPH 21.6. **Top-third share ≥ 30 on all 43**, lowest WIND_SPRITE 30.1, VAULT_JELLY 31.9, FROST_WISP 34.4, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 35.0 — every floater clears it. **Attack travels on all 43** (62.1 BOG_TOAD – 97.5 WIND_EEL, absolute against idle 0) and reads as one where I opened the sheet: `poses-SUNKEN_KING.png` swings the trident forward and settles, `poses-BOG_TOAD.png` opens the jaw as a dark wedge and rears, `poses-ASH_HOUND.png` lunges — though the hound's attack-1 grid loses the ash markings and the coat and reads as one flat tan mass, the weakest single frame in the cast. **Deaths**: all 43 collapse at **25 % (SABLE) – 56 % (SUNKEN_KING)** of idle height, no rotated faces, props detached; **max pairwise dead IoU 74.5** (CRYPT_WARDEN↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS), then FORGE_GOLEM↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS 74.0, HOLLOW_KING↔FORGE_SAINT 73.8, DUST_WRAITH↔GALE_MONK 73.5; median 39.2 over all 43 (nineteen 37.5, late 42.4), nothing at 78. **Components**: 645 bakes, **9 multi-component and all nine in dead** — GALE's dagger ×3, FROST_WISP's shatter ×3, FEN_FIRE's embers ×3 — so every one of the 516 idle/attack/cast/hurt bakes is a single 8-connected region. Diffing all 645 bakes against round 11's, **only the twenty-five actors the two artists named moved**; the other eighteen are byte-identical, so nothing was broken in passing.

## Rule questions for the coordinator

1. **Criterion 7's lower bound versus FROST_WISP.** Its settle has read **20.8 %** for three rounds — 0.2 under the band — and both artists have now declined it in writing. Either the band is 20–39 and the wisp passes, or `actors.ts:2089`'s bare `{ part: 'wisp_body_c' }` gets a carry and it is closed in one edit. As written it is the only criterion 1–8 failure on the whole sheet, and it is 0.2 wide.
2. **"No enemy torso above the party median + 5" is now a relative rule the artists cannot pass.** Round 12 took every named enemy down (CINDER_IMP −6, DUST_WRAITH −8, CRYPT_WARDEN −5, HOLLOW_KING −14, PALE_SAINT −17 in scene) and the rate did not move, because the party came down with it: the crypt party median is now 34.5–45.5 and `legal()`'s 3.2:1 floor puts an enemy authored at the floor at L ≈ 51 on the sheet and 47–52 in scene. To clear CRYPT_WARDEN's 52.2 against a 34.5 median needs a sheet torso p50 near 40 — 11 points under the floor. **Decide whether this becomes an absolute ceiling** (e.g. no enemy torso above 50 in scene, which CINDER_IMP, DUST_WRAITH and ASH_HOUND already meet and CRYPT_WARDEN misses by 2), **or is folded into decision 5's re-specification** and suspended until then. As it stands it is unpassable and it is the round's first listed defect.
3. **The ≥ 1.5:1 ground ruler at the u+30 strip.** The failing strip is always u+30, where the floor is out of the contact shadow at L 37.4–42.5. Over that ground the passing band is *below* L 31.3 or *above* L 53.5, and `legal()` parks garment midtones at L ≈ 51 — so BASALT (whole p50 38.9–45.1) and GALE (35.4–40.6) sit inside the dead band by construction and read 1.03–1.33. Either the ruler is measured at the u+6 strip only (every seat in the game passes there: minimum 1.75), or the floor's own value has to leave the actors' band, which is `engine/light.ts` / `backdrops.ts`, not an artist's file. As written, BASALT and GALE are marked on someone else's file for the second round running.
4. **Plane blob ≥ 40 is written on humanoids and the beasts are now failing it silently.** CINDER_WOLF went 227 → **10** cells this round because B's paired ribs cut the plane apart, and FROST_WISP (0), EMBER_ELEMENTAL (0), CINDER_IMP (21) and LEVIATHAN_SPAWN (33) are also under it. Either the rule extends to the quadrupeds with a size-scaled threshold, or the review should say in as many words that the beasts are exempt, so a rib pass is not silently trading a rule the next critic will re-apply.
5. **Seat uniformity cannot be certified from driven frames.** The rig's ±5 L band is a same-sprite test, and on driven frames the frame phase is the same size as the seat effect: EMBER at hero0 alone spans whole-figure p50 **41.0–48.5** across 25 seeds, while BASALT's seat difference (hero1 **38.9** against hero2 **45.0–45.1**) is 6.1 L. I can confirm the *outcome* — every hero seat passes its own criterion on the named references — but not the band. If the band is to stay a ruler it needs the rig's one-sprite probe as its instrument, named in the review.

Frames viewed: `tools/ref/octopath-1-job-lineup.jpg` and `tools/ref/octopath-4-desert-battle.jpg` (first, and kept open); my own worktree bakes in `scratchpad/critic-r12/sheets/` — `all-x2.png`, `lineup-x2.png`, `lineup-x2-grey.png`, `lineup-x2-sil.png`, `late-x2.png`, `late-x2-grey.png`, `late-x2-sil.png`; over each biome's own measured ground, `heroes-x4-floor.png` and `crypt-x4-floor.png` (`#774d47`), `marsh-x4-floor.png` (`#417078`), `late-ruins-x4-floor.png` (`#6b5460`), `late-forge-x4-floor.png` (`#5d3827`), `late-vault-x4-floor.png` (`#32606a`), `late-spire-x4-floor.png` (`#565f92`), `spire-x2-floor.png`; `beasts1-x6.png` (ASH_HOUND / BOG_TOAD / CINDER_WOLF / RUIN_RAPTOR), `beasts2-x6.png` (LIGHTNING_HAWK / STORM_DRAKE / LEVIATHAN_SPAWN / SILT_CRAB), `weapons-x6.png` (SUNKEN_KING / SPIRE_SERAPH / FORGE_SAINT / CRYPT_WARDEN), `a-bosses-x6.png` (CINDER_IMP / DUST_WRAITH / HOLLOW_KING / PALE_SAINT), `ht-x10.png` (ASH_HOUND / BOG_TOAD at ×10), `lev-sil-x6.png` (LEVIATHAN_SPAWN / CINDER_WOLF in `mode=sil`), `herobody-x3.png` (SMITH_PRIEST / DROWNED_SENTINEL / GALE_MONK / SPIRE_WARDEN beside the six heroes); `poses-ASH_HOUND.png`, `poses-BOG_TOAD.png`, `poses-SUNKEN_KING.png`, `poses-CINDER_WOLF.png`; the six HIGH backdrops `bd-EMBER_CRYPT / FROST_MARSH / SKY_RUINS / ASHEN_FORGE / SUNKEN_VAULT / STORM_SPIRE.png` at `actors=0`; battle frames at 1× — `cap16/battle-1.png`, `cap16/battle-hit-0a.png`, `cap20/battle-1.png`, `s12-hit-0a.png`, `pf/playfull-battle-boss.png`, and `marsh-battle.png` (act 2, FROST MARSH, driven for this verdict). Measurements: `node tools/capture.mjs sheets` (43/43 PASS) and `battle seed=N` for seeds 1, 3, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 16, 20, plus `playfull acts=2 seed=7` (PLAYFULL ENDED — the party died in act 2 at room 12), from a detached worktree of HEAD 6aa5801 on `claude/fable-credits-reset-wbtv7t`; and my own drivers over seeds 1–25 (`sweep.mjs`, 111 actor-seats, 24 landed hits), `sheet.mjs` / `report.mjs` / `detail.mjs` / `anim.mjs` / `dead.mjs` off the real bake, `floorhex.mjs` for the six floors, `gap.mjs` and `refval.mjs` for the reference gap, and a byte-diff of all 645 pose bakes against the round-11 critic's.

### Coordinator decisions after round 12 (2026-09-06)

The critic's five rule questions, answered so round 13 is measured against a fixed target.

1. **Criterion 7's band is 20–39 %.** A 0.2-point miss on a 19-point band is noise; FROST_WISP passes as it stands. Round 13 may still give `wisp_body_c`'s settle keyframe a carry, but nothing is owed.
2. **The relative enemy-vs-party rule is replaced by an absolute ceiling.** In scene, on the named references (crypt seeds 1, 4, 12, 16, 20 at all three slots; the FROST MARSH frame from `playfull acts=2 seed=7`): a standard enemy's torso p50 ≤ **50**, an elite's or boss's ≤ **55**. CINDER_IMP, DUST_WRAITH and ASH_HOUND already meet it; CRYPT_WARDEN (51.2–52.2) misses by two; HOLLOW_KING and PALE_SAINT (54.7) meet the boss line. "Above the party median + 5" is retired — it could not be passed while `legal()`'s floor and the party's own darkening move together.
3. **The ≥ 1.5:1 ground ruler is measured at the u+6 strip only** (inside the contact shadow, where every seat in the game passes at ≥ 1.75) until scene round 6 lands its ask that the ground inside both foot clusters reads p50 ≥ 45; after that the u+30 strip is the scene owner's number, not an artist's. BASALT and GALE owe nothing on it.
4. **The plane-blob rule extends to every actor, size-scaled**: the largest contiguous L* 28–38 blob ≥ 40 cells on a humanoid and ≥ **2 % of the sprite's cells** on a creature or elemental (CINDER_WOLF's 10, FROST_WISP's 0, EMBER_ELEMENTAL's 0, CINDER_IMP's 21 and LEVIATHAN_SPAWN's 33 are round-13 items; the licensed glows FROST_WISP and FEN_FIRE keep their exemption on chroma, not on this).
5. **Seat uniformity is certified by the rig's one-sprite probe** (the scene verifier's `tools/seatprobe.ts` method: one recipe planted at all six anchors, replaying battle.ts's call order), never from driven frames, whose phase spread is the size of the effect. The review names the instrument.

### Round 13 (2026-09-06): what artist B changed

Files: `game/art/parts-late.ts`, `game/art/actors-late.ts`. `tools/lineup.ts` needed no change.

**1. CINDER_WOLF — rebuilt on wolf proportions.** The build was a grazer's (a thin neck at 45° carrying the skull eleven rows above the withers, a shallow barrel, four long thin legs). Rebuilt as five masses whose undersides tell the story — rump, haunch, a tucked barrel, a deep brisket, a short thick neck running forward out of the withers (13 cells at the shoulder, 8 at the poll); the skull four rows below the shoulder line with a poll four rows proud, a seven-row stop from brow to bridge, the round-12 muzzle hung off the skull's bottom, erect ears; the legs off the elbow and stifle with the tuck between them. Grid 50 → 64 wide. cells 949 → 1259, edge 72.2 → 70.4, blob 5.2 → 12.7, mirror 42.6, sub-3:1 35.7, chroma 14.11, idle 49.4, settle 22.7, crownDy −1, plane blob 10 cells (1.05 %) → 106 (8.42 %) — `WOLF_HIDE` gained `{ plane: 2 }` (its plane step baked at L* 27.96, just outside the 28–38 bin) and a new `underPlane` runs the belly plane before the legs. The dead grid's four dorsal spines are gone (one-cell tufts as the standing sprite). At ×6 and in mode=sil a deep-chested canid with erect ears, a long snout, a tucked loin and short legs.

**2. STEAM_WRAITH — modelled.** Each of the five billows is four concentric tones (veil, shadow body, a dense core in a second material — the new `VAPOUR_CORE` ramp — and a lit heart), the cores leaning alternately, a crescent under each; both vent arms got a core; the far side falls away on an `underPlane` plus a stepped plane. edge 56.9 → 75.4, colours 10 → 14, blob 8.2, > L 75 29.5 → 19.7, plane blob 198 (22.9 %), one component, mirror 73.7, top-third 34.6, settle 30.2 (its own `steam_settle` grid — the old `dy: −1` changed 47.1 % once the billows had four tones).

**3. The three schematics.** VAULT_JELLY: nine radial canals apex to rim in a three-tone cycle, drawn after the ring arc — blob 19.0 → 11.2, edge 74.3 → 89.2, sub-3:1 43.4, mirror 65.1, plane blob 113 (15.4 %). DROWNED_CLOUD: the rain as broken diagonal streaks of two lengths (4–6 cells of run, 21+ vs 12+ rows, a deep dash breaking each in value), the base course broken every fifth column — blob 11.4, edge 74.5, colours 16, mirror 65.4, settle 24.0 (`cloud_settle`), plane blob 311 (34.8 %). EMBER_ELEMENTAL: the ring broken into flame tongues of three heights (3-4-8 cycle) thrown out of the orbit, the clinker off-centre, an authored plane on the far-lower quadrant; `CLINKER` `{ plane: 5 }` (the default baked at L* 26 and +8 at 38 — only the middle satisfies both bins) — plane blob 0 → 62 (8.78 %), edge 79.6, blob 9.3, mirror 52.4, sub-3:1 44.2, interior < L 35 28.5, chroma 21.92, top-third 41.2.

**4. SPIRE_SERAPH.** One `featherPass` over the six-wing fan in MID / SHADOW / DARK (no specular on a wing) so the lance's vane stays the only pale spike; the blob was the ROBE (336 cells of the shadow step) — four folds belt to hem and a stepped PLANE down the far skirt: blob 16.2 → 9.9, edge 70.3 → 75.7, sub-3:1 44.2, mirror 69.5, plane blob 240.

### Round 13 verdict: ONE MORE ROUND — 9/10 (from 9/10)

Every one of round 12's three reasons is closed, and closed properly. **BOG_TOAD is a drawing**: the five full-width stripes are gone (rows with a one-colour run over 60 % of the width **8 → 0**, longest run 47 → **26 cells = 53 %**), single-colour share 26.4 → **19.4 %**, largest blob 11.1 → **6.9 %**, and at ×2 it is an egg with a stepped dome, a leaning brow, two eye crowns and a cream vocal sac with a catchlight under the jaw — not a box with four posts. **The interiors came back where the value work had flattened them** — PALE_SAINT 67.9 → **72.3**, HOLLOW_KING 74.3 → **77.1**, CINDER_IMP 73.3 → **77.1**, DUST_WRAITH's blob 15.8 → **7.8 %** — and **STEAM_WRAITH, the flattest interior in the cast for three rounds, went 56.9 → 75.4** with four tones per billow and a dense core; the whole round-12 blob list is off it (VAULT_JELLY 19.0 → **11.2**, SPIRE_SERAPH 16.2 → **9.9**). The full-frame critic's mirror item is closed outright — BOG_TOAD 100 → **77.3**, CINDER_IMP 97.1 → **66.6**, DUST_WRAITH 94.3 → **63.0**, SILT_CRAB 91.6 → **66.2** — and its three schematics are drawn: VAULT_JELLY has nine radial canals (edge 74.3 → **89.2**, the cast's highest), DROWNED_CLOUD's rain is broken diagonals, EMBER_ELEMENTAL is a clinker inside thrown flame tongues. CINDER_WOLF is a canid at last, deep-chested with the skull below the shoulder line and erect ears, and it **dies as the same animal** — the four dorsal spines are gone from the dead grid. The sheet is spotless: `metrics.md` **43 PASS / 0 FAIL** and I re-derive every criterion off my own zoom-1 bake; one 8-connected component in **all 516 non-dead bakes** (9 multi-part bakes, all in dead); palette overlap max **18.4 %**; humanoid mirror max **80.2**; nearest silhouette max **76.7**, none at 78; **plane blob passes on all 43** under the size-scaled rule (tightest CINDER_IMP 21 cells = 2.16 %, LEVIATHAN_SPAWN 33 = 2.99 %); crownDy **−1 to −3 on all 43**; dead height **25–56 %**; max pairwise dead IoU **74.5**; settles **20.4–38.8**, idle **17.9–55.8**, top-third **30.1–96.6**; chroma cast mean **12.53** with nothing over 14.5 but the two licensed glows. And the frame it all sits in is transformed — scene round 6 put a light well above the horizon and took the lit floor under the enemy rank from L 37.3 to **44.6** in the crypt (marsh 44.3 → **52.4**, ruins 38.4 → **44.4**, forge 27.6 → **42.2**, vault 38.0 → **40.1**, spire 41.6 → **46.0**), the frame's share above L 75 went 0.7 % → **7.5–7.7 %**, and the enemy rank now sits **below** the party plane for the first time (enemy whole p50 mean **50.5** against the party's **53.9**).

It is not SHIP, for four measured misses, all narrow and all pinned by a coordinator decision. **First, the absolute enemy ceiling (decision 2) is broken twice**: DUST_WRAITH's torso p50 is **52.9 at every seat it is drawn** (seeds 3, 11 and the named seed 20; whole-figure 52.0–53.0), against the ≤ 50 standard-enemy line, and PALE_SAINT reads **55.9** in the driven FROST MARSH frame against the ≤ 55 boss line. And the sheet says why: measured with one convention on both trees, **not one enemy's authored torso value moved this round** — DUST_WRAITH 51.2 → **51.2**, CINDER_IMP 51.3 → **51.3**, HOLLOW_KING 51.7 → **51.7**, PALE_SAINT 53.6 → **53.6**, ASH_HOUND 51.7 → **51.7** — so every in-scene improvement artist A claims under item 4 is the scene writer's rig, not a ramp. **Second, the hero bar fails on the named references**: LUMEN's torso reads **56.1** at hero1 (seeds 1, 2, 11) and **55.2** at hero2 (seed 20) against p50 ≤ 55, on a sheet torso of 51.9 that the rig lifts +4.2; whole-figure it is 57.3–58.9, the brightest actor in the party by 3–5 L. **Third, the ≥ 1.5:1 ground ruler at the u+6 strip (decision 3) misses at 6 of 30 named seats** — CRYPT_WARDEN **1.39** at all five crypt seats, TIDE **1.34** and **1.42**, BASALT **1.41** — and the cause is not the art: round 6's derived foot pools peak under the *middle* seat of each rank, so the contact strip there now reads L **40.3–49.2** where the outer seats read 20.9–27.6, and decision 3's premise ("inside the contact shadow, where every seat passes at ≥ 1.75") is void on this floor. Artist A's "u+6 2.52–2.55, so it passes as written" is measured on the round-5 rig and is **false at HEAD**. **Fourth, CRYPT_WARDEN's lit-from-above margin fell from +12 to 7.7 L** (top quarter 51.1, bottom 43.4) — `metrics.md` rounds both to integers and reads 51 − 43 = 8, so the rig passes it, but unrounded it is the one actor under criterion 1's bar. Beside `octopath-4` at a glance nothing in the frame embarrasses the game — the ground has value, the light well sits behind the stage, the sprites sit into it and the boss reads dark — and the one thing I would still point at is ASH_HOUND, which at ×2 reads as a small cow: the white face blaze, the horn-shaped ears, the blocky hooved legs and a pale ventral plank that is still the largest flat shape on the animal. The reference's value law is unchanged and I do not fail the round on it (decision 5): our seats sit **+8.2 to +32.2 L above the ground at u+6, median +25.1** (+4.0 to +33.5, median +18.8 at u+30) where `octopath-4`'s front figure sits 13–40 L **below** its ground.

**A note on the instrument.** `battle.ts:1359` draws every hero with `facing: -1`, and `drawActor` turns that into `flipX` about `recipe.feet.x` — so a hero in a battle frame is **mirrored** relative to its sheet bake. Rounds 10–12 planted the silhouette mask un-mirrored and anchored on the bounding-box centre rather than the feet anchor. I rebuilt the rig this round: the lineup sheet's feet anchor at zoom 1 is local (54, 98) in each 108 × 108 cell, so a mask cell's offset is `(px − 54, py − 98)` — exactly the `(cx − originX, cy − originY)` `drawBaked` uses — and heroes map `dx → −dx − 1`. `overlay-s1.png` paints the rows-18–40 band back onto the frame and it lands on the torso to the pixel. With the old convention TIDE's torso read 55.3 and CRYPT_WARDEN's +14.7 above its sheet; with the corrected one every actor's sheet→scene lift is **−2.5 to +5.1 L**, which is what `light.ts`'s contract says it should be. So LUMEN's miss is newly *visible*, not newly *caused*, and round 12's hero seat numbers are not comparable to these.

## Per character

- **DUST_WRAITH** — Global 1. Untouched this round (byte-identical to round 12) and the corrected instrument shows it is the party's ceiling problem: torso **56.1 / 55.2**, whole figure **57.3–58.9**, against BASALT 51.7–53.4 and EMBER 51.8–52.3 at the same seats. Its contrast holds (3.32, sub-3:1 44.6). Two costs. And its lit-from-above delta fell to **7.7**.
- **ASH_HOUND** — its numbers all pass and the coat work is genuine: the ventral coat's straight edge 29 → **20 cells (40 %)**, a comb that travels with the breath (idle change **20.5**), and attack 1 keeps the coat (interior edge 58.2 → **69.6**, colours 17 → **20**). At ×2 it still reads as a small cow, not a hound — a white face blaze, ears that read as horns, four blocky hooved legs, and the pale ventral coat as a straight-topped plank. Its dead pose is a horizontal slab with four leg stubs below the ground line, the weakest corpse in the cast.
- **BOG_TOAD** — **closed.** Every number A quotes reproduces exactly (run 26 = 53 %, rows over 60 % **0 of 42**, single-colour 19.4 %, blob 6.9 %, colours 19 / 11.4 per 1000, mirror 77.3, edge 77.7, < L 35 38.8, contrast 3.52, chroma 13.41), and as a drawing the egg, the turned brow, the vocal sac with its catchlight and the broken wet band all read at ×2. HOLLOW_KING is inside the boss line at **54.1**; PALE_SAINT is **55.9** in the marsh (Global 1), though at ×1 in `marsh-battle.png` it does not read as the brightest thing in the frame.
- **SILT_CRAB** — Global 5. The mirror fix is real (91.6 → **66.2**) but the new claw cost it: interior edge **72.6 → 69.3**, back onto the flattest five, unclaimed.
- **CINDER_WOLF** — resolved as a build and admitted as an interior. At ×6 and in `late-x2-sil.png` it is a deep-chested canid with erect ears, a level skull, a tucked loin and short legs, and the dead grid is the same animal — B's claim holds. cells 949 → **1259**, plane blob 10 → **106 (8.42 %)**. The cost is exactly as declared: edge **70.4**, blob **12.7**, and the legs are unmodelled 2-cell sticks.
- **STEAM_WRAITH** — resolved, and it was the oldest item on the list. Edge **56.9 → 75.4**, colours 10 → 14, > L 75 29.5 → **19.7**, plane blob 198 (22.9 %). At ×6 the five billows have four tones with a lit heart and a crescent under each. It is still the palest figure in the forge (whole p50 **52.1**, chroma **7.43**, the cast's lowest) and its outline is unchanged.
- **VAULT_JELLY / DROWNED_CLOUD / EMBER_ELEMENTAL** — the three schematics are drawn. The jelly's nine canals give it the cast's highest edge density (**89.2**) and blob 19.0 → **11.2**; the cloud's rain is broken diagonals of two lengths; the ember is a clinker inside thrown tongues with plane blob 0 → **62 (8.78 %)**. All three read as objects rather than diagrams at ×6. The jelly's outline is still a smooth parasol with straight tendrils.

The wings still read as flat dark shards at ×6 rather than feathers, and its head is a pale drum without a face — the weakest boss read in the cast. Taste.
- **LEVIATHAN_SPAWN** — the gaps are real (edge **85.6**, blob 10.4, plane blob 33 = 2.99 %) and at ×6 in colour the coils are parted. On `late-x2-sil.png` at game scale they are **not** three separated coils — a mass with a scalloped bottom edge and a head. B's claim overstates it, as round 12's did.
- **SMITH_PRIEST / DROWNED_SENTINEL / GALE_MONK / SPIRE_WARDEN** — the headwear landed and each number reproduces to the decimal (73.4 / 73.2 / 70.2 / 73.7 against their worst mainline pair, all under the < 78 rule). Beside `octopath-1` they are still four variants of the same head-on-a-column trapezoid, which is what B says. Two of them grew out of the size band (Global 7).
- **MARSH_HAG** — nothing owed on a criterion, but it now carries the cast's worst non-licensed single-colour blob at **15.9 %** and one row at 75 % of its width, and has not been touched since round 9.
- **TIDE** — Globals 3 and 8. Untouched; its ground ratio at the middle seat is the worst in the game (1.34) and it holds the cast's longest single-colour run (93 % of its width).
- **EMBER, GALE, BASALT, SABLE, PYRE_KNIGHT, FROST_WISP, FEN_FIRE, DROWNED_KNIGHT, RUIN_RAPTOR, WIND_SPRITE, RUIN_SENTINEL, STORM_DRAKE, SKYFALLEN_KING, FORGE_GOLEM, FURNACE_KNIGHT, FORGE_SAINT, TIDE_ORACLE, WIND_EEL, SUNKEN_KING, LIGHTNING_HAWK, GALE_MONK, EMBER_ELEMENTAL, THUNDER_COLOSSUS** — nothing owed on the sheet. LIGHTNING_HAWK is the flattest interior in the cast at **68.6** and byte-identical to round 12, so it inherits the title STEAM_WRAITH vacated.

## Animation

Still the best row on the sheet, and nothing regressed. **Settles** (absolute diff of attack 2 against idle 0) run **20.4 (FROST_WISP) – 38.8 (WIND_SPRITE)** — all 43 inside the widened 20–39 band, with FROST_WISP's 20.8 → **20.4** exactly as A records and SUNKEN_KING 38.7, SPIRE_SERAPH 38.3, EMBER_ELEMENTAL 38.1 at the top. **Idle change clears 17 on all 43**, floor **SILT_CRAB 17.9**, then ASH_HOUND 20.5 (from 18.2), SPIRE_SERAPH 21.6, DUST_WRAITH 22.9; the ceiling is VAULT_JELLY 55.8. **Top-third share ≥ 30 on every floater**, lowest WIND_SPRITE **30.1**, VAULT_JELLY 33.3, FROST_WISP 34.4, STEAM_WRAITH 34.6. **crownDy is −1 to −3 on all 43** on hurt 0, head-centroid dy −0.4 to −3.2. **Deaths** collapse at **25 % (SABLE) – 56 % (SUNKEN_KING)** of idle height, max pairwise dead IoU **74.5** (CRYPT_WARDEN↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS), then FORGE_GOLEM↔THUNDER_COLOSSUS 74.0, HOLLOW_KING↔FORGE_SAINT 73.8; median 38.5, nothing at 78. CINDER_WOLF's corpse is the round's animation win — it is the same animal it was standing, where round 12 killed a boar. **Components**: 645 bakes, **9 multi-component and all nine in dead** (GALE's dagger ×3, FROST_WISP's shatter ×3, FEN_FIRE's embers ×3). **Attack travels on all 43**, and ASH_HOUND's attack-1 frame — round 12's "weakest single frame in the cast" — now keeps its coat and its markings (interior edge 58.2 → **69.6**, colours 20). **Hash diff against round 12: 275 of 645 bakes moved and every one is in an actor the two artists named** — A's nine account for exactly **110**, B's thirteen for 165, and the other 21 actors (the six heroes among them) are byte-identical.

**The hit frame**, on the target's own masked pixels across the 11 driven seeds: peak share above L 75 **7.1–32.1 %, median 18.1**, and at +360 ms **7.1–9.9 %, median 7.8**. The +360 ms figure is comfortably inside the ≤ 15 % bar; the peak crosses the ≤ 30 % bar on two seeds (seed 12 **32.1**, seed 1 **30.9**), both CINDER_IMP. Frame-wide, above L 75 reads **7.5–7.7 % at rest and 7.5–8.3 % at the peak** — the scene's light well, not the impact, is what put light in the picture, and a hit still adds only 0.2–0.6 points.

## Rule questions for the coordinator

1. **Decision 3's premise is void on the round-6 floor.** The u+6 strip was chosen because it sits inside the contact shadow "where every seat in the game passes at ≥ 1.75". The derived foot pools peak at each rank's middle seat, so the u+6 ground there now reads **L 40.3–49.2** against 20.9–27.6 at the outer seats, and six named seats fall to **1.34–1.49**. No ramp change can lift CRYPT_WARDEN (49.3) to 1.5:1 over a ground of 40.3 without breaking the 50 ceiling from the other side — it would have to go *darker* than 33, 18 L under `legal()`'s floor. Is the u+6 ruler still the artist's, or does it follow u+30 to the scene owner now that the pools light the seats?
2. **The torso band is defined in absolute rows and a silhouette that grows re-registers it.** CRYPT_WARDEN's horned helm moved its sheet torso p50 from 51.8 to **34.7** with no ramp touched, and its whole figure is 49.3. Should the band be a fraction of the silhouette's height (rows 0.33–0.72, say) so that headwear cannot pass or fail the ceiling by itself? Both readings pass for the warden; DUST_WRAITH fails on both and PALE_SAINT fails on the band only (55.9) and passes on the whole figure (50.8).
3. **Criterion 1's "lit from above ≥ 8 L".** `tools/lineup.ts` rounds the top-quarter and bottom-quarter means to integers before subtracting, so CRYPT_WARDEN reads 51 − 43 = 8 and PASSes; unrounded it is **7.7**. Is the rig's integer arithmetic the rule, or the measurement?
4. **The measurement convention should be recorded.** `battle.ts:1359` draws heroes `facing: -1` → `flipX` about `recipe.feet.x`; rounds 10–12 measured in-scene actors with an un-mirrored mask anchored at the bounding-box centre. The corrected rig (mask offsets `(px − 54, py − 98)` off the zoom-1 sheet cell, `dx → −dx − 1` for heroes) puts every sheet→scene lift at −2.5 to +5.1 L, which matches `light.ts`'s stated contract; the old one produced +14.7 and +23.8 outliers. Round 12's hero seat numbers are not comparable to these, and LUMEN's miss is newly visible rather than newly caused.
5. **The size band 11–14.7 %.** Two standard enemies are now outside it (SPIRE_WARDEN 16.1, DROWNED_SENTINEL 15.3) because silhouette work grew them, and SPIRE_WARDEN is taller in frame than five of six heroes. Is the band a rule the artists must hold, or a description to update?

Frames viewed: the two references, `tools/ref/octopath-1-job-lineup.jpg` and `octopath-4-desert-battle.jpg`, kept open throughout. From a detached worktree at HEAD 59bef99 on :5198, held under `scratchpad/critic-r13/`: `sheets/metrics.md` (43 PASS / 0 FAIL) and `metrics.json`; `all-x2.png`, `all-x2-grey.png`, `all-x2-sil.png`, `late-x2-sil.png`; `crypt-x4-floor.png` and `heroes-x4-floor.png` over the crypt's measured floor `#8d5e51`, `marsh-x4-floor.png` over `#558492`; at ×6 `x6-BOG_TOAD.png`, `x6-wolf-hound-wraith.png` (over the forge's `#9e4c2d`), `x6-saint-king-imp.png`, `x6-three-schematics.png`, `x6-seraph-leviathan.png`, `x6-tide-warden.png`; `sil-x4-herobody.png`; pose sheets `poses-ASH_HOUND.png`, `poses-CINDER_WOLF.png` (and the captured set for the other 18 mainline actors plus STEAM_WRAITH and VAULT_JELLY); the six HIGH backdrops at `actors=0` (`bd-EMBER_CRYPT/FROST_MARSH/SKY_RUINS/ASHEN_FORGE/SUNKEN_VAULT/STORM_SPIRE.png`); driven battle frames `cap1/…/cap20` (11 seeds, `battle-0/1/2`, `-hit-*`, `-inspect`, `-paused`) and `sw-{1,2,3,4,5,8,9,11,12,16,20}-battle.png` with their 132 hit frames; the driven FROST MARSH frame `marsh-battle.png` (PALE_SAINT, seed 7 act 2); `pf/playfull-*.png` (27 screens, `acts=2 seed=7`, incl. `playfull-battle-boss.png`); crops `c-tide-s16.png`, `c-ember-s2.png`, `c-dust-s20.png`, `c-toad-x2.png`, `c-hound-x2.png`, and the mask-alignment proofs `c-overlay-lumen.png` / `c-overlay-warden.png`. Measurements: `sheet.mjs` (43 actors' bakes + silhouettes + 645 pose bakes), `report.mjs`, `pairs13.mjs`, `detail11.mjs`, `dead.mjs`, `anim.mjs`, `claims13.mjs`, `floorhex.mjs`, `sweep.mjs` ×11 seeds, `scene13.mjs` (the corrected in-scene rig, 56 seats), `flash13.mjs`, `flipchk.mjs`, and an md5 diff of all 645 bakes against `../critic-r12/allcells.json`.

### Coordinator decisions after round 13 (2026-09-06)

1. **The ground ruler leaves the artists' file.** Scene round 6 put the derived foot pools' peak under each rank's middle seat, so the u+6 strip there reads L 40–49 and no ramp can clear 1.5:1 over it without breaking the enemy ceiling from the other side. Actor-vs-ground at either strip is now the scene owner's number, and it joins decision 5's re-specification (the reference's ground is ABOVE its figures; ours is still 8–32 L below them at u+6). It is not a sprite criterion.
2. **The torso band is a fraction of the silhouette's height** — rows 0.33–0.72 from the top — so headwear cannot pass or fail a value ruler by re-registering absolute rows. The enemy ceiling (standard ≤ 50, elite/boss ≤ 55) is read on that band. On round 13's tree that leaves one open ceiling item, DUST_WRAITH (52.9 on either reading), and clears PALE_SAINT (50.8 whole-figure, 55.9 on the old band).
3. **Criterion 1's "lit from above ≥ 8 L" is the unrounded measurement**, not `tools/lineup.ts`'s integer arithmetic. CRYPT_WARDEN's 7.7 is a miss; the rig's rounding is a next-session fix in the tool.
4. **The in-scene instrument is recorded**: heroes are drawn `facing: -1` (mirrored about `recipe.feet.x`); an in-scene mask is the zoom-1 sheet cell's silhouette with offsets `(px − 54, py − 98)` from the cell's feet anchor, `dx → −dx − 1` for heroes, planted at the layout anchors. Rounds 10–12's hero seat numbers were taken with an un-mirrored, box-centred mask and are not comparable; LUMEN's miss is newly visible, not newly caused.
5. **The size band is a description, updated**: standard enemies 11–16 % of frame height (SPIRE_WARDEN 16.1 and DROWNED_SENTINEL 15.3 stand), elites 13.6–15.6, bosses 18–26, heroes 15.6–16.7.

What the sheet still owes after round 13, for a bounded round 14 (artist A's file unless noted): DUST_WRAITH's shroud ramp so its torso reads ≤ 50 in scene (sheet ≈ 48); LUMEN's cream-gold ramp so its torso reads ≤ 55 at every named seat (sheet ≈ 50.5 under a +4.2 lift); CRYPT_WARDEN's helm top quarter lit so top − bottom ≥ 8 unrounded; SILT_CRAB's new major claw modelled (edge back ≥ 72). Everything else the critic lists is taste (ASH_HOUND's cow read, MARSH_HAG's blob, LIGHTNING_HAWK's interior, the seraph's face, LEVIATHAN's outline at game scale) or decision 5.

## The full-frame critic (step 6 of the plan)

### Full-frame verdict, round 1: ONE MORE ROUND — sprites 8 · scene 6 · UI 5 · VFX 7 · composition 5

Beside `octopath-4-desert-battle.jpg`, the first thing that reads in `play-battle-3.png` is not a character — it is twelve saturated green and blue bars lying on the floor. That is the whole verdict in one sentence. What already stands up is real and hard-won: the crypt is genuinely *lit*, with a true depth-of-field split (blurred arch and columns, razor-sharp actor plane), three shafts, a warm key pool, drifting motes and an amber-over-violet grade; the heroes sit at ~15 % of the frame height with 2-px cells, a warm rim and a real elliptical contact shadow, exactly the reference's density; the turn ribbon's diamond portrait chips are the reference's own idea, executed well; `vfx-all.png` is thirteen genuinely distinct particle families with hot cores, not blobs; and `bd-FROST_MARSH.png` is a place — moon bloom, banked fog, dead trees, wisps, reeds in a foreground plane. What gives it away is everything drawn *on top of* and *underneath* that: the ground is a perspective grid (`floorGrid()` is the only ground texture in the game, and on `bd-STORM_SPIRE.png` it is unmistakable); the panels are opaque boxes at `PLATE_ALPHA 0.6`, six of them, walling the stage into 51 % of the frame width, where Octopath's are transparent text-and-hairline and only on one side; three near-black slabs 72 % empty run the full width along the bottom; and status, intent and equipment are all communicated by letters in squares — `r`, `B`, `Z`, `*`, and `Wp/Bt/Ar/Nk/Ch/Tm`. The scene reads as a place. The interface reads as a prototype standing in front of it, and it is the interface round — not the art round — that is left.

**Scene, round 2:**
1. `game/art/backdrops.ts` — retire `floorGrid()` as the ground's *only* texture (called for the crypt, SKY_RUINS, ASHEN_FORGE, STORM_SPIRE at α 0.11–0.16; plainly readable under the actors in `battle-0.png` and dominant in `bd-STORM_SPIRE.png`). Replace with a per-biome ground pass: 60–120 scattered stones, tufts and cracks at three sizes and random rotation, 6–10 % contrast, denser inside the lit pool and thinning into the vignette, plus 2–3 broad scuff bands along the stage diagonal. A grid may survive only as one texture among several. Biggest single point on this axis.
2. `game/art/backdrops.ts` — break the horizon. SKY_RUINS, SUNKEN_VAULT, ASHEN_FORGE and STORM_SPIRE each draw a straight full-width dark rule at y ≈ 378; a 1280-px straight line reads as a seam between two offscreens. Step it 8–20 px over 6–10 segments, occlude it at 2–3 points with a mid-ground prop, and feather the joint with the existing helper.
3. `game/art/backdrops.ts` — every biome is one hue (teal, violet, orange, blue). Add one opposing accent per biome — a warm brazier at the far end of a cold room, a cool sky wash into ASHEN_FORGE — so key and fill sit across the wheel, as `octopath-3-village-shafts.jpg` does with cold shafts over warm timber and green grass.
4. `game/art/backdrops.ts` — fill the mid-ground. Each biome carries one or two prop shapes repeated identically: two mirrored anvils (ASHEN_FORGE), five identical radial reed fans (FROST_MARSH), six plain rectangles (STORM_SPIRE), four identical sarcophagus wedges (the crypt). Give each biome 4–6 shapes at three depths and three scales, unmirrored, each with a rim on the key side and a cast shadow on the ground plane; put a mass into the crypt's empty x 560–760 column.
5. `engine/light.ts` — the vignette is a rectangle. `bd-SKY_RUINS.png` and `bd-FROST_MARSH.png` show a visibly darker ~60 px band down each edge with a straight inner boundary. Make it an elliptical falloff, or feather the rect over ≥ 120 px.
6. `engine/light.ts` — the enemy plane is unlit. In a crop of `battle-0.png` Cinder Imp and Ash Hound carry no rim, no key and no fill; the pool sits entirely on the party's side. Give the enemy band its own pool, or drive the rim term off distance to the *nearest* pool.
7. `game/art/backdrops.ts` — the crypt's floor stain at ~(250, 478) is a blurred ellipse with a hard inner edge (`title.png`, zoomed); texture it or drop it.

**UI, round 2:**
1. **Get the bars off the ground.** `game/screens/battle.ts` (stage gauges) + `game/screens/layout.ts` `HP_GAUGE`/`ATB_GAUGE`. Six green + six blue 96-px rules lie on the floor in `play-battle-3.png`, one primary green for every actor, offset to the outer side so none reads as attached. Octopath's ground carries actors, shadows and the VULNERABLE plate and nothing else. Replace with a 3-px HP hairline no wider than the foot span, tucked into the contact shadow, shown only when not full or for ~1.5 s after a change; move ATB entirely into the panel. **CONTRACT** — DESIGN.md's stage row fixes "HP 96×12 and ATB 96×6 under the feet on the outer side". Biggest single point in the whole review.
2. **Make the panels plates.** `game/screens/hud.ts` — `PLATE_ALPHA = 0.6` over `rgb(6,8,16)` is opaque against this scene. Drop to a top-down gradient ~0.35 → 0, delete the panel border except on the focused/target panel, make the HP bar a 4-px rule spanning only the number's width over a dark trough and tinted to the actor's element, and collapse the 28-px status shelf when nothing is active — right now it is empty black holding one flat colour square.
3. **Icons, not letters.** `game/screens/hud.ts` `drawStatusChip`, the ribbon intent badge and the inspect rows. `r` and `B` above the head and in the panel, `*` on the boss, `Z` for stun intent, and `Wp/Bt/Ar/Nk/Ch/Tm` as the six equipment slots on `battle-inspect.png` — where the contract already promises a 32-px slot icon. Draw 6 slot and ~8 status pictograms as small vector paths at `STATUS_ICON = 28`: flame, droplet, shield, boot, eye, spiral. Colour carries family, shape carries effect.
4. **Shrink the skill bar.** `game/screens/battle.ts` + `layout.ts` `SKILL_W/SKILL_H/SKILL_X`. Three 400×96 slabs at ~72 % empty across 100 % of the width are the largest empty area in the game. Go to a compact command list as in `octopath-4` — name, cost and cooldown pips on one 40-px row, ~320 px wide, three rows stacked — or keep three buttons at ~260×56, centred, pips inline. **CONTRACT** — the skill-bar row in DESIGN.md's region table.
5. **Fill the cards or shrink them.** `game/screens/cards.ts`. The relic card is 45 % empty below the set line; the room card ~62 % empty; the wear cards are 74 % empty with the word **EMPTY** printed in each. Height the card from the rows actually drawn, and put the candidate's sprite or the slot silhouette into the void on the wear cards.
6. **Fix the two overlays.** `game/screens/battle.ts`. Pause dims the world and then draws the HUD over it at full strength, so PAUSED sits in front of six full-saturation green bars, and two of the three buttons have no border and dissolve into the dimmed stage (`battle-paused.png`). Inspect draws its panel at plate alpha over a live HUD, so the battle's HP numbers, enemy names and log line ghost through it (`battle-inspect.png`); raise that panel to ~0.92 or suppress the HUD beneath it.
7. **One accent palette.** `title.ts`, `cards.ts`, `end.ts`. Pure yellow (VICTORY, FIGHT, TREASURE, the room card keyline), pure cyan (relic titles) and pure green (every HP bar) belong to no biome and are the most saturated colours in the game. Pull all three onto the grade: an amber that matches the key light, a cyan that matches Tide's orb, a green that matches Gale.
8. **The ribbon's NEXT TURN.** `game/screens/battle.ts`. The marker is a caret under chip 0 only. Enlarge the current chip ~1.4× and break the run with a chevron divider at the round rollover, so the ribbon reads *this turn / next turn* rather than eight equal chips.
9. **Retire the generic button.** `title.ts`, `cards.ts`, `end.ts`, `battle.ts` — PRESS TO BEGIN, CONTINUE, SKIP and BACK are all the same 1-px-bordered rounded rectangle, a web control on an HD-2D diorama. Give the primary action a lit borderless plate with a soft key-coloured glow and the secondary one plain text with a focus underline.
10. **The end screen has no payoff.** `game/screens/end.ts` — `play-end-WIN.png` is four centred lines and a button on an empty stage. Put the surviving party into it under the key light; that is the frame a player screenshots.

**VFX, round 2:**
1. `game/art/vfx.ts` — scale the in-scene effect. On `vfx-all.png` an effect is 1.5–2× the target; in `battle-hit-1b.png` Ember's scorch is a one-sprite-wide dotted arc and in `battle-hit-3b.png` a *crit* is a 40-px sparkle cluster. Multiply emitter radius and particle count on the impact half of every family by ~1.8 at battle scale, and add a ground-contact wash — a short elliptical light pool under the target — which is what plants `octopath-2-water-skill-bloom.jpg`'s skill in the world.
2. `game/art/vfx.ts` — every impact needs a shockwave and a target flash. Add a 1-frame white core on the target silhouette, a thin expanding ring (the `shockwave` family already owns the shape) and 6–10 fast radial sparks with hot white cores, all inside 120 ms.
3. `game/art/vfx.ts` — separate the warm families from the warm crypt. `fireBurst`, `burnFlicker`, `projectile` and `shockwave` are amber on an amber ground and vanish; the only family that reads in-scene is Tide's cool water. Push their cores toward white, add a 1-px dark contact edge, or bias emitted hue 15–20° off the biome key.
4. `game/art/vfx.ts` — hold longer. Most families on the sheet are spent by t 56 % and gone by t 69 %; at 60 Hz that is a flash. Stretch the impact tail to ~450 ms with a decaying ember/spray so the frame is still moving when the damage pop lands.
5. `game/screens/battle.ts` pop draw — the damage pop. Grey bitmap with no outline is illegible over a lit floor, and it collides with panels and other actors' heads. Outline every pop, colour it by family, drop the `(glance)`/`(crit)` parenthetical (the log already says it), and clamp the rise so a pop never crosses an actor or a panel.
6. `game/art/actors.ts` — the hurt white-out. At ×2 in a warm frame the target becomes a featureless cream ghost; cut the tint from 70 % to ~45 % so the recoil pose survives it.

**Composition, round 2:**
1. **Give the world the frame back.** `game/screens/layout.ts` + `game/screens/battle.ts`. The stage is x 312–968 — 51 % of the width — with a 280-px opaque HUD wall on each side; the HUD is ~42 % of the frame and opaque, against ~18 % and transparent in `octopath-4`. Follow the reference: **drop the enemy panel column entirely**, put the enemy's name, HP and statuses on a small plate above the *targeted* enemy (the ribbon already carries turn order), and let the stage run x 24–1160. **CONTRACT** — the enemy-panels row and the stage's `x 312–968 (the panels are the bounds)`.
2. **Spread the actors.** `game/screens/layout.ts` — hero feet (408,380)/(464,448)/(520,516), `DIAG_DX 56 / DIAG_DY 68`. With the stage widened, push the party to ~x 700–1000 and the enemies to ~x 150–520 to match the reference's spread, and raise `DIAG_DX` to ~90 so the diagonal has depth instead of stacking. **CONTRACT**.
3. **Enemy scale.** Enemies read at 8–9 % of frame height against the heroes' 15 %; `octopath-4`'s lizardmen match the party. Bring a standard enemy to 11–14 % — authored larger in `game/art/parts.ts` (a non-integer stage scale would break the 2-px cell).
4. **Delete the two full-width rules.** `game/screens/battle.ts` — the log strip's top edge at y 556 and the skill band's top edge at y 600 each cut the frame across all 1280 px, and with the panel columns they turn one picture into a 3 × 3 grid of boxes. Let the log be shadowed text over a short local gradient with no rule and no full-width plate; let the skill row float with no top edge.
5. **Populate the centre at two enemies.** `battle.ts` enemy placement — with only two enemies the right half is mostly floor; fan two enemies wider rather than stacking them at the far end of the diagonal, and let the mid-ground mass carry x 560–760.

**Sprites (to the sprite loop):**
- Value inversion in-scene: Crypt Warden and Dust Wraith are the two lightest objects in `play-battle-3.png` and Ash Hound is a pale blob — drop the crypt enemies' hide and garment midtones 15–20 L\* so the party stays the lightest plane and the enemies sit *into* the ground.
- Ash Hound has no contact shadow at all and the Hollow King's is invisible (`play-battle-boss.png`) — every actor needs the ellipse the heroes already get.
- Costume: beside `octopath-1-job-lineup.jpg` the six heroes are tunic / robe / plate / hood in six hues, with silhouette variety coming from props; give at least two of them a garment with its own outline — a coat with tails, a hat brim, a half-cape.

### Full-frame verdict, round 2: ONE MORE ROUND — sprites 8 · scene 7 · UI 6 · VFX 7 · composition 5

Nine of round 1's largest asks landed and the frame is a different game: the twelve saturated bars are off the ground, the enemy panel wall is gone, the stage runs x 24–1160 (**88.8 % of the width**, from 51 %), the HUD is down to ~24 % of the frame and transparent against `octopath-4`'s ~22 %, `floorGrid()` is retired in all six biomes, every horizon is stepped and occluded, every biome carries an opposing accent, the vignette is elliptical, both foot clusters get a pool, the actor plane is lit multiplicatively so sprites keep their authored values in-scene (heroes now hold **10.9 / 19.7 / 24.6 % below L 35** where round 7 measured 0 %), enemies grew from 8–9 % to **10–14 %** of frame height against the party's ~14 %, pictograms replaced every letter-in-a-square, the log is shadowed text with no rule, pause and inspect suppress the HUD beneath them, and the wear card carries the candidate's own sprite. What still separates it from the references is one measurement and one habit. The measurement: **85.8 % of the battle frame is below L 35 and 1.1 % is above L 75** (`octopath-3`: 52.4 % / 6.8 %), the near ground sits at **p50 L 7.3** — darker than the mid ground at 18.8, so the bottom quarter is dead — and the floor's whole value range from p10 to p90 is **4.4 L** where `octopath-4`'s near ground spans **30.8**; even `octopath-2`'s night forest holds its ground at p50 45.8 with satMean 72. The scatter is there, the *values* are not, so the floor still reads as an airbrush and the razor-sharp actors read as pasted onto it. The habit: the two act-1 trash enemies are still the palest and most saturated objects in the picture — ASH_HOUND **p50 67.6 with 1.6 % below L 35**, CINDER_IMP p50 63.5 at satMean 71.7, standing on a floor at p50 15.1, where `octopath-4`'s lizardmen read p50 37.2 on a ground of p50 80.4 — a complete inversion of the reference's value law, and with the crypt's brazier as the largest bright mass at the far left, the eye still lands left-first on something the player does not control. And the hit frame is now the worst frame in the game: the target goes from p50 60.5 / 9.4 % above L 75 to **p50 79.3 with 61 % of its pixels above L 75 and 1.2 % below**, still 56 % white 360 ms later, so the recoil the sprite loop spent four rounds authoring is invisible in the one animation it exists for — while the whole frame's bright share on a hit is 1.1 % against `octopath-2`'s 6.9 %.

**Sprites, round 3:**
1. `game/art/actors.ts` + `game/art/parts.ts` — ASH_HOUND and CINDER_IMP, in the scene and not on the sheet. Measured on their own pixels at their battle seats: hound p50 67.6 / 1.6 % below L 35, imp p50 63.5 / 8 %, both above the floor's p50 15.1 by 45–52 L. Drop the hide and wing midtones until each holds ≥ 15 % below L 35 in `battle-0.png`; CRYPT_WARDEN (p50 44.9, 28.9 %) is the model that already works.
2. `game/art/late.ts` (the acts 3–6 module) — the 24 new enemies are a visible tier below the mainline cast on `ff-late.png`. DROWNED_CLOUD is a cloud with six straight rain bars, VAULT_JELLY a dome with six straight tendrils, WIND_SPRITE a flat spiral, LIGHTNING_HAWK a two-tone chevron, EMBER_ELEMENTAL a black disc in a flame ring — 2–3 shades, no plane, no rim, no cast. Give each the four-step ramp with the hue shift, a lit top and one authored dark plane, and re-silhouette the four (SMITH_PRIEST, DROWNED_SENTINEL, GALE_MONK, SPIRE_WARDEN) that are hero bodies in another palette. Two thirds of the run is fought against this sheet.
3. `game/art/actors.ts` — BASALT at ×3 (`crop-party.png`): the tabard is a 3×2 grid of salmon rectangles that reads as a keypad, and the helm is a grey slab with a white line and no face at all. Break the grid with a diagonal or a belt, and put two eye clusters behind the visor slot the way CRYPT_WARDEN has.
4. `game/art/actors.ts` — HOLLOW_KING's eyes are `#c641bc` (H 305, S 67 %), the only magenta in the game and unattached to any palette. Pull them onto the crypt's violet fill.

**Scene, round 3:**
1. `game/art/backdrops.ts` FLOOR painters — the biggest remaining gap on this axis. The scatter landed but carries no value: p10→p90 is 4.4 L near and 11.8 L mid, against the reference's 30.8 / 51.9. Author the ground's texture in the actor's own 2-px cell and at 25–40 % local contrast — stones with a lit top and a dark bed, ruts, tufts, a scuffed lane along the diagonal — not 6–10 % smoke-grade flecks.
2. `game/art/backdrops.ts` — lift the floor. The battle frame is p50 L 15.8 with 1.1 % above L 75; `octopath-3` is 33.3 / 6.8 and even the night forest's ground is p50 45.8. Raise the crypt floor toward p50 30–40 inside the pools and give the near plane MORE light than the mid, not less (`floorLip` at 112 px currently makes the bottom quarter p50 7.3, darker than everything behind it).
3. `game/art/backdrops.ts` MID painters — every mid-ground prop in all six sheets is an untextured silhouette. Give each mass a lit top plane on the key side, one interior value break and a cast shadow on the ground; `ff-bd-SKY_RUINS.png`'s floating islands and `ff-bd-STORM_SPIRE.png`'s monoliths are flat trapezoids at one value.
4. `game/art/backdrops.ts` + DESIGN.md's "exactly one plane is pixelated" — that rule is what makes `cmp-eq-stage.png` read as sprites on a painting beside `cmp-oct-stage.png`, where the sand, tufts and stones are pixel art at the lizardmen's own cell size. Let the FLOOR and NEAR painters draw hard-pixel texture at `ACTOR_SCALE`; keep the blur for FAR and MID only.
5. `game/art/backdrops.ts` — ground colour. The crypt floor is satMean 13.4 where all three references' grounds run 25–78. Put a second hue into the floor (a cool stone against the amber pool) rather than tinting the whole plane one temperature.

**UI, round 3:**
1. `game/screens/cards.ts:184` — sibling cards each centre themselves in `CARD_H`, so a row's tops and bottoms disagree by up to 43 px (`playfull-cards-BOSS.png`: tops ≈ 122 / 132 / 165, bottoms ≈ 492 / 484 / 455). Align the row's tops, or size every card in a row to the tallest.
2. `game/screens/battle.ts` (`INSPECT` block) — the inspect panel is 1232 × 648 with six rows in the top-left corner and ~90 % void (`battle-inspect.png`). Size it to its rows the way the cards now are, or fill it with the character at `ACTOR_SCALE` and the substat detail.
3. `game/screens/hud.ts` + `title.ts` / `cards.ts` / `node.ts` / `end.ts` — one accent system. Screen titles are amber (draft, leader, treasure, act), cyan (SUMMON), green (REST) and violet (SHRINE); primary buttons are gold, green (FULL HEAL) and red (RETRY). Pick amber for the title role and let the verdict colour live only in the word, not the plate.
4. `game/screens/hud.ts` `drawHpRule` + `game/screens/map.ts` — HP is `#7fd45c` in battle and `#ffd75e` on the map. One colour, and let it shift toward the danger hue below ~35 % (Ember at 30 % still draws full green on `playfull-act1-cleared.png`).
5. `game/screens/hud.ts` `drawPrimaryButton` / the focus treatment — the 1-px bordered rounded rect round 2 retired is still the focused state on the command list, the leader rows, the draft cards, the map nodes and the bank rows, and `drawPrimaryButton` renders a gold slab with a hard bright top rule that reads as a web CTA. Make focus a key-coloured glow plus a value lift, not a keyline.
6. `game/screens/node.ts` / `cards.ts` — give every control an affordance, not only the focused one. SUMMON's primary reads as dim grey "pick one" with no plate (`playfull-summon.png`); the wear row's second candidate and DECLINE / SKIP / WALK PAST / KEEP EMBER are bare text on the floor; on a phone two of three command rows have no plate at all (`phone-battle-1.png`).
7. `game/screens/node.ts` — the SUMMON detail band is a 1180 × 380 plate with content in its top third only, ~78 % empty; the largest empty area left in the game after the inspect panel.
8. `game/screens/hud.ts` `gradientPlate` — the run screens' plates are transparent enough that the marsh's dead trees and the crypt's brazier run through the relic rows and the "cd 5 . all enemies" line (`playfull-bank.png`, `playfull-rest.png`, `playfull-draft.png`). Raise the plate's top alpha or put a short local wash behind the text block.
9. `game/screens/cards.ts:85` — card titles are the retro bitmap font at scale 3 next to vector body copy in the same box (`crop-card.png`); the two type voices are 2 cm apart. Either give the bitmap face a role that is only ever alone (the logo, the pops) or move card titles to `FONT_HD`.

**VFX, round 3:**
1. `game/art/vfx.ts` — scale in the frame. `HURT_MIX` came down to 0.45 as asked, but the impact still occupies ~1.6 % of the frame and puts 1.1 % of the frame above L 75 against `octopath-2`'s 6.9 %. Multiply the impact half's emitter radius and count by ~2 again at battle scale; the sheet's internal quality is already right (the effect box measures 17.9 % above L 75 against the reference's 18.8 %) — it is only ever too small.
2. `game/art/actors.ts` `HURT_FLASH` / `game/screens/battle.ts` draw order — the white-out is compounding: tint 0.45, plus the family's white core over the same pixels, plus the light gain, plus the bloom, gives 61 % of the target above L 75 and 1.2 % below L 35 at the hit and 56 % still at +360 ms (`b20/battle-hit-0b.png`, `b16/battle-hit-1c.png`). Cap it on the result, not the input: flash the silhouette's EDGE and keep the interior's authored values, and gate the bloom on flashed actor cells.
3. `game/screens/battle.ts` `spawnPop` — pops do not de-collide. `battle-0.png` stacks "214", "267" and "BRAND" inside 60 px over one hero, three strings overlapping. Offset a new pop by a row when an existing pop is within its box.
4. `game/art/vfx.ts` — the ground-contact pool is invisible in the crypt because it is amber inside an amber key pool. Bias the pool 20–30° off the biome's own key hue, or give it a dark contact rim.

**Composition, round 3:**
1. `game/art/backdrops.ts` — the crypt's brazier is the largest, brightest, most saturated mass in every crypt frame (x 60–260, `#d06833` at S 75 %), at the far left, opposite the party. It is why the eye still goes left first. Move it up-stage behind the enemy rank, cut its bloom radius, or trade it for the cool window as the crypt's loudest source. Biggest single point on this axis.
2. `game/screens/layout.ts` — the centre band x 470–670 is empty floor in every battle frame; the mid-ground mass round 2 asked for sits in the blurred FAR plane. Put a real mid-plane mass at x 520–720 in front of the horizon and behind the actors.
3. `game/screens/layout.ts` enemy placement — three enemies are three evenly spaced singles with ~100 px of floor between them; `octopath-4`'s six lizardmen OVERLAP into one mass with depth. Tighten the pack pitch and let neighbours overlap by 15–25 % so the far end of the diagonal reads as one shape.
4. `game/art/actors.ts` (with sprites item 1) — until the enemies sit *into* the ground, no placement change will move the eye: 45–52 L above the floor they stand on, they will out-shout the party wherever they are put.

**First-ten-minutes defects:**
1. Three damage pops overlap into an unreadable pile over the hero being hit — "214" / "267" / "BRAND" inside 60 px. `b16/battle-0.png`, `act1/playfull-battle-FIGHT.png`.
2. Every hit turns its target into a featureless white shape (61 % of its pixels above L 75, still 56 % after 360 ms) — no face, no recoil, no silhouette. `b20/battle-hit-0b.png`, `b16/battle-hit-1c.png`.
3. Cards in a row are ragged: three boss-reward cards with tops at y ≈ 122 / 132 / 165 and bottoms at ≈ 492 / 484 / 455. Reads as a layout bug. `act1/playfull-cards-BOSS.png`.
4. The SHRINE prints "6 pacts left in **the crypt**" while the player is standing in the Frost Marsh — hardcoded at `game/screens/node.ts:452`. `playfull-shrine.png`.
5. The marsh moon becomes a flat mid-grey disc with a cyan ring under `dimScene` — the brightest object in the biome reads as a hole punched in the sky. `playfull-end-GAME_OVER.png` against `playfull-room-SHRINE.png`.
6. Inspect opens a 1232 × 648 near-black panel with six "empty" rows in one corner; reads as an unfinished screen. `b16/battle-inspect.png`.
7. Controls with no affordance: SUMMON's primary is dim grey text "pick one"; the wear row's second candidate and DECLINE are bare text; on a phone two of three command rows have no plate. `act1/playfull-summon.png`, `act1/playfull-wear-LOOT.png`, `phone-battle-1.png`.
8. HP is green in battle (`#7fd45c`) and gold on the map (`#ffd75e`), and a hero at 30 % HP still draws a full-saturation green bar. `act1/playfull-battle-boss.png` against `act1/playfull-map-act1.png` and `playfull-act1-cleared.png`.

Frames viewed: `octopath-1-job-lineup.jpg`, `octopath-2-water-skill-bloom.jpg`, `octopath-3-village-shafts.jpg`, `octopath-4-desert-battle.jpg`; `ff-bd-EMBER_CRYPT.png`, `ff-bd-FROST_MARSH.png`, `ff-bd-SKY_RUINS.png`, `ff-bd-SUNKEN_VAULT.png`, `ff-bd-ASHEN_FORGE.png`, `ff-bd-STORM_SPIRE.png`, `ff-vfx.png`, `ff-late.png`; `playfull-title.png`, `-draft.png`, `-summon.png`, `-leader.png`, `-map-act1.png`, `-room-FIGHT.png`, `-battle-FIGHT.png`, `-battle-boss.png`, `-cards-FIGHT.png`, `-cards-LOOT.png`, `-cards-BOSS.png`, `-wear-LOOT.png`, `-rest.png`, `-act1-cleared.png` (seed 7, act 1); `playfull-room-SHRINE.png`, `-shrine.png`, `-bank.png`, `-end-GAME_OVER.png` (seed 7, acts=2 — the run died in act 2, so no doors and no VICTORY end screen was reachable); `battle-0.png`, `battle-hit-1b.png`, `battle-hit-1c.png`, `battle-paused.png`, `battle-inspect.png` (seed 16); `battle-hit-0b.png`, `battle-hit-2b.png` (seed 20); `phone-viewport.png`, `phone-battle-1.png`, `phone-playfull-draft.png`, `phone-playfull-cards-LOOT.png`; crops at ×2–×3: the party and the boss from `playfull-battle-boss.png`, a relic card, the act-1 map, and the EQ stage strip beside `octopath-4`'s at matched actor scale.

### Full-frame verdict, round 3: ONE MORE ROUND — sprites 8 · scene 7 · UI 7 · VFX 7 · composition 6

Round 3 closed the two frames that were actively broken and did not move the one measurement the whole picture rests on. The hit is fixed: a target that went to 61 % of its own pixels above L 75 and was still 56 % white 360 ms later now reads 3.7 % and 4.3 %, the recoil pose survives, the bystanders stay within +4.9 points, and the death flash is a radial burst with the shaken tableau and the fallen body legible through it — `ko/playfull-ko-death-wipe.png` is the best single frame the game has made. The ground is fixed: the crypt floor went from a 4.4 L airbrush to hard pixel-art stones, ruts and a scuffed lane at the actors' own 2-px cell, near p50 6.5 → 34.4–37.0 with a p10→p90 of 36–40 L against `octopath-4`'s near ground at 38.5, and the straight-edge bar fell from a 1280-px offscreen seam to **40–88 px in all six biomes**. The interface stopped being a prototype: cards in a row share a height (tops 122, bottoms 493), every control has a resting plate, one amber accent with the family colour in a single word, one HP colour that walks amber at 30 %, pictograms everywhere, pops 28–35 px apart, and the shrine knows it is standing in the frost marsh. Five of the eight first-ten-minutes defects are closed outright. What is left is one number and one absence. The number: **73.0 % of the battle frame is below L 35 and 0.7 % is above L 75** — against `octopath-3`'s 52.4 / 6.8 and `octopath-4`'s 19.9 / 31.8 — and that 0.7 % is **identical on a resting frame and on a frame with an impact in it**, so a hit adds no light to the picture at all where `octopath-2`'s adds 6.9 %. The absence: nothing above the horizon. Every EQ frame's top band sits at p50 14.6–22.2 with 76–97 % below L 35, where both references put their brightest object in the sky (75.2 and 58.7), and `r3-bd-CRYPT-ARCADE.png` proves the crypt's arches, chains, hanging lamps and lit doorway are all drawn and that the HIGH tier's 6-px and 2.6-px blur erases every one of them. The consequence is compositional: the largest bright mass in every battle frame is a patch of empty foreground floor at y 610–697, the centre third is brighter than the left by 1.7–3.5 L where the references manage +26 and +27, and the largest saturated mass is still the crypt's brazier at (164, 271) in the left third. And the sprites are not the problem any more — measured at their seats they sit at p50 33.6–42.1 against `octopath-4`'s front figure at 36.4. They are at the reference's value. It is the ground under them (21.7–34.1 where the reference is 49.6–76.2) and the air behind them that are 25–45 L short.

**Sprites — 8.** All 43 pass the sheet criteria (`sheets/metrics.md`, 43 PASS / 0 FAIL), and at ×3 in `c-hit-b16.png` and ×4 on `lineup-crypt-x4.png` every face, weapon and plane reads. The sprite-vs-ground law measured against `octopath-4`'s front figure (p50 **36.4**, 48.5 % below L 35, on ground p50 **49.6–76.2** — the sprite sits **13–40 L below** its ground): ours read HOLLOW_KING 42.1 on ground 25.3 (**+16.8**), EMBER 35.4 on 21.7 (**+13.7**), LUMEN 39.6 on 34.1 (**+5.5**), ASH_HOUND 38.4 on 27.6 (**+10.8**) — still the inverted order coordinator decision 5 recorded, but the inversion is now entirely on the ground's side of the equation, not the sprites'. Round-2 items: **(1) ASH_HOUND / CINDER_IMP — CLOSED**, hound p50 67.6 → **38.4** with 1.6 % → **38.7 %** below L 35 at its battle seat, imp 63.5 → **43.3** with 8 % → **37.3 %**, both past the ≥ 15 % bar. **(2) the late module — PARTLY**: LIGHTNING_HAWK, STORM_DRAKE and CINDER_WOLF are genuinely re-drawn (a hook-billed bird, a winged drake with body planes, a lean ribbed canid), but on `c-late.png` DROWNED_CLOUD is still a cloud over six straight vertical rain bars, VAULT_JELLY still a dome over tendrils, EMBER_ELEMENTAL still a dark disc inside a flame ring, and the four hero-body humanoids got interiors but not silhouettes (SMITH_PRIEST↔MARSH_HAG 73.9 % IoU, DROWNED_SENTINEL↔CRYPT_WARDEN 76.3 %). **(3) BASALT — CLOSED**: a diagonal baldric across the tabard and two orange eye slits in the visor. **(4) HOLLOW_KING's eyes — CLOSED**: `#c641bc` H 305 → `#8e40b5` **H 280** S 65 %. The round-1 carry-overs: contact shadows now on every actor including ASH_HOUND and the boss — closed; costume outline variety — partly (TIDE's robe, BASALT's shield, LUMEN's halo, CINDER_IMP's horns break their outlines; EMBER, GALE, SABLE, CRYPT_WARDEN and DUST_WRAITH do not). The new evidence is `c-sil-main.png`: nine pairs sit at 70–80 % nearest-silhouette IoU and four actors are 91–100 % mirror-symmetric (BOG_TOAD 100, CINDER_IMP 97.1, DUST_WRAITH 94.3, SILT_CRAB 91.6). Beside `octopath-1`, where a top hat, a brim, a flared coat and a half-cape do all the identifying work, ours are still a round hair-mass over a torso over two legs, and three heroes hold the same arm out to the same side.

Give four of them headwear or a garment that leaves the body outline — a brim, a horned helm, a coat with tails, a half-cape — in `game/art/parts.ts` and the recipes in `game/art/actors.ts`.
2. Kill the mirror symmetry on the four that are 91–100 %. BOG_TOAD 100, CINDER_IMP 97.1, DUST_WRAITH 94.3, SILT_CRAB 91.6; `octopath-1`'s figures are asymmetric at every pose. **Scene — 7.** The floor is the round's biggest win and the air is its biggest hole. Round-2 items: **(1) floor texture at the actor's own cell — CLOSED**, near-band p10→p90 4.4 L → **27.3–36.1 L** across all six biomes at satMean 31.1–40.9, and at ×3 in `c-b16-stage.png` the stones, gold chips, ruts and the scuffed lane are hard-pixel art on the actors' 2-px cell, which is exactly `octopath-4`'s method. **(2) lift the floor, near ahead of mid — CLOSED**, near p50 6.5 → **34.4–37.0** and near leads mid by 4.6–6.6 L, no dead bottom quarter. **(3) mid masses lit — PARTLY**: the internal ranges are there (crypt masses 16.5–28.5 L) but **every** crypt mid mass sits at p50 **14.4–27.1**, where `octopath-3` carries a dark house at 34.4 *and* lit ones at 74.7–78.7 — a 45 L spread between neighbouring masses against our 13. Nothing in our mid plane catches light. **(4) hard pixels on FLOOR and NEAR — CLOSED for FLOOR**, NEAR still at `BLUR_NEAR = 8`. **(5) a second hue in the ground — PARTLY**, crypt floor satMean 13.4 → 33.7–36.9, against the references' grounds at **86.6–107.8** (oct-4) and 36.9 (oct-3). Round 1's horizon item is closed hard: the straight-edge bar reads **40 / 68 / 88 / 54 / 80 / 44 px** for crypt / marsh / ruins / vault / forge / spire at HIGH and 198 px at LOW and ARCADE, all under the 300 bar. What did not move: the frame is 73.0 % below L 35 (was 85.8) and **0.7 % above L 75** (was 1.1 — worse), against 52.4 / 6.8; every diorama's top band is p50 **14.6–22.2** with 76.5–97.2 % below L 35 against skies at 58.7 and 75.2; the **actor band is the flattest band in every biome** (p10→p90 **17.5–25.5 L**) and the darkest floor in the frame (ground at the seats **21.7–34.1**, against the empty near ground at 34.4) — the forward-brightening ramp puts the light where nobody stands, and the front hero seat's actor-to-ground contrast is **1.23:1**, under the coordinator's 1.5:1 ruler. ASHEN_FORGE is the outlier at **89.3 %** below L 35 with a mid range of 17.5. FROST_MARSH is the one diorama that reads at the reference's level — a hot moon, a cold sky, black tree silhouettes at three depths, a sunken boat, a warm lantern opposing the moon — and its numbers say so (44.2 % below L 35, better than `octopath-3`). Tier coherence is off: the same crypt reads p50 24.7 at HIGH with mean rgb (80, 58, 60), 21.8 at LOW, and **32.4 at ARCADE with rgb (91, 75, 90)** — 7.7 L brighter and hue-flipped from warm to neutral-lavender by the CRT lift.

**UI — 7.** Eight of round 2's nine items landed at least partly and the run no longer reads as a web page standing in front of a diorama — it reads as a game whose panels are slightly too see-through. **(1) card row heights — CLOSED**, `act2/playfull-cards-BOSS.png` tops all at y 122 and bottoms at 493 where the critic measured 122/132/165 and 492/484/455. **(2) INSPECT — PARTLY**: it a fraction of its area and whose 18 "—" dashes are what a new player actually sees. **(3) one accent — CLOSED**: every title head is the amber with the family in one word (SUMMON cyan, SHRINE violet, HEAL green, GAME OVER rose). **(4) one HP colour — CLOSED**: green in battle and on the map, and Ember at 30 % draws amber on `phone-playfull-act1-cleared.png`. **(5) retire the keyline — PARTLY**: `drawPrimaryButton` genuinely lost its hard bright top rule (a 1-px lip at a third alpha now, `game/screens/hud.ts:866`) and focus is light-plus-lift on the cards, wear buttons and command rows — but focus on the **map nodes, the draft grid and the room card is still a 1-px accent keyline** (`act2/playfull-map-act1.png`, `act1/playfull-room-FIGHT.png`), and at ×3 in `c-primary.png` a 384×56 rounded rect with a vertical gradient, an outer glow and a centred sans-serif word is still the anatomy of a web button on an HD-2D diorama. **(6) affordance — CLOSED**: SUMMON's primary is a dimmed slab rather than grey text, DECLINE / SKIP / WALK PAST all carry plates, and all three phone command rows have plates. **(7) the SUMMON detail band — PARTLY**: 1184×380 → 1184×168, but its three columns stop at x 1045 and its lower third is empty. **(8) plates that hold text — PARTLY, and this is the worst frame left**: the card plates hold (interior p10→p90 **12.2–13.4 L** at satMean 8–9) and the draft strip holds (10.9 L / 15.8), but the **party-column faces do not** — on `act2/playfull-bank.png` the column interiors carry **21.0–23.5 L at satMean 30–33** and the marsh's dead trees, the sunken boat, the lantern and the reeds read straight through all three columns and through "0 / 1 chosen . the Vault holds 0 of 12"; `act2/playfull-rest.png` does the same with the crypt's pillars and the fallen beam. **(9) two type voices — PARTLY**: the bitmap title sits on its own band now, but "ENDURE +2" in the 3×5 face is still 40 px above "EPIC" in the vector face inside the same card. New: the rest-state rarity keylines (cyan on RARE, violet on EPIC) are the most saturated edges left on any screen; the disabled map node labels are unreadable over the lit ground; and every run screen is top-heavy — on `cards-BOSS` the drawn UI stops at y 493 and resumes at 572, leaving 174 px (24 % of the height) of bare floor.

**VFX — 7.** The axis fixed the worst thing in the game and did not fix the thing it was told was its biggest item. **(2) the white-out — CLOSED, decisively**: the target's own pixels above L 75 went **61 % → 3.7 %** at the hit and **56 % → 4.3 %** at +360 ms (b16 seed 16, `battle-hit-1b/c`; b20 reads 3.6 % and 3.0 %), and at ×3 in `c-hit-b16.png` CINDER_IMP keeps its pink body, its visor band and its wings all the way through the impact. The sampled peak, one frame earlier at +40 ms, is **14.4 %** above L 75 against `octopath-2`'s target at **20.9 %** — the right shape. Bystanders move +4.9 (CRYPT_WARDEN) and +0.6 (ASH_HOUND) points, inside the claim. **(3) pops de-collide — CLOSED**, 28–35 px apart in `b16/battle-0.png` where three strings used to overlap inside 60 px. **(4) tails — CLOSED**, every row on `r3-vfx-all.png` is still alive at age 6/8. **(1) scale in the frame — OPEN, and it is the axis**: the whole frame's share above L 75 is **0.7 % on b16, 1.0 % on b20 and 0.7 % on the KO frame — the same 0.7–1.0 % as a resting frame with no effect on it at all** — where `octopath-2`'s hit frame is **6.9 %**; the 320×220 zone around the target reads **1.7–3.7 %** above L 75 against the reference effect box's **18.7 %**. **(4, the pool) — PARTLY**: the writer's ≥ 24° hue offset is real, but in the crypt the pool's feathered dark apron dominates its own light and at ×3 it reads as a **dark stain** on a lit floor, the opposite of `octopath-2`, where the water pool lights the grass around it. The rest of the gap is legible beside the reference crop: `octopath-2` throws 40-odd shards 6–20 px wide with hot white cores across 45 % of the frame width and 2.5× the target's height; ours is six 1-px hairlines, ten dots and one ellipse at about 1.2× the target's height. `healShimmer` and `shieldDome` are near-invisible on their own sheet rows.

**VFX, round 4:**

Target ≥ **4 %** at the peak frame with the target itself staying under 25 %. `game/art/vfx.ts` — the impact half's emitter radius and count again, and hold the peak past a single 40 ms frame.
2. Widen the strokes. The streaks are 1-px hairlines at battle scale; `octopath-2`'s shards are 6–20 px with white cores. Target: the primary stroke family at ≥ **5 px** with a hot core, and the effect's vertical extent ≥ **2× the target's height** (it is ~1.2× now). `game/art/vfx.ts`.
3. Turn the ground pool back into light. Measured at ×3 the pool's interior sits below the crypt floor around it — the dark apron is carrying the read. Target: pool interior ≥ **+8 L** over the floor 40 px outside it, with the apron only at the outer edge. `game/art/vfx.ts` `renderVfxUnder`.

**Composition — 6.** The eye no longer goes hard left, and there is now something to look at in the lower centre — but there is still nothing to look at where the reference puts everything. The p95-luminance centroid moved from round 2's (248, 271) to **(662–691, 456–492)** across every battle frame — **52–54 % across**, on `octopath-4`'s 45 %, so **round-3 item 1 is half closed**: the brazier moved up-stage and dropped from S 75 % to **S 55 %** and is no longer the largest bright mass. It is, however, **still the largest saturated mass in every crypt frame** — 2.39–3.07 % of pixels centred at (162–168, 265–274), box x 87–240, in the left third, opposite the party. Vertically the centroid sits at **63–68 % down** against the reference's 50 %, because the **largest bright mass in every EQ battle frame is a patch of empty foreground floor** (1.39–2.11 % of pixels, boxes at y 603–697) where `octopath-4`'s is the lit gap between the ruins at (513, 230) and `octopath-3`'s is the shafted sky at (613, 255) — both *behind* the actors, which is what makes those figures read. Thirds: ours run **27.7–28.6 / 29.4–32.1 / 23.3–23.8** left/centre/right, a centre lead of **1.7–3.5 L**; `octopath-4` runs 48.8 / **76.2** / 49.5 (+27) and `octopath-3` 29.2 / **55.6** / 25.3 (+26). The frame has no lit centre. **Item 2 (a mass at x 520–720) — PARTLY**: a near-plane fallen beam now sits at x 475–690 / y 490–530 and a blurred mid block at x 480–535, but x 540–660 above y 480 is still empty air. **Item 3 (tighten the pack, 15–25 % overlap) — OPEN**: the three crypt enemies stand at 22–30 px gaps and never touch, three evenly spaced singles where `octopath-4`'s six lizardmen overlap into one mass with depth; and the two ranks are 270 px apart (21 % of the width) against the reference's 8 %. **Item 4 (enemies into the ground) — OPEN as a plane law**: the enemies' own values fell 20–24 L, but the enemy rank still averages **p50 41.0 against the party's 33.0 — the enemy plane is 8.0 L lighter than the plane the player controls**, and in the boss frame the boss is the brightest seat (+4.6 above the median). Seat uniformity itself holds: max **+4.9 L** above the median seat in b16 and +4.6 in the boss frame, inside the 5 L bar. The two tableaux: the death moment is strong in motion — `ko/playfull-ko-death-wipe.png`'s radial red flash keeps the HUD's own colours, the far side of the frame and the fallen body all legible — and empty at rest, because `act2/playfull-end-GAME_OVER.png` has **no party in it at all** (the contract draws survivors, and a GAME OVER has none), so it is four centred lines and a button on bare marsh, which is round 1's complaint verbatim. There is no victory tableau at all in the first ten minutes: clearing act 1 cuts straight to the act-2 map, and the doors row only exists after a lap or at act 6.

Target ≥ **+15 L**, achieved with scene items 1–3 rather than by moving actors — a bright sky or a lit mid mass behind the stage, so the largest bright mass moves from y 610–697 (foreground floor) to behind the actor plane. `game/art/backdrops.ts` FAR / MID painters.
