### Full-frame verdict, round 4: ONE MORE ROUND — sprites 8 · scene 8 · UI 8 · VFX 8 · composition 7

Round 4 fixed the number the whole picture rested on. The battle frame was 73.0 % below L 35 and 0.7 % above L 75; it is now **54.0–57.6 % and 7.6–8.1 %**, which is past `octopath-3`'s 52.4 / 6.8 on the same detector and on the same side of it. There is a lit thing above the horizon in every biome, the centre third leads the left by **+22.2 to +22.5 L** where round 3 managed +1.7 to +3.5 and the references manage +26 to +27, and the largest bright mass in every battle frame has moved from a patch of empty foreground floor at y 610–697 to a **4.5 % mass at (634–644, 94–100) behind the actor plane** — the reference's own arrangement. The pack is one mass, the ranks are 150 px apart (11.7 %), the party plane now sits above the enemy plane (party torso mean 44.0–47.2 against enemies at 34.4–37.7), the plates hold (bank/rest/party column interiors 4.1–6.0 L at satMean 4.8–10.2, from 21.0–23.5 at 30–33), a hit throws a real fan of white-cored spindles 2.6× the target's height and lights the ground under it by **+23.6 L**, a heal and a ward can be seen (12.9 % and 11.8 % above L 75 in their own bounds), and `playfull-act-clear.png` is a tableau the game did not have. That is five axes moving at once and none of it is cosmetic. What stops it shipping is that the light it added is the wrong SHAPE. Beside `octopath-3`, where the shaft is cut by a roofline and passes trees you can count, our light wells are value plateaus — the crypt's reads **p50 81.7 over a p10→p90 range of 8.1 L**, the marsh's **3.5 L**, against `octopath-3`'s shaft zone at 30.1 L: a wash behind fog, not an opening. Because that wash is at the top of the frame and 4.5 % of it, the p95-luminance centroid overshot from round 3's 63–68 % down to **16–18 % down**, where both references sit at 50 %; our top third now leads the frame by 12–14 L where `octopath-3`'s middle leads and `octopath-4`'s bottom leads. And the reference's governing law is still inverted: `octopath-4`'s front figure reads p50 **35.8 on a ground of 53.5** — 17.7 L *below* what it stands on — while our five crypt seats read 32.6 / 36.3 / 34.3 / 37.4 / 56.9 on grounds of 27.7 / 36.5 / 17.4 / 26.9 / 40.4, an average of **+9.7 L above**, a 27 L disagreement that coordinator decision 5 recorded and this tree has not moved. Two other things a viewer sees, both new: the focused primary's lit patch is cut by a rectangle — a **+31 L step across 4 px running 340 px** on the title screen, the first frame of the game — and the whole picture is one hue, satMean 25.4–28.7 against `octopath-4`'s 57.

**Sprites — 8.** The sheet is spotless: **43 PASS / 0 FAIL** in `sheets/metrics.md`, and at ×2 in `c-b16-stage.png` every face, weapon, helm and coat reads — Ember's torch and hair, Tide's hood and orb shield, the imp's horns, the warden's helm, the hound's muzzle. In scene the sprites are at the reference's value: torso p50 **32.6 (CINDER_IMP) / 36.3 (CRYPT_WARDEN) / 34.3 (ASH_HOUND) / 37.4 (EMBER) / 56.9 (TIDE)** on b16 and 37.7 (HOLLOW_KING) / 37.4 / 51.5 / 43.0 on the boss frame, against `octopath-4`'s front figure at **35.8 with 49.0 % below L 35**; every enemy is inside the ceiling (standard ≤ 50, boss ≤ 55). Round-3 items: **(1) break the head silhouette — OPEN.** Target was no pair above 65 % nearest IoU among the six heroes and six crypt enemies; measured EMBER 72.4 · GALE 68.5 · TIDE 76.7 · BASALT 70.1 · SABLE 75.2 · LUMEN 79.3 · CINDER_IMP 66.6 · ASH_HOUND 57.2 · CRYPT_WARDEN 70.8 · DUST_WRAITH 63.0 · PYRE_KNIGHT 71.0 · HOLLOW_KING 79.4 — **ten of twelve still above 65**, and the artists reverted the garments that measured worse. On `lineup-x2.png` beside `octopath-1`, where a top hat, a brim, a flared coat and a half-cape identify eight jobs at a glance, our six heroes are one column silhouette in six colours, and seven actors hold the same diagonal staff out to the same side. What it costs: in `b16/battle-1.png` you tell Ember from Tide by hue and by what they carry, never by outline — at 15.6 % of frame height that is the read the reference gets for free. **(2) mirror symmetry — CLOSED**: BOG_TOAD 100 → **77.3**, CINDER_IMP 97.1 → **66.6**, DUST_WRAITH 94.3 → **63.0**, SILT_CRAB 91.6 → **66.2**, all under the 80 bar. **(3) the three schematic late enemies — PARTLY**: DROWNED_CLOUD's six straight bars are now broken rain under a modelled cloud and VAULT_JELLY is a belled jelly with canals, but EMBER_ELEMENTAL is still a dark disc inside a flame ring on `lineup-x2.png`. **(4) the four hero-body humanoids — OPEN**: SMITH_PRIEST 73.4 (MARSH_HAG), DROWNED_SENTINEL 73.2 (CRYPT_WARDEN), SPIRE_WARDEN 73.7 (TIDE) all still above the 65 target; only GALE_MONK's nearest is a late actor. The hit flash holds — the target's own pixels peak at **20.6 % above L 75** at +40 ms and fall to 10.7 / 7.6 over the next two frames, and at ×2 in `c-hit-b16.png` the imp keeps its pink body, its horns and its wings throughout. The deaths are real poses: `ko/playfull-ko-death-wipe.png` shows Basalt fallen and legible through a radial red flash that leaves the HUD and the far side of the frame their own colours. ASH_HOUND still reads as a brown-and-white cow.

The number was certified on the wrong artefact. **(5) forge and tier coherence — PARTLY**: ASHEN_FORGE is **64.1 %** below L 35 (target ≤ 65) with a mid range of **29.0 L** (target ≥ 28) — both closed; but the crypt reads p50 32.1 at HIGH and **36.1 at ARCADE, 4.0 L apart** against the ≤ 3 target (R−B 20 → 13, inside the ≤ 8 ask). The light wells are the round's hole: measured at their own boxes the crypt's is **p50 81.7 over an 8.1 L range at satMean 10.4** and the marsh's **81.0 over 3.5 L**, against `octopath-3`'s shaft zone at 30.1 L — a flat bright plateau behind fog, with the shaft's cone and the roofline that would shape it drawn on the FAR plane and blurred away. Only SKY_RUINS' well (30.0 L) has internal structure. The straight-edge bar is clean where the scene owns it: **64 / 58 / 92 / 131 / 55 / 56 px** for crypt / marsh / ruins / vault / forge / spire, well under the references' own 200 (`octopath-2`) and 221 (`octopath-4`) — those are not lines a viewer sees, and the writer's 200–400 px report is measuring UI plates, not gradients (see UI). Colour is the other gap: frame satMean **25.4–28.7** against `octopath-4`'s 57 and its ground's 77.8; every biome is one hue end to end.

What is new, and it is on the first frame of the game: **the focused primary's lit patch is cut by a rectangle.** `drawPrimaryButton` paints its "light from above" as a live radial gradient through `ctx.fillRect(-pw*0.1, -ph*0.3, pw*1.2, ph*1.6)` (`game/screens/hud.ts:977`), and the gradient has not reached zero at that rect's edge, so it leaves a hard horizontal step — **+31 L across 4 px at y ≈ 522, running 340 px** on `playfull-title.png`, +33 L at y ≈ 568 running 312 px on `playfull-end-GAME_OVER.png`, and the same band on `room-FIGHT` and `battle-paused` (`c-go-rect.png`, `c-primary.png`). The un-focused primary is clean, which is why `bank`'s reads as a genuine lit patch of floor. Second: the longest straight edge in the game is now always a UI plate — **1182 px at y 322 on `summon`, 1165 px at y 25 on `map-act1`, 955 px at y 57 on `battle-inspect`, 384–400 px on the cards and the pause buttons, 317 px at y 537 on every battle frame** (the command list's top row) — against `octopath-4`'s own HUD at 221 and `octopath-2`'s at 200. A 1165-px rule across a 1280-px frame is exactly what the contract retired for the battle log and it is alive on three screens. What still reads as a web page: `playfull-map-act1.png` is eleven identical dark rounded squares in a grid under a full-width translucent header — an app launcher standing on a diorama.

**VFX — 8** (from 7). **(1) make a hit change the frame's light — the letter met, the delta small.** The peak frame `b16/battle-hit-1a.png` reads **9.3 % of the frame above L 75 with the target itself at 20.6 %**, against the ≥ 4 % / ≤ 25 % target — but a resting frame with no effect on it reads **7.6–8.1 %**, so the hit adds **+1.7 points frame-wide**; the sky is doing the rest. Restricted to the stage band (x 0–900, y 240–660) the same comparison is **1.0 % → 3.7 %**, a genuine 3.7×, and that is the honest measure of what the player sees. `octopath-2`'s whole frame is 6.9 % above L 75 on a 69.5 %-dark forest, i.e. its effect alone carries what our whole picture carries. **(2) widen the strokes — CLOSED**: at ×2 in `c-hit-b16.png` the fan is ten to fourteen point-first spindles 6–20 px wide with hot white cores, vertical extent ~290 px against a 110-px target, **2.6× the target's height** against the ≥ 2× ask. **(3) the ground pool as light — CLOSED, decisively**: the pool interior at the target's feet reads **48.4 against 24.8 for the floor 40 px outside it, +23.6 L**, where the ask was +8; the dark apron is now only at the rim. **(4) the heal and the ward — CLOSED**: inside their own bounds at their peak, `healShimmer` reads **12.9 %** above L 75 and `shieldDome` **11.8 %**, both past the 8 % bar, and on `r4-vfx-heal.png` the heal is a white-cyan ground pool with a shimmer up the body and the ward a lit dome with a rim. Bystanders: ASH_HOUND moves p50 41.6 → 40.0 and its share above L 75 4.3 → 4.2 — untouched; CRYPT_WARDEN's box goes 7.1 → 25.2 points, but the shards cross it, and at ×2 the warden visibly keeps its dark olive shadow side, which is what the guard promised. **The verifier's residual is confirmed and it is the axis's remaining item.** On `r4-vfx-all.png`, at the peak frame — the one the player looks at — `slash`, `fireBurst`, `windBlade`, `waterWave`, `lightBeam`, `darkPulse`, `stunStar`, `burnFlicker`, `shockwave` and `frostShards` are **the same upward fan of point-first white-cored spindles in ten different hues**; measured, `slash` reads 22.5 % above L 75 in its bounds and `fireBurst` 30.2 % with the same silhouette. They separate only from t ≈ 25 % on, by their trailing particles. Ten of thirteen families are one effect.

**First ten minutes, round 4:** (ranked; each round-3 item's verdict in brackets)
1. **The game's first frame has a rectangle drawn on it.** PRESS TO BEGIN sits on a lit band with a hard top edge — +31 L over 4 px, 340 px long — and the same band is on RETRY, CONTINUE and RESUME. `playfull-title.png`, `c-go-rect.png`. [round-3 #7 — the button itself CLOSED, this is new]
2. **The eye goes to the ceiling.** The brightest thing in every battle frame is a soft white patch at 13 % down; the fight happens in the dark half under it. Centroid 16–18 % down against the references' 50 %. `b16/battle-1.png`. [new]
3. **The light is a wash, not an opening.** The crypt's well has 8.1 L of internal range, the marsh's 3.5; `octopath-3`'s shaft has 30.1 because a roofline cuts it. Nothing shapes our light. `r4-bd-EMBER_CRYPT.png`, `r4-bd-FROST_MARSH.png`. [round-3 #3 — the brown fog is gone; what replaced it is a white fog]
4. **Damage numbers you cannot read.** "267" measures 78.4 on a background of 81.8 — 1.04:1. `b16/battle-0.png`, `c-pops.png`. [new — caused by fixing the sky]
5. **Your party is brighter than the floor it stands on and the enemies are not.** The five crypt seats sit +9.7 L above their ground where `octopath-4`'s front figure sits 17.7 L below its own. `b16/battle-1.png`. [round-3 #4 — the enemy-vs-party half CLOSED (34.4 against 47.2); the ground half OPEN]
6. **GAME OVER is still an empty stage.** The bodies are there and you cannot see them: p50 24.8 with no light on them. `playfull-end-GAME_OVER.png`, `c-go-seats.png`. [round-3 #6 — PARTLY]
7. **Every skill looks like every other skill.** Ten of thirteen effect families are the same white-cored fan in a different hue at the frame that matters. `r4-vfx-all.png`. [new — the verifier's residual, now the axis's item]
8. **Rules cut the frame in half.** 1182 px on SUMMON, 1165 px on the map, 955 px on INSPECT; the references' own HUD edges are 200–221. `playfull-summon.png`, `playfull-map-act1.png`. [round-3 #9 dead band CLOSED; this is what replaced it]
9. **The map is an app grid.** Eleven identical dark rounded squares under a full-width header, on a diorama. `playfull-map-act1.png`. [new]
10. **You cannot tell your heroes apart by shape.** Six column silhouettes at 68.5–79.3 % IoU; seven actors hold the same staff to the same side. `lineup-x2.png`. [round-3 carry-over — OPEN]
11. **Three plates still leak the diorama.** The room card (15.6 L), the draft detail strip (20.1 L), the middle relic card (13.4 L). `playfull-room-FIGHT.png`, `playfull-draft.png`. [round-3 #2 — CLOSED on the party columns, 4.1–6.0 L; PARTLY overall]
12. **A quarter of the stage is empty.** 216 px of bare floor between the front hero and the panel column, with a full party. `playfull-battle-boss.png`. [new]
